# VOLUME 03 OPEN THREADS — as they stand after Chapter 130, Friday 13 March 2015

**Read with the "VOLUME 03, BATCH 0003 — CHAPTERS 121–130" block at the end of `state/continuity.md`, which carries the canon. This file carries only what is still open and what it is forbidden to do.**

## 1. The counter, the shelf, and the drift

- **THE SHELF IS NINE SLIPS IN A DRAWER UNDER THE GROUND-FLOOR COUNTER, ONE HAND, DATED, UNSIGNED, NO REFERENCE, NO BOX, NO NAME ON ANY OF THEM INCLUDING NINA CALDER'S.** Jonas Mercer has seen the edges of them and has not counted, looked, or asked. There is no key on the drawer and no list of what is in it. **There is no form in this borough on which anybody could write that they are not a series, and there is not going to be one, and a box that says *not a series* is a series.**
- **NINE SLIPS AND ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE A DAY ARE TWO DIFFERENT NUMBERS AND MUST NEVER BE ADDED TOGETHER.** A slip is only written when a person stands in front of her and asks what was true on a day, and out of nine people a day, and then eleven, the ones who ask that are three men who have all come more than once and one other person about whom nobody has said a word, and that is the whole of them. **A form that added the two would say *this counter sees eleven people and produces one document*, which is a level of service. There is no level of service in that room.** The nine, exactly, are listed in the continuity block. Eight went into three inside pockets; the ninth went out on a Friday afternoon in the last week of February to somebody who was not in a room with anybody.
- **NINA CALDER HAS NEVER GIVEN EITHER NUMBER TO ANYBODY.** In the one answer she gave a stranger about herself on 6 March 2015 she described the drawer and said nothing about how many were in it and nothing about how many people came through the door. **No character in this batch knows either figure. The count of nine is the count of the account and not the count of a person.**
- **The drift, which is this batch's engine and nobody's instrument:** a state given on a Tuesday can be wrong on the Wednesday; a counter that is required to answer and is not required to make one goes on answering the same state for as long as nobody comes back to argue it; there is no obligation in this city to come back; a person turning up is not an instrument, it is a coincidence, and a coincidence every week is a service. **UNRESOLVED AND NOT GOING TO BE RESOLVED. Do not build an instrument for it in Batch 0004.**
- **The interval: 2 February to 13 February is eleven days, and eleven days has no word, and a series has a word for the gap. Somebody is going to work out what eleven days is.**
- **The throughput: about nine PEOPLE a day on 26 January, about eleven on 6 March, on the back of a hand, in no book. A number of nine in a record is a capacity; a capacity is a promise; the day there is a number on that counter there is a morning somebody can say *we can only do eight today* and be entirely correct. This is a count of people and not a count of documents, it is not the nine in the drawer, and the two may never be added together.**
- **NINA CALDER IS ABOUT TO BE PROVED RIGHT ABOUT THE SECOND HALF OF HER OWN SENTENCE — a register that grows when nobody is hurt is a register somebody can hold over you — and it will not be about the bulb. Jonas Mercer has worked out that he is not going to be in the room and has not told her. UNRESOLVED.**
- **The eleventh thing, added on 6 March 2015 and the only thing anybody has added: THE FLOOR CAN BE ASKED ABOUT ITSELF, AND THE ONLY REASON IT CAN BE ASKED ABOUT ITSELF IS THAT THERE IS A PERSON ON IT AND THE PERSON IS NOT FINDABLE. It is not an item, it is in no minute, and nobody may put it on a wall. The five items of 28 January may not be restated as a list in any form.**

## 2. The two new open objects

- **A CARD IN THE SLOT FOR POST, PUT THERE ON OR ABOUT FRIDAY 20 FEBRUARY 2015.** Four inches by two, folded once, not a form, no crest, no reference, no box, no envelope. About ninety people have seen it. **NOBODY HAS TAKEN IT OUT AND NOBODY HAS PUT IT UP. NINA CALDER HAS NOT READ IT AND IS NOT GOING TO.** The reason, given out loud: a slot that gets emptied is a rule, a rule is a service, and a service is a rota with one name on it, and hers is the name it would have. **WHAT IS ON THE CARD IS NOT KNOWN TO ANYBODY IN THIS ACCOUNT AND MAY NOT BE STATED.**
- **A LETTER IN A TRAY ON THE SECOND FLOOR, PUT THERE ON WEDNESDAY 18 FEBRUARY 2015 AT ABOUT HALF PAST NINE.** Not on borough stock. A real departmental reference in the correct typeface. About four lines long. **It is not a reason column. It is the smallest request anybody in this borough has made since the first of March, and its contents are not stated in this account, are not known to Jonas Mercer, and may not be stated in a room or opened in a document.** Nina Calder has not opened it in front of a second person, has told nobody it is there, has not moved it to the ground-floor tray, and has not answered it in twenty-three days as at Friday 13 March 2015 (nineteen days as at Monday 9 March, and the count is exact). **This is the batch's live hook and Batch 0004 inherits it. It may not be resolved by anybody being helped and nobody may be rescued by it.**

## 3. The three men

- **PETER NAYLOR, 44.** A wall. Signed an acceptance in ninety seconds in August; read two sentences in a corridor in January and able to say no and did not and does not know he was in it. **NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH IS ABLE TO ASK HIM ONE QUESTION ABOUT ANY OF IT, EVER.** He has a fortnight somewhere in this city with a bucket under a tap; one came round at the end of February in four degrees with a frost warning out, and **there is nobody able to ask him whether he has got a coat, and nobody helped him, and that is unresolved and must stay unresolved.**
- **ROYDEN ACHEBE, 49.** A door, which is not a favour. **STILL NOT TOLD, BY ANYBODY, AND JONAS MERCER HAS NOW SAID OUT LOUD IN A CORRIDOR THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO**, with the reason in the pad: a date about a trough costs eleven seconds and a date about himself saying a man's name in a room costs the same, and that is the problem, and a receipt is not an apology. Beatrice Osunde: the expensive half is not being found, it is being asked. **Findability and askability are two different currencies and this borough has been minting the first one very successfully and has not spent one unit of the second. UNRESOLVED.**
- **THE MAN OF FORTY-EIGHT.** Not a wall and not a door. Said a correct thing in a corridor in August, did a weekly verification for about eleven weeks in the autumn, stopped in October. **HAS NOT BEEN ASKED, HAS NOT BEEN RUNG, AND JONAS MERCER HAS NOW REFUSED OUT LOUD IN A CORRIDOR TO GO AND FIND HIM**, because telling him would make him the person who knows, and a person who knows is a person somebody can ask, and the only instrument in this borough that keeps a person from being askable is a person who does not know. **UNRESOLVED, AND THE COMPLIANCE PARAGRAPH IS UNANSWERED AND IS NOT GOING TO BE ANSWERED.**

## 4. The board, the seats, and the ninth

- **SEVEN OF NINE, unchanged for the whole of the batch.** The seventh chair empty since Wednesday 8 October 2014; the eighth since the evening of Wednesday 26 November 2014. **Nobody can fill either and nobody can unfill either and there is no power in this city that could.**
- **THERE IS NO CHAIR OF THAT BODY AND THERE NEVER WAS. A chair is not one of the three parts of its one power — require a party holding continuity to answer, receive the answer, be asked to consider a matter. Any later batch that gives that body a chair is wrong.**
- Four of the nine have said they will stop if it turns into a job. One has given a reason and **asked for it not to be written down, and it is not in the minute, and that was agreed and both of those were correct.** Nobody has stopped.
- **The ninth seat is findable at a shopfront, unpaid, doing it for nothing since 4 July, the only door in a building of thirty-one rented rooms, and nobody has told her and nobody is going to. Do not resolve it. Do not have the board notice it. Do not tell her. Do not have the eight learn what she is.**

## 5. The lowland

- **The lowland is not on the layer, is not in the board's eight districts, cannot be seated, and has no seat. THE REASON IS NOT THAT IT CANNOT PAY. A district that cannot pay has a seat and a person in it and has not thereby lost its vote. The reason is that a node with no party is not a party, and a place is not a person, and the registrar said on 13 January that the reason is correct, has been correct since 1 March, and is not going to be fixed. THAT REASON MAY NOT BE FIXED IN VOLUME 03.**
- Six water points at Tier 1, £11,280 a year, paragraph 6.1 of the minute of 19 May, recorded as received and not as evidence, a winter price that is one boiler each, and nobody has been asked to pay any part of it.
- **ABU BAKAYOKO, 58, CAME BACK TWICE IN THIS BATCH, HE IS RIGHT TO, AND THE BATCH MAY NOT MAKE HIM UN-RIGHT.** What he was given both times was a date. The minute of 15 January is a statement and not a return and does not bind anybody and cannot be produced at a water point, and a minute that says a person asked and names nobody cannot be put beside a bowser in February. **His refusal of the sixth stands, is minuted, and nobody may ask him where it is.**
- **NO SEVENTH SHAPE WAS ADDED TO ANY RECORD IN THIS BATCH. THE SEVENTH IS NOT RESOLVED.** One Lattice notice in Batch 0003, in Chapter 0122, at Saint Orra west, about the bench steriliser's own strip. Nothing defined *holder*.
- **The Weir, the annex, the retainer, the two-refusal question and the day after, the burnt letter, the boy of nineteen, the map's margin, Dev Singh's empty-crossing clause and the Selah Mora log are all unresolved and all forbidden to resolve. Margo Doré has not been asked anything since 1 June, is not angry and has not forgiven him, and nobody went up that path in this batch. A pump was not offered and none was, and there are pumps for sale in four shops at about £240 and none of them has ever been in a van on that street. NO WEIR RESIDENT IS DESCRIBED IN ANY DOCUMENT THAT LEAVES THE STREET, and nobody on the Weir has told the county, the department or a contractor that anything exists.**

## 6. The name in eleven people

- **BERNICE OFORI, 34, IS A NAME IN ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE IN A METHODIST HALL IN TIDEGLASS AND IN NO DOCUMENT, and there is no list of who has it and there is not going to be one, and nobody is to go down there, and nobody has, and NOTHING IN BATCH 0003 REFERS TO HER IN ANY WAY.**
- A name said in a room cannot be un-said, produced, checked, argued with, minuted, published, attached to a claim, or found by anybody who is not one of the eleven.
- **The two pieces of paper are her letter of 28 November, in a handbag in a kitchen off a landing, and her own four lines of 21 January at the top of a sheet of A4 that already carried four lines of the minute-taker's biro, in a plastic wallet in a drawer of the trestle table with NO KEY on it.** Neither is a document, neither has a crest, a form number, a reference or a box, and neither can be used to show anybody anything. **HER NAME IS NOT ON THE PRINTED SLIP FROM COMMITTEE SERVICES AND MAY NOT BE PUT ON IT.** Neither has been opened and neither is going to be.
- **BATCH 0004 MUST NOT: put that name in a form, in a minute, in a return, in a holder field or in any document; send anybody down there; have her rescued, helped, given water, given a seat or given anything; have her thank anybody; or resolve what her standing now is.**

## 7. The reason column, refused twice

- **THE REGISTER OF NARROWINGS IS AT NINETEEN AND DID NOT MOVE ONCE IN BATCH 0003. THE BLANK LINE UNDER THE NINETEENTH IS BLANK AND HAS BEEN BLANK SINCE 26 NOVEMBER 2014 — a hundred and seven days on 13 March, the longest it has ever been still since 4 August. Entry 19 is a yard light, the maintenance is a bulb, and the bulb is out.**
- **A REASON COLUMN WAS ASKED FOR IN WRITING TWICE AND REFUSED TWICE, AND IT IS NOT A LIST, AND THE WORDS WERE SAID OUT LOUD IN A ROOM IN DAYLIGHT IN FEBRUARY: IT IS NOT A LIST. Five words in biro on the back of the left hand of the man of about fifty-six, who is not a member, is not on any list, and has never been asked what is on his hand and is never going to be.** M. Furneaux, 61, rear block, Carrow Lane, wrote once on 2 January, was refused, and has not written and is not going to before about 2018. **HOWARD BRAMBLE, 61**, fourth floor of a block of forty flats in Tideglass, wrote in person on 16 February and was refused in about eleven seconds in the right order, and said afterwards that the refusal was right and that in about four years there is going to be nothing at all to put on the table for a frightened person in a kitchen. **THE TWO MEN ARE NOT TO BE CONFUSED AND THE COINCIDENCE OF THEIR AGE IS NOT A PATTERN AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH IS TO BE ALLOWED TO MAKE IT INTO ONE.**
- **NINA CALDER'S STANDING COUNT OF REFUSALS IS SEVEN AND SHE DID NOT ADD TO IT. THE SECOND REFUSAL WAS CHEAPER THAN THE FIRST, NOT EASIER, BECAUSE BY THE SECOND TIME BOTH OF THEM KNEW WHAT IT WAS FOR, AND IT COST HER ABOUT NINE MINUTES INSTEAD OF ELEVEN SECONDS. Nobody is going to be able to argue a refusal out of her by getting better at it, and that is a problem for a later volume.**
- A form with no name box on it has now been used to find a building three times, all of them by careful people doing exactly what it was designed to do, and there is nobody to blame except a man with a register who asked for a state and got one. A design is not a promise.

## 8. The two records, the reading rule, the page of four

- Record one, September: shapes, sixteen, no name box by decision. Record two, November: **51 nodes, 38 parties, 13 shapes, as at 18:00 on Monday 6 October 2014, checkable at a counter by anybody for nothing by post as of a date, UNSIGNED, no name box, about four hundred names added to it, about eleven extracts taken, two write-backs that were wrong, and the clerk's *enclosure received, no comment* in the file.** The difference is **four**. Calder can say what three are and will not say the fourth; Jonas Mercer read three and refused the fourth in a room and will not write it down. **Unresolved. Do not resolve.**
- **THE READING RULE HAS BEEN USED ONCE, IN A CORRIDOR, IN ABOUT ELEVEN SECONDS, ON AN UNDATED DAY IN JANUARY 2015, BY FRANCES TOLLEY, 61, TO PETER NAYLOR, 44, WHO WAS NOT TOLD HE WAS IN IT. She is not going to say whether it worked and nobody may ask her. He is not going to be told and nobody may ask him one question about any of it, ever. The one copy is still in her inside pocket and goes to Salter's Yard on a Tuesday, forty-one minutes each way, on her own time, and nobody knows.** Unused in this batch and it may not be used again in Volume 03.
- **Page four of section 11 is a dissolution clause at six vacancies, read by exactly three people and told to nobody: FRANCES TOLLEY, OREN PELL, NINA CALDER. On 28 January Jonas Mercer said out loud in a room of nine, in daylight, that he is NOT one of the three and is not going to say who they are, and is not going to say what is on it in a room, and asked for that to be minuted as a decision and not as a virtue. HE HAS NEVER READ IT. It may not be disclosed in Volume 03. Nobody is to act on it and nobody is to put it in front of the ninth seat.**

## 9. The winter and the dates that are not written down

- **A TERM IS SIX DAYS. IT IS READ AT 22:00 ON THE SATURDAY NIGHT BEFORE THE SUNDAY IT STARTS ON, AND THE GATE OPENS AT 07:00 ON THAT SUNDAY, AND THE TERM EXPIRES AT 06:00 ON THE FOLLOWING SUNDAY. THE SATURDAY NIGHTS ARE WHAT ARE A WEEK APART. NEVER CALL THE TERM SEVEN DAYS AND NEVER CALL IT A WEEK.** The chain is: 24 Jan, 31 Jan, 7, 14, 21, 28 Feb, 7 and 14 March 2015, all at 22:00, and **there is no date written down anywhere in this city for a single one of them and there is not going to be one, because a person is not required to be told and there is nobody in this borough who is required to write it.**
- **WHO READ THEM ALOUD, on every one since 3 January 2015: EZRA MBEKI, in and out of that building since 1992, and TREVOR NUNN, 58, a night porter, nineteen years, who is not on a roster because the roster does not exist. Neither is on any instrument. Neither knew until about nine o'clock. It took about nine minutes and then they sat down and went home at about half past six. JONAS MERCER HAS NEVER BEEN TOLD THAT ANY OF THEM OCCURRED, HAS NEVER ASKED, AND IS NOT GOING TO ASK.**
- **HE WAS IN THAT CORRIDOR ONCE, AT HALF PAST FOUR IN THE MORNING ON SUNDAY 4 JANUARY 2015, AND THE GATE HAS NEVER ONCE BEEN OPENED BY ANYTHING HE DID IN IT. He stood four hundred yards away in the rain at ten o'clock on 3 January and did not go. He did not go on 1 February and DID NOT DECIDE NOT TO GO, and that silence is the whole of why he could then refuse the next one out loud: A FOURTH IS A HABIT AND A HABIT IS A ROTA WITH ONE NAME IN IT. On Monday 2 March 2015, in daylight, in a ground-floor corridor, he told BRAM OSEEI out loud, on purpose, that he was not going to be in that room on Saturday 7 March. Bram Osei has told nobody. It cost nothing and it is the only thing in the batch he got right by not doing it.**
- **THE NEXT ONE IS SATURDAY 14 MARCH 2015 AT TEN O'CLOCK AT NIGHT, THE EIGHTH OF THEM IN SEVEN WEEKS, AND THERE IS NO DATE ON IT AND NO FORM ON WHICH ANYBODY COULD RECORD THAT IT HAPPENED.**
- The 11:00–13:30 row is still empty and still does not say *vacant*. Bram Osei's Thursday is still refused in his own handwriting with *no reason I can give* under a *no reason given* in somebody else's hand, and nobody has looked at it. Priya Ranganathan's card is still on the Friday and Saturday rows because she put it there. **The rota has still not been rebuilt and cannot be.**
- A counter in this borough was asked nothing about the gate by anybody else and looked for nothing about the gate, and the reason given out loud is that a counter with an opinion about a gate is a service. **NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH MAY LET THAT COUNTER ANSWER ANYTHING ABOUT A GATE, OR ABOUT A ROTA, OR ABOUT A COMPLIANCE PARAGRAPH, OR ABOUT A PERSON. A COUNTER THAT ANSWERS ABOUT ANY OF THOSE IS A SERVICE.** The claims box is closed and empty. The sealed envelope is in a drawer on a lower floor and may not be opened or written into.

## 10. The two conditions on the wall

- **SIXTEEN INCHES APART AND NEVER RECONCILED AND NOT RECONCILED IN BATCH 0003.** The city's own paper, printed 14 August, with two letters in the corner in a hand that is not a capital hand. And an enamel strip with nothing on its face, undated, unattributed, two letters and one word in pencil on the back of it, and the word is *not mine*. **THE TWO LETTERS ON THE BACK OF THE STRIP ARE NEVER DECIDED IN PROSE. The 1 March line is not reissued, restated or quoted. Nothing defines *holder*.**
- **NINA CALDER REFUSED OUT LOUD, UNASKED, TO SAY WHICH ONE IS OPERATIVE, and gave the reason, and the reason is the same reason Jonas Mercer gave in a room on 28 January about a page of a regulation from 2008: `if you ask me on the second of June I shall tell you the same thing in the same words.` She would rather stand under a contradiction for another four years than be the woman who settled it. UNRESOLVED. THE MOST DANGEROUS SENTENCE ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH COULD HAVE SAID IN FEBRUARY WAS *THAT IS THE RULES*, NOBODY SAID IT, AND ONE OF THE TWO WOULD HAVE BEEN RIGHT IF THEY HAD.**
- **The batch's finding on that wall, and it is physical: a person walking under two conditions that do not agree goes faster. A contradiction is not a pause, it is a floor that is not level, and everybody crosses a floor that is not level faster than a floor that is. And the corollary: THE FLOOR OF A SERVICE IS A PLACE WHERE A PERSON CAN STAND UNDER A CONTRADICTION AND BE ANSWERED. It is eleven feet by nine and it is not going on a wall.**

## 11. The refusals, eleven of them, none argued out

The mortuary porter's four words in nine seconds · Marguerite Oyelaran's nine seconds and a person-box · Emeka Rusk's, and helping does not go on a form · Margo Doré's advance refusal of a pump · Sanaa Iqbal's twenty minutes a week, **and the compliance paragraph behind it is unanswered and unanswered is the standing state** · Ivo Bamba's, spent in Chapter 90, not reused · Nina Calder's fifth page, **and the seventh of 2 January, and a second refusal of the same request on 16 February which is not the eighth because it is not a list** · Yolanda Fofana's refusal to answer the day after **and Tomas Renn's question, still unanswered, still the engine, which Batch 0004 may not resolve and may not let anybody else manufacture** · Frances Tolley's, in a corridor, on 26 November, and a fifth on 22 January, the price given out loud · Bernice Ofori's, and a second on 21 January, in nine seconds, in her own hand, on a sheet that is not a form · **and a man of forty-eight, in a corridor in August, about a job in November having a department in it, whom nobody has asked and whom Jonas Mercer has now refused out loud to go and find.**

**Nobody refuses to be thanked anywhere in Volume 03. No minute records that a person has not been thanked, and the ninth seat is not thanked for anything. The one place in Batch 0003 where a character came close to it was cut.**

## 12. Still reserved, and forbidden to any batch in this volume

No root reading, no root clause, no succession, no Iona, no Evan, no counter-amendment, no second signature, no Quiet Cut, no origin of the community-watch term, no three-line brass tokens. **Volumes 07, 09 and 15.** **Prue Calloway's location-of-record finding of 23 April was OPENED ONCE, IN A ROOM, IN DAYLIGHT, ON 28 JANUARY 2015, AND IT IS NOT RESOLVED, AND IT MAY NOT BE OPENED A SECOND TIME; what may be argued afterwards is its consequence, and nobody in this borough has been made to live under the consequence yet and nobody is going to be. The claim to open a door in this volume is spent.** The forty minutes of 15 April is time until the family knew; **no 48-minute figure may ever be printed; the forty *hours* of 3 April is the gate being open and is a different number about a different thing; and no other figure of minutes exists anywhere in this borough.** Nine hundred and forty is a number of times, nine hundred and eighty-six is a number of Wednesdays, and fourteen is a difference between two public numbers; **none of the three is a number of people, none may be, and they have never been in one sentence and must not be — and nine hundred and eighty-six appears exactly once in Chapters 121–130, correctly labelled.** A priced-access case is not a recognition case. A node with no term in force is not a person with no address. A duty is an act and not a place. **A person who is not counted is not a person who is helped, and Volume 03 may not refute that sentence in a chapter.**

# VOLUME 03 OPEN THREADS — as they stand after Chapter 140, Wednesday 29 April 2015

**Read with the "VOLUME 03, BATCH 0004 — CHAPTERS 131–140" block at the end of `state/continuity.md`, which carries the canon. This file carries only what is still open and what it is forbidden to do. Volume 03 is OPEN and runs to Chapter 144. Chapters 141–144 are the volume close and have not been written.**

## 1. The counter, the shelf, and the drift

- **THE SHELF IS A DRAWER UNDER THE GROUND-FLOOR COUNTER WITH NO KEY ON IT AND NO LIST OF WHAT IS IN IT, ONE HAND, DATED, UNSIGNED, NO REFERENCE, NO BOX, NO NAME ON ANY OF IT INCLUDING NINA CALDER'S, AND IT IS GROWING.** There were nine on 9 March 2015 and there are more now and **nobody can say how many, which is correct, and Jonas Mercer has not counted it, has not looked at it, and is not going to, and she is not going to tell him.** A tenth slip went out on 16 March 2015 to a man of about sixty with a galvanised can, about the trough by the lock-ups, and it is in the inside of a coat. **There is no form in this borough on which anybody could write that they are not a series, and there is not going to be one, and a box that says *not a series* is a series.**
- **NINE SLIPS IS A COUNT OF DOCUMENTS. ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE A DAY IS A COUNT OF PEOPLE. THEY MAY NEVER BE ADDED TOGETHER.** A slip is only written when a person stands in front of her and asks what was true on a day. **A form that added the two would say *this counter sees eleven people and produces one document*, which is a level of service, and there is no level of service in that room.** No character in this batch knows either figure. **Jonas Mercer counted the people through the door ONCE, on 20 March, and stopped, and stopping is the finding and not a victory: a count of people through a door is a level of service the moment anybody can be held to it.**
- **THE DRIFT, AND IT IS NOW THE BATCH'S FINDING RATHER THAN ITS HYPOTHESIS. A STATE GIVEN ON A DAY IS A STATE ABOUT THE PERSON WHO GAVE IT.** A stale state belongs to a thing and a thing can be looked at; a contradicted state belongs to a person and a person cannot be looked at and cannot be asked, and the only way to move one is to ask the person, and asking a person is a service. **A STATE GIVEN ON A DAY STOPS BEING A STATE AND BECOMES AN OBJECT IN A COAT: it cannot be paid, cannot be returned, cannot be filed and cannot be destroyed, because there is no procedure on the sheet, so it goes in a pocket, and nobody comes back to argue it.** There is no obligation in this city to come back. **UNRESOLVED. Do not build an instrument for it. Do not write a form about it. The moment a form is written about any of this the volume has lost the thing it spent two volumes building.**
- **NOBODY CAME BACK TO ARGUE A STATE.** Abu Bakayoko's first slip, five nodes, 26 January, is seven weeks old in an inside pocket and has not been looked at since. He came on 2 and 13 February and has not come since. **If he comes on a Friday for another four years that is not a man, that is a rota with one name on it that nobody wrote down and that nobody could write down.** UNRESOLVED.
- **THE PEOPLE WHO COME IN ARE NOT THE PEOPLE WHO COME BACK, AND IT IS NOT A DEFECT, IT IS THE REASON THE ROOM WORKS.** A contradiction requires a second visit; a second visit is a decision; nobody in this borough is required to decide anything. **UNRESOLVED and it may not be argued out in Chapter 141–144.**
- **NINA CALDER IS PROVED RIGHT ABOUT THE SECOND HALF OF HER OWN SENTENCE, IN ADVANCE, AND DOES NOT KNOW.** On 10 April 2015 Jonas Mercer worked out that the shelf, not the bulb, is what will be held over somebody in about four years, and that she is going to be proved right about a register that grows when nobody is hurt, and **he is not going to tell her, and the reason he wrote down is that if he told her she would be the woman who was warned. UNRESOLVED and may not be resolved.**
- **THE FUTURE, STATED TWICE AND BINDING: a drawer of slips grows out of correct things done by careful people, nobody is hurt by it, and in about four years somebody is going to hand a piece of paper to a frightened person in a kitchen, and it is not going to have a bulb on it, it is going to have nine dates and one hand on it. A SLIP IN A DRAWER IS GOING TO BE EVIDENCE ONE DAY, HANDED OVER BY A PERSON WHO THINKS THEY ARE HELPING. And there is no version of that piece of paper in which the person it was written for is in it, and nobody is going to be able to say afterwards whether it was a service or a choice. UNRESOLVED.**
- **A COUNTER IS THE CHEAPEST PRIVACY DEVICE ANYBODY IN THIS CITY HAS BUILT AND IT COST NOTHING AND NOBODY DESIGNED IT. FINDABILITY IS WHAT A PRICE DOES WHEN THERE IS NO COUNTER IN FRONT OF IT. The man who turns the bucket at the board of forty slots at a bus turning head has been the most findable man in the lowland for twenty-six years and nobody did it, and Jonas Mercer refused out loud on 20 March not to go and find him, and the reason is THE FINDER IS THE HANDLE. UNRESOLVED. He is not on any list and has not been asked anything and will not be.**
- **THE PRICE OF FINDABILITY CAME BACK AT HIM IN HIS OWN PERSON ON 24 APRIL 2015, when a stranger asked the counter whether the man who stands at the wall is on a list, and the counter was right, and the answer cost four seconds, and he stood there for about nine minutes and said nothing and there was no point at which he could have said anything. IT DOES NOT PROTECT ANYBODY. IT ONLY MEANS NOBODY IS AIMING.**

## 2. The two open objects

- **A CARD IN THE SLOT FOR POST, PUT THERE ON OR ABOUT FRIDAY 20 FEBRUARY 2015, AND AT THE END OF CHAPTER 140, WEDNESDAY 29 APRIL, IT HAD BEEN IN IT FOR SIXTY-EIGHT DAYS.** Four inches by two, folded once, not a form, no crest, no reference, no box, no envelope. About ninety people saw it before 20 March and it has been in that room six weeks longer since. **NOBODY HAS TAKEN IT OUT AND NOBODY HAS PUT IT UP. NINA CALDER HAS NOT READ IT AND IS NOT GOING TO.** A slot that gets emptied is a rule, a rule is a service, and hers is the name it would have. **WHAT IS ON THE CARD IS NOT KNOWN TO ANYBODY IN THIS ACCOUNT AND MAY NOT BE STATED.**
- **A LETTER IN A TRAY ON THE SECOND FLOOR, PUT THERE ON WEDNESDAY 18 FEBRUARY 2015 AT ABOUT HALF PAST NINE, AND AT THE END OF CHAPTER 140, WEDNESDAY 29 APRIL, IT HAD BEEN THERE SEVENTY DAYS.** Not on borough stock. A real departmental reference in the correct typeface. About four lines long. It is not a reason column. It is the smallest request anybody in this borough has made since the first of March. **Nina Calder has not opened it in front of a second person, has told nobody it is there, has not moved it to the ground-floor tray, and has not answered it. SOMEBODY HAS NOW STARTED TO LEAVE POST ON TOP OF IT.** Jonas Mercer saw the tray on 10 April, did not move the post, does not know what is under it and is never going to. **A piece of paper under two other pieces of paper is a piece of paper that has stopped being dealt with, and there is no instrument in this city that could go and find a thing like that on a tray and ask what it is.** Its contents are not stated in this account, are not known to anybody, and may not be stated in a room or opened in a document. **IT WAS NOT OPENED IN THIS BATCH AND IT IS NOT GOING TO BE OPENED IN CHAPTERS 141–144. UNRESOLVED and live.**

## 3. The three men and the man of thirty-eight

- **PETER NAYLOR, 44.** A wall. **NOT ASKED ANYTHING, EVER, IN THIS BATCH. Not in any room, not in any corridor in it, only in a sentence.** He has a fortnight somewhere in this city with a bucket under a tap and **there is nobody able to ask him whether he has got a coat, and nobody helped him. UNRESOLVED and must stay unresolved.**
- **ROYDEN ACHEBE, 49.** A door, which is not a favour. **STILL NOT TOLD, BY NOBODY, AND NOT ASKED AND NOT RUNG IN THIS BATCH. The five routes are unchanged and every one of them is a document and a person who will remember it.** He is forty-nine, he is not untouchable, there is no argument in this borough in which not asking him is the right thing to do, and he is not going to be asked. Findability and askability are two different currencies and this borough has been minting the first one very successfully and has not spent one unit of the second. UNRESOLVED.
- **THE MAN OF FORTY-EIGHT.** Not a wall and not a door. **HAS NOT BEEN ASKED, HAS NOT BEEN RUNG, AND JONAS MERCER HAS SAID OUT LOUD IN A CORRIDOR THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO LOOK FOR HIM, TWICE now, and the reason is that the only instrument in this borough that keeps a person from being askable is a person who does not know.** The compliance paragraph is unanswered and is not going to be. UNRESOLVED.
- **EMMANUEL OTIENO, 38.** **GAVE HIS METHOD TO THREE PEOPLE AND TOLD NOBODY AND WROTE NOTHING DOWN, and the discovery has travelled sideways to at least three more, and a girl on a till has used it on a landlord, and a man of thirty-four in a canteen said on 25 March that it is not strange any more. IT IS THE FIRST TIME IN NINE MONTHS THAT ANYTHING HAS COME OUT OF THAT COUNTER AND TOUCHED A DISTRICT, and Jonas Mercer does not know which of the three it is and is not going to find out. HE ASKED HIS ONE QUESTION OF THE WHOLE BATCH IN A CORRIDOR AND HE WILL NOT ASK A SECOND: I AM THE PERSON WHO MADE IT CHEAP.** **NOTHING MAY BE WRITTEN DOWN ABOUT THE THREE, OR ABOUT WHICH DISTRICT ONE OF THEM WORKS IN, IN CHAPTERS 141–144. UNRESOLVED, and it travels, and nobody may stop it.**

## 4. The board, the seats, the minute-taker and the ninth

- **SEVEN OF NINE, unchanged. The seventh chair empty since Wednesday 8 October 2014; the eighth since the evening of Wednesday 26 November 2014. Nobody can fill either and nobody can unfill either and there is no power in this city that could.**

- Four of the nine have said they will stop if it turns into a job. One has given a reason and **asked for it not to be written down, and it is not in the minute, and that was agreed and both of those were correct.** Nobody has stopped. Nobody has been asked again.
- **THE NINTH SEAT is findable at a shopfront, unpaid, doing it for nothing since 4 July, the only door in a building of thirty-one rented rooms, and nobody has told her and nobody is going to. Do not resolve it. Do not have the board notice it. Do not tell her. Do not have the eight learn what she can do.**
- **THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THE MINUTES IS THE SAME PERSON WHO STANDS AT THAT COUNTER, AND THAT IS NOW CANON.** Her name is in a box on the sheet that says *minute taken by* and that is the only name-box on the ground floor. **The sheet is the minute until about four o'clock and after that it is only a sheet, and she is the only person in this city who can end a minute by picking one up and putting it down.** If any box ever tied that to the counter it would be a rota with one name in it. **She is not on the board, not on a rota, and cannot be made to be on one. AND THERE IS ONE RECORDS SUPERVISOR IN THIS ACCOUNT AND SHE IS HER: every use of *a records supervisor of about fifty in a vestibule eleven feet by nine* means her and never a second person. The woman of about fifty at the counter on the second floor who has been there eleven years is a different person, on the same floor and the same age, and the two have never been confused for each other in public. THE DRAWER UNDER THE COUNTER IS NOT A FILE: she keeps a copy of every state she has ever written, which is not a copy of a document because there is no document, and a man walks out with a state in a pocket, and neither piece of paper is a record of the other.**
- **ON 8 APRIL 2015 SHE SAID ONE SENTENCE ABOUT HERSELF, OUT LOUD, IN A ROOM WITH ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE IN IT, AND NOBODY ASKED HER, AND IT IS IN ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE AND IN NO DOCUMENT, and it is in one hand in a pad in a shop that goes nowhere, and she knew that and let it. NOBODY MAY ASK HER A QUESTION ABOUT IT AFTERWARDS, NOBODY MAY IMPROVE IT, NOBODY MAY ASK HER WHETHER SHE IS GOING TO PUT IT IN THE MINUTE, AND SHE DID NOT AND IT IS NOT IN ANY MINUTE. Nobody may open the pad and nobody may ask what is in it. HER NINE WORDS STAND.**

## 9. The winter and the dates that are not written down

- **A TERM IS SIX DAYS. IT IS READ AT 22:00 ON THE SATURDAY NIGHT BEFORE THE SUNDAY IT STARTS ON, AND THE GATE OPENS AT 07:00 ON THAT SUNDAY, AND THE TERM EXPIRES AT 06:00 ON THE FOLLOWING SUNDAY. THE SATURDAY NIGHTS ARE WHAT ARE A WEEK APART. NEVER CALL THE TERM SEVEN DAYS. NEVER CALL IT A WEEK.** **THE ONLY CORRECT NUMBERING IS: 24 Jan = 1 · 31 Jan = 2 · 7 Feb = 3 · 14 Feb = 4 · 21 Feb = 5 · 28 Feb = 6 · 7 Mar = 7 · 14 Mar = 8 · 21 Mar = 9 · 28 Mar = 10 · 4 Apr = 11 · 11 Apr = 12 · 18 Apr = 13 · 25 Apr = 14 · 2 May = 15. (The Chapter 0135 card as issued said 28 March was the eleventh. It is the tenth. The corrected numbering is binding.)** **Fourteen Saturday nights have been reached and there is no date written down anywhere in this city for a single one of them and there is not going to be one. THE TERM IN FORCE AT THE END OF CHAPTER 140 IS THE FOURTEENTH AND IT EXPIRES AT 06:00 ON SUNDAY 3 MAY 2015. THE FIFTEENTH IS READ AT 22:00 ON SATURDAY 2 MAY 2015.**
- **WHO READ THEM ALOUD, on every one since 3 January 2015: EZRA MBEKI, in and out of that building since 1992, and TREVOR NUNN, 58, a night porter, nineteen years, who is not on a roster because the roster does not exist. Neither is on any instrument. Neither knew until about nine o'clock. It took about nine minutes and then they sat down and went home at about half past six. NOTHING WAS WRITTEN DOWN AND NOTHING IS GOING TO BE. NEITHER HAS EVER BEEN ASKED ABOUT ANY OF IT AND NEITHER EVER WILL BE, AND THE REASON IS NOT DISCRETION — IT IS THAT THERE IS NOTHING TO ASK ABOUT. NEITHER OF THEM KNOWS THAT A MAN REFUSED OUT LOUD, IN A CORRIDOR, IN DAYLIGHT, ON 2 MARCH 2015, TO BE IN THAT ROOM, AND NEITHER IS GOING TO BE TOLD.**
- **THE ONLY HANDOVER THAT WAS EVER STAGED IN CHAPTERS 131–140 IS THE EIGHTH, AT 22:00 ON SATURDAY 14 MARCH 2015, IN CHAPTER 0131. AND `state/continuity.md` §0b ITEM 8 IS THE LINE THAT AUTHORISES ONE FURTHER STAGING IN THE CLOSE: THE FIFTEENTH, AT 22:00 ON SATURDAY 2 MAY 2015, IN CHAPTER 0144 ONLY, ONCE.** No minute, no log entry, no clipboard line, no date and no name beyond Mbeki and Nunn, and a sixteenth falls at 22:00 on Saturday 9 May 2015 inside that chapter and is neither staged nor named nor dated.
- **THE HOUR BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN EXISTS ON EVERY SUNDAY AND WAS STAGED IN EXACTLY THREE CHAPTERS OF BATCH 0004 — 0135 (Sunday 5 April), 0137 (Sunday 12 April) and 0139 (Sunday 26 April) — AND IT WAS NOT STAGED ON 3 APRIL UNTIL A LATER PASS CORRECTED THE HOUR: 3 APRIL IS TWENTY PAST SIX AND IS IN THAT HOUR, AND THE CORRIDOR VISIT OF 4 JANUARY WAS AT HALF PAST FOUR, WHICH IS THE LAST HOUR AND A HALF OF A TERM AND NOT THAT HOUR AT ALL. `state/continuity.md` §0b ITEM 7 IS NOW THE LINE THAT SAYS SO: the hour may be staged ONE LAST TIME, in Chapter 0144 only, and only if Chapter 0144 says on the page that it is the last time it is going to be staged in Volume 03. No fourth staging is authorised and none may be invented in Chapters 0141–0143.** **The finding of Chapter 0137: THE HOUR CANNOT BE SCHEDULED WITHOUT SOMEBODY BEING REQUIRED TO BE IN IT, SO THE ONLY WAY TO KEEP THE HOUR IS TO KEEP IT UNSCHEDULED, AND THE INSTRUMENT THAT KEEPS IT UNSCHEDULED IS THE SAME INSTRUMENT THAT HAS BEEN DOING EVERYTHING ELSE FOR NINE MONTHS, WHICH IS TWO PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT ON ANYTHING TURNING UP AT TEN O'CLOCK ON A SATURDAY BECAUSE NOBODY WROTE ANYTHING DOWN. It is the cheapest thing in this borough and it is the only thing that works and there is no instrument for it and there cannot be one.**

FROM FOUR HUNDRED YARDS YOU CANNOT TELL WHETHER A DOOR IS OPEN OR SHUT, AND THAT IS THE CORRECT AMOUNT OF EVIDENCE. - **NINA CALDER REFUSED OUT LOUD, UNASKED, TO SAY WHICH ONE IS OPERATIVE, and gave the reason, and she would rather stand under a contradiction for another four years than be the woman who settled it. UNRESOLVED. THE MOST DANGEROUS SENTENCE ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH COULD HAVE SAID WAS *THAT IS THE RULES*, NOBODY SAID IT, AND ONE OF THE TWO WOULD HAVE BEEN RIGHT IF THEY HAD.**
- The batch's finding on that wall, and it is physical: a person walking under two conditions that do not agree goes faster. A contradiction is not a pause, it is a floor that is not level, and everybody crosses a floor that is not level faster than a floor that is. And the corollary: THE FLOOR OF A SERVICE IS A PLACE WHERE A PERSON CAN STAND UNDER A CONTRADICTION AND BE ANSWERED. It is eleven feet by nine and it is not going on a wall.

## 1. The counter, the shelf, and the drift

- **THE SHELF IS A DRAWER UNDER THE GROUND-FLOOR COUNTER WITH NO KEY ON IT AND NO LIST OF WHAT IS IN IT, ONE HAND, DATED, UNSIGNED, NO REFERENCE, NO BOX, NO NAME ON ANY OF IT INCLUDING NINA CALDER'S, AND IT IS GROWING. There are more in it than there were in February and NOBODY CAN SAY HOW MANY, WHICH IS CORRECT, INCLUDING JONAS MERCER, INCLUDING THE NARRATION. The only figure that has ever existed is NINE, as at 9 March 2015. She keeps a copy of every state she has written, which is not a copy of a document because there is no document and there never was one, and the state a man carries in a pocket is the same state about a day and NEITHER PIECE OF PAPER IS A RECORD OF THE OTHER. There is no form in this borough on which anybody could write that they are not a series, and a box that says *not a series* is a series. UNRESOLVED, and the most load-bearing unresolved thread in the book.**
- **AND A STATE WITH NO OWNER AT ALL IS NOT IN THE DRAWER. IT IS IN A VAN, IN A CARDBOARD WALLET, THREE OF THEM, SINCE ABOUT CHRISTMAS, AND NOBODY OWNS THEM AND NOBODY CAN BE ASKED WHO THEY ARE — NOT THE COUNTER, NOT THE MAN, NOT ANYBODY. The answer to what that is going to become is nothing, because there is no instrument, and that is correct. UNRESOLVED.**
- **NINE SLIPS IS A COUNT OF DOCUMENTS. ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE A DAY IS A COUNT OF PEOPLE. THEY MAY NEVER BE ADDED TOGETHER AND WERE NEVER ADDED IN NINE MONTHS. No character in either of the last two phases knows either figure and neither of them was stated.**
- **THE DRIFT WAS NEVER ANSWERED, BY NOBODY, EVER. A STATE GIVEN ON A DAY IS A STATE ABOUT THE PERSON WHO GAVE IT. A stale state belongs to a THING and a thing can be looked at; a contradicted state belongs to a PERSON and cannot be looked at and cannot be asked about, and nobody may be required to answer anything including a correction. THE ONLY THING HE DID ABOUT IT WAS LOOK AT THE TROUGH ON 30 APRIL AND WRITE NOTHING DOWN, and the reason is that a second state that did not agree would be a contradiction that could never be moved by anybody, ever, and he would have been the one who put it there. UNRESOLVED. Do not build an instrument for it. Do not write a form about it.**
- **NOBODY CAME BACK TO ARGUE A STATE. THE MAN OF FIFTY-EIGHT CAME ON 2 FEBRUARY AND 13 FEBRUARY AND HAS NOT COME SINCE THE MIDDLE OF FEBRUARY, and he is right to, and his three pieces of paper are in one inside pocket and the two about the trough and the one about five nodes. A piece of paper in an inside pocket has become a thing with a routine. A ROUTINE WITH ONE MAN IN IT IS A ROTA AND NOBODY WROTE THE ROTA, and Jonas Mercer worked out in April that he could not stop it and all three ways were closed, and he has not tried again and is not going to. UNRESOLVED. THE MAN WITH THE GALVANISED CAN HAS NOT BEEN BACK SINCE 20 APRIL AND IS CARRYING ONE SLIP, and nobody can ask him whether he has been given another one. UNRESOLVED.**
- **NINA CALDER IS PROVED RIGHT ABOUT THE SECOND HALF OF HER OWN SENTENCE AND DOES NOT KNOW, AND ON 30 APRIL SHE WAS PROVED RIGHT AGAIN, CORRECTLY, IN FRONT OF A MAN WITH THREE PIECES OF PAPER IN A WALLET, AND SHE IS NOT GOING TO BE TOLD ANY OF IT. Jonas Mercer knows and is not going to tell her, because if he told her she would be the woman who was warned. UNRESOLVED and may not be resolved.**
- **THE FUTURE, STATED TWICE AND BINDING: a drawer of slips grows out of correct things done by careful people, nobody is hurt by it, and in about four years somebody is going to hand a piece of paper to a frightened person in a kitchen, and it is not going to have a bulb on it, it is going to have a date and a hand on it. A SLIP IN A DRAWER IS GOING TO BE EVIDENCE ONE DAY, HANDED OVER BY A PERSON WHO THINDS THEY ARE HELPING. And there is no version of it in which the person it was written for is in it. UNRESOLVED.**
- **FINDABILITY IS WHAT A PRICE DOES WHEN THERE IS NO COUNTER IN FRONT OF IT. The man who turns the bucket on the board of forty slots has been the most findable man in the lowland for twenty-six years and nobody did it, and Jonas Mercer refused out loud not to go and find him, and the price of findability came back at him in his own person on 24 April and was paid on 1 May by somebody else on a landing. A FINDABLE PERSON IS A PERSON WHO CAN BE ASKED, and the cost of it lands on the person who is asked and not on the person who asked, and it is paid by a person nobody is counting. UNRESOLVED.**

## 2. The two open objects

- **A CARD IN THE SLOT FOR POST, PUT THERE ON OR ABOUT FRIDAY 20 FEBRUARY 2015, EIGHTY-TWO DAYS AT THE END OF CHAPTER 144. Four inches by two, folded once, not a form, no crest, no reference, no box, no envelope. NOBODY HAS TAKEN IT OUT AND NOBODY HAS PUT IT UP. NINA CALDER HAS NOT READ IT AND IS NOT GOING TO. A slot that gets emptied is a rule, a rule is a service, and hers is the name it would have. WHAT IS ON THE CARD IS NOT KNOWN TO ANYBODY IN THIS ACCOUNT AND MAY NOT BE STATED.**
- **A LETTER IN A TRAY ON THE SECOND FLOOR, PUT THERE ON WEDNESDAY 18 FEBRUARY 2015 AT ABOUT HALF PAST NINE, EIGHTY-FOUR DAYS AT THE END OF CHAPTER 144. Not on borough stock. A real departmental reference in the correct typeface. About four lines long. It is not a reason column. IT IS STILL SEALED. NOBODY HAS OPENED IT IN FRONT OF A SECOND PERSON, NOBODY HAS TOLD NOBODY IT IS THERE, NOBODY HAS PUT IT IN THE GROUND-FLOOR TRAY, AND NOBODY HAS ANSWERED IT, and somebody has started to leave post on top of it, which is the oldest way in the world of saying a thing has not been dealt with. **WHAT IS UNDER THE POST IS NOT KNOWN TO ANYBODY IN THIS ACCOUNT AND MAY NOT BE STATED IN A ROOM OR IN A DOCUMENT, AND NO CHAPTER IN VOLUME 03 OPENED IT AND NO CHAPTER IN VOLUME 04 MAY OPEN IT WITHOUT A LINE IN `state/continuity.md` SAYING SO.** UNRESOLVED and live.**

## 3. The three men and the man of thirty-eight

- **PETER NAYLOR, 44. A WALL. NOT ASKED ANYTHING, EVER, IN THE CLOSE, not in a room, not in a corridor, only in a sentence. He has a fortnight somewhere in this city with a bucket under a tap and there is nobody able to ask him whether he has got a coat, and nobody helped him. UNRESOLVED, his fortnight is not restaged, and his thread is still open.**
- **ROYDEN ACHEBE, 49. A DOOR, WHICH IS NOT A FAVOUR. STILL NOT TOLD, BY NOBODY, BY NO ROUTE, AND NOT ASKED AND NOT RUNG IN THE CLOSE. He is forty-nine, he is not untouchable, there is no argument in this borough in which not asking him is the right thing to do, and he is not going to be asked. Findability and askability are two different currencies and this borough has been minting the first one very successfully and has not spent one unit of the second. UNRESOLVED, left exactly where it was, and no chapter may resolve it.**
- **THE MAN OF FORTY-EIGHT. Not a wall and not a door. NOT ASKED, NOT RUNG, NOT FOUND, and Jonas Mercer has said out loud in a corridor that he is not going to, twice. The compliance paragraph is unanswered and is not going to be. UNRESOLVED.**
- **EMMANUEL OTIENO, 38. GAVE HIS METHOD TO THREE PEOPLE AND TOLD NOBODY AND WROTE NOTHING DOWN, and the discovery has travelled sideways to at least three more, and a girl on a till has used it on a landlord, and a man of thirty-four in a canteen said on 25 March that it is not strange any more. IT HAS TOUCHED A DISTRICT AND IT HAS GONE FURTHER THAN HE KNOWS ABOUT AND WILL GO FURTHER, AND IT IS IN NO DOCUMENT AND CANNOT BE PRODUCED BY ANYBODY. HE ASKED NO QUESTION ABOUT IT IN THE CLOSE AND IS NEVER GOING TO ASK ONE, AND THE REASON IS EIGHT WORDS AND IT IS IN A PAD. NOTHING MAY BE WRITTEN DOWN ABOUT THE THREE, OR ABOUT WHICH DISTRICT ONE OF THEM WORKS IN. UNRESOLVED, and it travels, and nobody may stop it.**
- **AND ONE NEW THING, WHICH IS NOT A THREAD AND IS NOT TO BE MADE INTO ONE: a man of thirty-three who works nights read the minute of 27 April on a Saturday morning, asked the question it was never written to answer, was told the wall, got nothing, did not say the name of the person he was asking about, and put the paper in the inside of his coat. HE IS NOT ON ANY LIST AND HE IS NEVER GOING TO BE ASKED ANYTHING, and he is not a thread and he is not owed anything and he must not be followed up.**

- **SEVEN OF NINE, unchanged, TWO CHAIRS, THE SEVENTH EMPTY SINCE 8 OCTOBER 2014 AND THE EIGHTH SINCE THE EVENING OF 26 NOVEMBER 2014. Nobody can fill either and nobody can unfill either. THE BOARD HAS NOT MET SINCE THE MIDDLE OF JANUARY AND IS NOT REQUIRED TO. Any later volume that gives that body a chair, or that has it sitting, is wrong.**

- Four of the nine have said they will stop if it turns into a job. One gave a reason and asked for it not to be written down, and it is not in the minute. Nobody has stopped and nobody has been asked again.
- **THE NINTH SEAT is fifty-four and unpaid and findable at a shopfront and has been since the fourth of July, and the only door in a building of thirty-one rented rooms, and she has not been told and nobody is going to tell her, and Jonas Mercer said out loud in the close that he is not going to put himself in a room with her this week. Do not resolve it. Do not have the board notice it. Do not have the eight learn what she can do. Do not thank her for anything.**
- **THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS THE MINUTES IS THE SAME PERSON WHO STANDS AT THAT COUNTER, AND THERE IS ONE RECORDS SUPERVISOR IN THIS ACCOUNT AND SHE IS HER, with about nineteen years.** The woman of about fifty at the second-floor counter who has been there eleven years is a DIFFERENT PERSON, and a third woman, who comes down from the second floor at about ten and about three, is the same as the minute-taker, and the two are never merged. On 8 April 2015 the minute-taker said one sentence about herself, out loud, in a room, in daylight, unasked, and it is in about eleven people and in no document, and it is in one hand in a pad in a shop that goes nowhere, and she knew that and let it. **NOBODY MAY ASK HER A QUESTION ABOUT IT, NOBODY MAY IMPROVE IT, NOBODY MAY ASK HER WHETHER SHE IS GOING TO PUT IT IN A MINUTE, NOBODY MAY OPEN THE PAD, AND NOBODY ASKED HER AFTER THE CORRECT REFUSAL OF 30 APRIL.** HER NINE WORDS STAND. And she was correct on 30 April and was not asked afterwards and is not going to be.
- **WHAT A SERVICE FLOOR IS FOR, IN ONE SENTENCE, AND WHO SAID IT, AND ON WHAT DATE, AND IN WHICH MINUTE: `IT IS FOR THE PEOPLE THE BOROUGH CANNOT COUNT.` JONAS MERCER, Monday 27 April 2015, in a vestibule eleven feet by nine, in daylight, to a man of about fifty-six who is not on the agenda either, in a public minute with no names in it, Item 4. Nothing in that item requires any person to do anything. NONE OF THE THREE ANSWERS HAS BEEN REPEATED AND NONE HAS BEEN PLACED ON A WALL, AND THE SENTENCE OF 27 APRIL MAY NOT BE RESTATED, IMPROVED, ASKED FOR AGAIN, OR MADE INTO A FOURTH ANSWER, AND IT WAS NOT.**

- **The lowland is not on the layer, is not in the board's eight districts, cannot be seated, and has no seat. THE REASON IS NOT THAT IT CANNOT PAY. THE REASON IS THAT A NODE WITH NO PARTY IS NOT A PARTY, AND A PLACE IS NOT A PERSON, and that reason is correct and was not fixed in Volume 03 and may not be fixed by saying out loud what the floor is for. - **ABU BAKAYOKO, 58, CAME BACK TWICE IN FEBRUARY AND HAS NOT COME SINCE, and he is right to, and the batch may not make him un-right. HIS REFUSAL OF THE SIXTH STANDS, IS MINUTED, AND NOBODY MAY ASK HIM WHERE IT IS.**
- **NO SEVENTH SHAPE WAS ADDED TO ANY RECORD AND THE SEVENTH IS NOT RESOLVED. Nothing defines *holder*. The sixth of the six is still not named in any document that leaves the street, and the man is still not findable and the water still costs what it costs and nobody was helped.**
- **The Weir, the annex, the retainer, the two-refusal question and the day after, the burnt letter, the boy of nineteen, the map's margin, Dev Singh's empty-crossing clause and the Selah Mora log are all unresolved and all forbidden to resolve. Margo Doré has not been asked anything since 1 June, nobody went up that path in the close, a pump was not offered and none was, and NO WEIR RESIDENT IS DESCRIBED IN ANY DOCUMENT THAT LEAVES THE STREET — and nothing at all was written in Chapters 141–144, so nothing left any street. And the one thing said to the county from that street is a denial that something is there, and nobody has taken it off her.**

- **A WOMAN OF THIRTY-FOUR IS A NAME IN ABOUT ELEVEN PEOPLE IN A METHODIST HALL IN TIDEGLASS AND IN NO DOCUMENT, and there is no list of who has it and there is not going to be one, and nobody is to go down there, and nobody has, and NOT ONE SENTENCE OF CHAPTERS 141–144 NAMES HER, REFERENCES HER, OR PUTS HER IN ANY DOCUMENT.** The attribution stands: Jonas Mercer did not write the four lines and never claimed them; what he did was say her name out loud on Wednesday 3 December 2014, at about twenty to seven in the evening, in a room with about eleven people in it, in about nine seconds, and the minute says that a name was stated and does not contain it. **THE FOUR LINES AT THE TOP OF THAT SHEET OF A4 ARE THE MINUTE-TAKER'S, IN BIRO, WRITTEN ON 21 JANUARY 2015 IN ABOUT FOUR MINUTES ON A SECOND SHEET THAT HAD NOTHING AT THE TOP OF IT.** The other piece of paper is her letter in a handbag in a kitchen off a landing and nobody has been in that kitchen. **NEITHER IS A DOCUMENT. HER NAME IS NOT ON THE PRINTED SLIP AND MAY NOT BE PUT ON IT. NEITHER HAS BEEN OPENED AND NEITHER IS GOING TO BE.**
- **THERE IS NO NUMBER IN THIS BOROUGH FOR ANYBODY TO TELEPHONE AND IT IS NOT ON A LIST AND IT IS NOT GOING ON ONE, AND THE REASON IS FOUR WORDS LONG: NOBODY OWNS THAT NUMBER.** A body of nine seats cannot require a person to do anything, not a form, not a signature, not a telephone call, and not to attend a sitting it is not required to hold. **AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH CAN MAKE HER COME, AND NOBODY CAN EVEN MAKE ANYBODY TRY.**
- **THE PRINTED EXTRACT WENT BACK INTO A TRAY IN THAT HALL ON 30 MARCH 2015 IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS, AND THE PUTTING BACK IS THE ONLY THING ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS DONE ABOUT THAT PERSON IN FOUR MONTHS.**
- **A HOLE IN A RECORD IS THE ONLY HONEST THING ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS PRODUCED ABOUT A PERSON IN NINE MONTHS, AND THE PRICE OF IT IS EXACT: THE MOST EXPENSIVE THING ANYBODY IN THIS CITY HAS PRODUCED ABOUT A PERSON IN NINE MONTHS IS A PIECE OF A4 IN A DRAWER WITH NO KEY ON IT, and nobody is going to open it, and the price of that piece of paper is that a woman of thirty-four is a person this borough can say nothing about at all. Not nothing nearly. Nothing at all. And that is the correct outcome.**
- **VOLUME 04 MUST NOT: put that name in a form, a minute, a return, a holder field or any document; send anybody down there; have her rescued, helped, given water, given a seat or given anything; have her thank anybody; or resolve what her standing now is.**

- **THE REGISTER OF NARROWINGS IS AT NINETEEN AND DID NOT MOVE ONCE IN CHAPTERS 131–144, AND THE BLANK LINE UNDER THE NINETEENTH IS BLANK AND HAD BEEN BLANK FOR A HUNDRED AND SIXTY-EIGHT DAYS ON 13 MAY 2015 — THE LONGEST IT HAS EVER BEEN STILL SINCE 4 AUGUST, AND THE ONLY FIXED POINT IN THE BUILDING, AND THE SINGLE MOST LOAD-BEARING OBJECT IN VOLUME 03, WHICH ENDS WITH IT BLANK.** Entry 19 is a yard light, the maintenance is a bulb, and the bulb is out. **NINA CALDER WAS RIGHT ON 1 DECEMBER 2014 AND SHE IS STILL RIGHT AND BEING RIGHT ABOUT IT IS WHY THE LINE HAS STAYED EMPTY FOR LONGER THAN IT HAS EVER STAYED, AND SHE IS PROVED RIGHT ABOUT THE OTHER HALF OF IT AS WELL, AND SHE DOES NOT KNOW, AND NOBODY IS GOING TO TELL HER.**

Calder can say what three are and will not say the fourth; Jonas Mercer read three and refused the fourth in a room and will not write it down. **Unresolved. Do not resolve. Do not repeat the difference of fourteen in a sentence with nine hundred and forty or nine hundred and eighty-six in it.** The printed extract went back into a tray on 30 March 2015 and her name is not on it and may not be put on it.
- **THE READING RULE WAS NOT USED AGAIN AND MAY NOT BE USED AGAIN IN VOLUME 03.** Frances Tolley is not named in any chapter of the close, and the rule is referred to once, as an object in the inside pocket of a clerk of sixty-one on no board who is not going to be asked about it. **NOBODY MAY ASK HER WHETHER IT WORKED AND NOBODY MAY ASK PETER NAYLOR ONE QUESTION ABOUT ANY OF IT, EVER.** The one copy goes to Salter's Yard on a Tuesday, forty-one minutes each way, on her own time, and nobody knows.

HE HAS NEVER READ IT. It may not be disclosed in Volume 04. Nobody is to act on it and nobody is to put it in front of the ninth seat.**

## 9. The winter and the dates that are not written down

- **A TERM IS SIX DAYS. IT IS READ AT 22:00 ON THE SATURDAY NIGHT BEFORE THE SUNDAY IT STARTS ON, AND THE GATE OPENS AT 07:00 ON THAT SUNDAY, AND THE TERM EXPIRES AT 06:00 ON THE FOLLOWING SUNDAY. THE SATURDAY NIGHTS ARE WHAT ARE A WEEK APART. NEVER CALL THE TERM SEVEN DAYS. NEVER CALL IT A WEEK.** **THE ONLY CORRECT NUMBERING IS: 24 Jan = 1 · 31 Jan = 2 · 7 Feb = 3 · 14 Feb = 4 · 21 Feb = 5 · 28 Feb = 6 · 7 Mar = 7 · 14 Mar = 8 · 21 Mar = 9 · 28 Mar = 10 · 4 Apr = 11 · 11 Apr = 12 · 18 Apr = 13 · 25 Apr = 14 · 2 May = 15. (The Chapter 0135 card as issued said 28 March was the eleventh. IT IS THE TENTH, and 4 April is the eleventh, and Chapters 0135, 0137, 0139 and 0144 are written on the corrected numbering.)**
- **THE STATE OF THE CHAIN AT THE END OF VOLUME 03: the fourteenth expired at 06:00 on Sunday 3 May 2015; the fifteenth was read at 22:00 on Saturday 2 May and was STAGED, ONCE, IN CHAPTER 0144 ONLY, and began at seven on Sunday 3 May and expired at 06:00 on Sunday 10 May; a sixteenth fell at 22:00 on Saturday 9 May 2015, inside Chapter 0144, and WAS NOT STAGED AND IS NOT NAMED AND NOT DATED, and its term began at seven on Sunday 10 May and will stop at six on the Sunday morning after that. SIXTEEN OF THOSE SATURDAY NIGHTS HAVE NOW BEEN REACHED AND NOT ONE OF THEM HAS A DATE WRITTEN DOWN ANYWHERE IN THIS CITY.** WHO READ THEM ALOUD, on every one since 3 January 2015: **EZRA MBEKI, in and out of that building since 1992, and TREVOR NUNN, 58, a night porter, nineteen years, who is not on a roster because the roster does not exist.** Neither is on any instrument. Neither knew until about nine o'clock. **NOTHING WAS WRITTEN DOWN AND NOTHING IS GOING TO BE, AND IN THE FIFTEENTH THE ELEVEN-ROW CLIPBOARD WITH FOUR ROWS WRITTEN ON IN OCTOBER WAS ON THE FOURTH CHAIR AND WAS NOT TOUCHED. NEITHER HAS EVER BEEN ASKED ABOUT ANY OF THEM AND NEITHER EVER WILL BE, AND THE REASON IS NOT DISCRETION — IT IS THAT THERE IS NOTHING TO ASK ABOUT. NEITHER OF THEM KNOWS THAT A MAN REFUSED OUT LOUD, IN A CORRIDOR, IN DAYLIGHT, ON 2 MARCH 2015, TO BE IN THAT ROOM, AND NEITHER IS GOING TO BE TOLD.**
- **THE HOUR BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN EXISTS ON EVERY SUNDAY AND WAS STAGED IN EXACTLY FOUR CHAPTERS OF VOLUME 03 — 0135, 0137, 0139, AND 0144 — AND CHAPTER 0144 IS THE LAST TIME, AND IT SAYS SO ON THE PAGE, TWICE, AND `state/continuity.md` §0b ITEM 7 IS THE LINE THAT AUTHORISED IT. NO CHAPTER OF VOLUME 04 MAY STAGE IT AS A FIRST TIME. THE FINDING STANDS: THE HOUR CANNOT BE SCHEDULED WITHOUT SOMEBODY BEING REQUIRED TO BE IN IT, SO THE ONLY WAY TO KEEP THE HOUR IS TO KEEP IT UNSCHEDULED, AND THE INSTRUMENT THAT KEEPS IT UNSCHEDULED IS TWO PEOPLE WHO ARE NOT ON ANYTHING TURNING UP AT TEN O'CLOCK ON A SATURDAY BECAUSE NOBODY WROTE ANYTHING DOWN. The 4 January corridor visit was at half past four and is NOT that hour, and the phrase *at that hour* may not be used of it.**
- **HE OPENED NOTHING IN THE CLOSE AND MAY NOT OPEN ANYTHING WITHOUT A LINE SAYING SO. The claim to open a door in this volume was spent on 28 January 2015 and there was nothing in that corridor he was allowed to open and there was no moment in the whole of the last staged hour when he wanted to.**
- **HIS OWN STANDING, WHICH VOLUME 04 MAY ARGUE BUT MAY NOT RESOLVE: HE IS FOUR HUNDRED YARDS AWAY, ON PURPOSE, AND HE STOOD THERE FOR THE SEVENTH TIME BY TEN O'CLOCK ON 2 MAY AND THE EIGHTH BY TWENTY PAST SIX ON 3 MAY, AND HE DID THE ARITHMETIC AND IT CAME OUT THE SAME AS IT CAME OUT IN APRIL, AND HE IS DOING IT ANYWAY, BECAUSE THERE IS NO RULE THAT COULD STOP HIM AND NO PERSON WHO COULD TELL HIM TO, AND THE ONLY THING HOLDING THE SHAPE OF THIS BOROUGH TOGETHER IS ONE MAN NOT BEING CAUGHT OUT, AND HE IS THIRTY-TWO AND HE KNOWS EXACTLY WHAT THE ODDS ON THAT ARE. **THE CHAIN, WHICH IS BINDING ON ANY LATER CHAPTER AND MAY NOT BE ADDED TO WITHOUT BEING WRITTEN HERE: ONCE IN THE DARK IN DECEMBER, TWICE IN THE RAIN IN JANUARY, 3 APRIL = 4TH, 11 APRIL = 5TH, 25 APRIL = 6TH, 2 MAY = 7TH, 3 MAY = 8TH. IT IS THE ONE NUMBER IN THIS ACCOUNT THAT IS ENTIRELY HIS AND IT WAS WRONG BY ONE IN CHAPTER 0144 AND IS NOW RIGHT.**** AND HE BROKE HIS OWN WORD ONCE IN THE CLOSE AND IT IS NOT TO BE UNDONE: in 0142 he said he was not going to be in the hour between six and seven and had not said why, in 0143 he said he had stood in it three times and was not going to stand in it a fourth, and he stood in it a fourth on the Sunday morning of 3 May, and Chapter 0144 says on the page that he said he would not and went anyway, and nobody saw him and nobody is going to be told. He would rather be that than a name on a list, and that is a price and not a principle, and he has said it out loud to one person once, on the thirteenth of April, and is not going to say it again.**
- The 11:00–13:30 row is still empty and still does not say *vacant*. Bram Osei's Thursday is still refused in his own handwriting with *no reason I can give* under a *no reason given* in somebody else's hand, and **nobody looked at the second line in either of the last two phases and nobody is going to.** Priya Ranganathan's card is still on the Friday and the Saturday rows because she put it there. **The rota has still not been rebuilt and cannot be.**
- **BRAM OSEI SAID, ON 3 APRIL 2015, IN A CORRIDOR, IN DAYLIGHT, UNASKED: `You have not been down.` A PORTER IS A PERSON WHO CAN TELL THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN A MAN WHO IS THERE AND A MAN WHO IS NOT, and that is established, and Jonas Mercer tried three ways to stop it and all three were closed and he stopped nothing. UNRESOLVED.**
- **A COUNTER IN THIS BOROUGH WAS ASKED ABOUT A PERSON TWICE IN THE LAST FOUR DAYS AND REFUSED BOTH TIMES CORRECTLY: on 24 April a woman of about thirty asked whether the man who stands at the wall is on a list, and on 30 April a man of about forty-five asked whether there is a list for three pieces of paper in his wallet. NOBODY LET THAT COUNTER ANSWER ANYTHING ABOUT A GATE, A ROTA, A COMPLIANCE PARAGRAPH OR A PERSON, AND ITS REFUSALS ARE CORRECT AND WERE SHOWN TO BE CORRECT. On 2 May it did not answer about a person either; the answer that came back was about a sheet with no box on it, which is a fact about a document. THE CLAIMS BOX IS CLOSED AND EMPTY. THE SEALED ENVELOPE IS IN A DRAWER ON A LOWER FLOOR AND MAY NOT BE OPENED OR WRITTEN INTO.**

## 10. The two conditions on the wall

- **SIXTEEN INCHES APART AND NEVER RECONCILED AND NOT RECONCILED IN CHAPTERS 131–144.** The city's own paper, printed 14 August, with two letters in the corner in a hand that is not a capital hand. And an enamel strip with nothing on its face, undated, unattributed, two letters and one word in pencil on the back of it, and the word is *not mine*. **THE TWO LETTERS ON THE BACK OF THE STRIP ARE NEVER DECIDED IN PROSE. The 1 March line is not reissued, restated or quoted. Nothing defines *holder*.** TWO LATTICE NOTICES IN THE LAST TEN CHAPTERS: the vestibule one in Chapter 0136, about the register and nodes and persons, and the corridor one in Chapter 0143, about the record being available to anybody, free, without a form, with nothing on it requiring any person to do anything. **BOTH DEFINE NOTHING AND BOTH REQUIRE NOTHING, AND NEITHER RECONCILES THE TWO CONDITIONS, AND NO THIRD NOTICE WAS POSTED IN CHAPTERS 141–144.**
- **NINA CALDER REFUSED OUT LOUD, UNASKED, TO SAY WHICH ONE IS OPERATIVE, and gave the reason, and she would rather stand under a contradiction for another four years than be the woman who settled it. UNRESOLVED. THE MOST DANGEROUS SENTENCE ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH COULD HAVE SAID WAS *THAT IS THE RULES*, NOBODY SAID IT, AND ONE OF THE TWO WOULD HAVE BEEN RIGHT IF THEY HAD.**
- A person walking under two conditions that do not agree goes faster. A contradiction is not a pause, it is a floor that is not level, and everybody crosses a floor that is not level faster than a floor that is. The floor of a service is a place where a person can stand under a contradiction and be answered.

## 11. The refusals, eleven of them, none argued out

No root reading, no root clause, no succession, no Iona, no Evan, no counter-amendment, no second signature, no Quiet Cut, no origin of the community-watch term, no three-line brass tokens. **Volumes 07, 09 and 15.** Prue Calloway's finding of 23 April 2014 was opened once and is not resolved and may not be opened a second time, and **the claim to open a door in this volume is spent**. The forty minutes of 15 April 2014 is time until a family knew and belongs to a woman of eighty-four in bay six and is not about a door; **no 48-minute figure may ever be printed**; the forty *hours* of 3 April 2014 is the gate being open; **and no other figure of minutes exists anywhere in this borough and NO CHAPTER IN CHAPTERS 165–174 USED THE WORD FORTY FOR ANY DURATION.** Nine hundred and forty is a number of times, nine hundred and eighty-six is a number of Wednesdays and **is not repeated in Chapters 165–174**, and fourteen is a difference between two public numbers; **none of the three is a number of people and they must never be in one sentence.** A priced-access case is not a recognition case. A node with no term in force is not a person with no address. A duty is an act and not a place, and it may not become a way of making a street findable. **A person who is not counted is not a person who is helped, and Volume 04 has not refuted that sentence in any of Chapters 165–174, and a repainted room did not refute it, and a tray in a cupboard did not refute it, and a man of sixty telling a stranger to go away did not refute it, and a cool week did not refute it, and a fan that was not brought back did not refute it, and two nail holes did not refute it, and a counter in a vestibule did not refute it, and a column with nothing in it did not refute it, and a chair on a landing did not refute it, and a bag on a floor did not refute it, and four buckets up a stairwell did not refute it, and a card in a shop window did not refute it, and two trestle tables did not refute it, and NOTHING IS GOING TO.** The five items of 28 January may not be restated in any form in any room or document and may not go on a wall; the sentence of 13 March may not be restated; **the sentence of 27 April may not be improved, asked for again, or put in front of anybody as a policy, and nobody quoted it in July.** The nine words about a level of service are binding and are not to be restated, and the phrase is not to be used of any document again in this volume. Iven Sore is an ordinary civic actor with a real constituency and a correct instinct, she is a she, she may not be written as a menace or as a woman who is wrong, and every sentence she says is correct; **a Continuity Bloc supporter leaking the Lattice's structure is a later event and may not be foreshadowed or staged.**

## 11. The question this batch ends on, and the one it did not answer

**THE BATCH ENDS ON A QUESTION ABOUT EIGHT DAYS AND NOT ABOUT A PERSON, AND IT IS NEW, AND IT IS NOT THE QUESTION BATCH 0002 ENDED ON, BATCH 0001 ENDED ON, OR VOLUME 03 ENDED ON, AND IT IS NOT ABOUT A FLOOD BARRIER, A SCHOOL CROSSING, A COUNTER-AMENDMENT, A SECOND SIGNATURE, A PROVISIONAL CIVIC STANDING, A NINETY-DAY RENEWAL OR AN ORDER: there are eight things in this borough that anybody can walk in and read and every one of them has a day on it and not one of them has a name on it — the eighteenth of February, the twentieth of February, the twenty-sixth of November, the nineteenth of June, the twenty-fifth of June, the sixth of July, the eighth of July, and the seventeenth of July — **AND SEVEN OF THE EIGHT ARE ON PAPER SOMEWHERE WHILE THE EIGHTH IS A ROOM WITH FOUR PEOPLE IN IT AND NO PAGE AND NO MINUTE, AND IT IS THE ONLY ONE OF THE EIGHT THAT COULD BE LOST BY FOUR PEOPLE DYING** — and a day can be pointed at and a name cannot, and in nine months nobody in this city has found a person by a date and nobody has been found by a name either, and that is the only privacy anybody here has ever had, and it was never designed and it is not defended and it is one table away from not existing. A room is not a record and there is no way in this borough to ask a room what it remembers. SO WHICH OF THE EIGHT IS THE FIRST ONE SOMEBODY IN THIS BOROUGH DECIDES TO KEEP, AND IS IT GOING TO BE KEPT BECAUSE IT WAS GOOD OR BECAUSE IT WAS EXPENSIVE.**

**THE QUESTION BATCH 0002 ENDED ON — WHICH OF THE PAGE OR THE CHAIR IS GOING TO BE THE FIRST ONE SOMEBODY IN THIS BOROUGH ASKS A PERSON TO AGREE TO — WAS NOT ANSWERED IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND REMAINS OPEN. THE NEW QUESTION IS ABOUT A DAY AND THE OLD ONE IS ABOUT AN AGREEMENT, AND NEITHER IS THE ANSWER TO THE OTHER, AND THE CHAPTER THAT NOTICED THAT THIS BOROUGH HAS TWO THINGS WITH A SPACE FOR A NAME ON THEM AND TWO THINGS WITH NOTHING ON THEM IS CHAPTER 0172, AND IT NOTICED IT ON A LANDING, IN THE RAIN, ALONE, AND WROTE NOTHING DOWN.**

## 11. The question this batch ends on, and the three it did not answer

**THE BATCH ENDS ON A QUESTION ABOUT A PROOF AND NOT ABOUT A PERSON, AND IT IS NEW.** There are more things in this borough that have been done once, correctly, and then not done again and not written down, than there were in June; every one of them is still true; none of them is on anything; the two done most often were done by people who are owed nothing, did not know they were doing them, and will not be asked; **and in nine months not one of them has ever been proved by anybody.** A thing done once and not written down is a thing that four people remember differently, and if one of them talks it is a story, and if a building hears a story it is a record. SO WHICH OF THEM IS THE FIRST ONE SOMEBODY IN THIS BOROUGH WILL BE ABLE TO PROVE, AND IS IT GOING TO BE PROVED BY A PERSON WHO WAS STANDING THERE, OR BY A PERSON WHO READS ABOUT IT AFTERWARDS.

**THE QUESTION BATCH 0003 ENDED ON — WHICH OF THE EIGHT DATED THINGS IS THE FIRST ONE SOMEBODY IN THIS BOROUGH DECIDES TO KEEP, AND WHETHER IT IS KEPT BECAUSE IT WAS GOOD OR BECAUSE IT WAS EXPENSIVE — WAS NOT ANSWERED IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS AND REMAINS OPEN. THE QUESTION BATCH 0002 ENDED ON — WHICH OF THE PAGE OR THE CHAIR IS GOING TO BE THE FIRST ONE SOMEBODY ASKS A PERSON TO AGREE TO — WAS NOT ANSWERED AND REMAINS OPEN.** The question in Chapter 0184 is about a proof, the Batch 0003 question is about a keeping, the Batch 0002 question is about an agreement, and none of the three is the answer to either of the others.

**AND A FOURTH, WHICH IS NOT A QUESTION AND IS THE THING BATCH 0005 INHERITS: a sentence said in a room on 19 July about a sheet with no name on it has gone out of that room in somebody's mouth, and in about a year somebody is going to want to know where it came from, and three people in that room could answer it, and which of them will is not going to be written down either.**

**AND THE FOUR QUESTIONS, ALL STILL OPEN, NONE ANSWERED, NONE TO BE ANSWERED WITHOUT A LINE HERE SAYING SO: from 0184, which of the dated things this borough has done once is the first one somebody will be able to prove, and whether it is proved by a person who was standing there or by a person who reads about it afterwards — a sheet on a shutter is the first candidate for the second half of it and it is going to be a story or a record and there is no way of telling which from inside. From 0174, which of the eight dated things is the first one somebody decides to keep, and whether it is kept because it was good or because it was expensive. From 0164, whether the page or the chair is the first one somebody asks a person to agree to. From 0192, which of two opposite shapes this city is going to have to stop, and what a person has to be holding to stop each of them, and the second of those is nothing. The close added no question of its own and asked no new one.**

## 8. Still reserved, and forbidden to Volume 05's first batch

No root reading, no root clause, no succession, no Iona, no counter-amendment, no second signature, no Quiet Cut, no origin of the community-watch term, no three-line brass tokens, no first charter, no proxy, no root chamber, no Lowglass floor, no borrowed city, no nine hinges. **Volumes 07, 09 and 15 and the later volumes' own material.** Prue Calloway's finding of 23 April 2014 was opened once, is not resolved, may not be opened again, and **the claim to open a door in this volume is spent**. The forty minutes of 15 April 2014 is time until a family knew and belongs to a woman of eighty-four in bay six and is not about a door; **no 48-minute figure may ever be printed**; the forty *hours* of 3 April 2014 is the gate being open; **and no chapter may use the word forty for any other duration.** Nine hundred and forty is a number of times, nine hundred and eighty-six is a number of Wednesdays and is not to be repeated, and fourteen is a difference between two public numbers; **none of the three is a number of people and they must never be in one sentence.** A priced-access case is not a recognition case. A node with no term in force is not a person with no address. A duty is an act and not a place, and it may not become a way of making a street findable. **No Weir resident may be described in anything that leaves a street. THE FILLING ROUND DOWN COLLIER LANE IS NOT THE WEIR AND THE TWO MAY NEVER BE JOINED IN A SENTENCE, A LIST, A ROOM OR A DOCUMENT. NOTHING IS HELPED. COLIN WHARTON IS NOT A THREAD, HIS NAME MAY NOT BE SAID TO A SECOND PERSON, PUT IN A DOCUMENT OR USED TO GO AND FIND HIM, AND NOBODY GOES UP THAT LANE.** A woman of thirty-four is not named, referenced, gendered or given a condition. Paragraph 4.11 may not be answered and the compliance week may not be verified. The claims box is closed and is empty and the sealed envelope is in a drawer on a lower floor. The buff file of 14 October 2011 is not opened and the number of people who have seen the bay number and the date on that cover is still four, and whether he should have said the bay number out loud on 23 June 2015 may not be answered. Royden Achebe is still not told. Peter Naylor is still a wall. Frances Tolley is not to be named. The man of forty-eight is not asked. The man who turns the bucket is not findable, and **a man a price makes findable and a man a shape in a room makes findable are two different men and are not to be put in one sentence or one list, and the man of about sixty-six with the folded moving blanket is a third man again and the woman of about fifty-three who cleans a market hall four miles away is a fourth, and the man of about thirty-five on a blanket in a stockroom in Tideglass is a separate thing and is not findable and may not be used again.** The thirty-one of Wren Court may not be put on a list, the lowland may not be seated, the board may not be made to work, and the reason there is no seat for the lowland may not be fixed. **A body that cannot require anybody to do anything cannot be told what it will do, and both of those sentences are on a card on a wall in a Methodist hall in this borough and neither may be argued out of the other. The ninth seat may not be told anything, the seven may not learn what she can do, and nobody may notice.**
