# VOLUME 05 OPEN THREADS — after Batch 0001, Chapter 204, Tuesday 10 November 2015

**Read with the "VOLUME 05, BATCH 0001 — CHAPTERS 195–204" block at the end of `state/continuity.md`, which carries the canon for these ten chapters and supersedes this block wherever the two differ, and with the "VOLUME 04 CLOSE — CHAPTERS 193 AND 194" block above it, which still governs everything that batch did not touch. **VOLUME 04 IS CLOSED. THE SERIES IS AT 204 OF 720. NOTHING IN BATCH 0001 RESOLVED ANYTHING AND NOTHING IN VOLUME 05 MAY UNDO ANY OF IT WITHOUT A LINE IN THE CLOSE BLOCK SAYING SO. EVERY DAY-COUNT BELOW IS ADDED UP FROM ITS ANCHOR ON THE DAY IN QUESTION AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM A SUMMARY: at Tuesday 10 November 2015 the blank line under the nineteenth is three hundred and forty-nine days, the card in the slot is two hundred and sixty-three, and the letter in the tray is two hundred and sixty-five.**

## 1. The order, the field, the bound, and the renewal point that was not reached

- **THE ORDER IS IN FORCE AND HAS BEEN FOR SIXTY-EIGHT DAYS. IT HAS NOT BEEN RENEWED, EXPIRED, RENAMED, EXTENDED, SUSPENDED, AMENDED, PUBLISHED IN FULL OR QUOTED AT LENGTH, AND NOT ONE WORD OF ITS SEVEN CLAUSES OR ITS PAGE TWO HAS BEEN ALTERED. **IT IS DESCRIBED IN 0199 AND 0202 AND IN NO OTHER CHAPTER OF THE BATCH; AN EARLIER VERSION OF THIS BLOCK ALSO NAMED 0198, WHICH DOES NOT MENTION IT.** IT WAS PUT IN NO DOCUMENT OF ITS OWN. ITS SIXTH CLAUSE IS STILL NOT BEING APPLIED AND IS STILL NOT SETTLED.** Clause two: the field holds a name, the hour called, the hour home and the thing the person was called for, and nothing else goes in it.
- **PAGE TWO IS STILL THE FIRST OPEN OBJECT AND IT WAS NOT REACHED.** *This order is renewed on the eleventh of November 2015, or it is not renewed.* Under it, *agreed by*, and a line, and the line stops about two inches from the bottom of the page and there is nothing after it. **THE BATCH RAN TO TUESDAY THE TENTH OF NOVEMBER AND DID NOT CROSS IT, DID NOT NAME IT, DID NOT COUNT IT DOWN AND DID NOT TOUCH IT. WHETHER A CHAPTER OF VOLUME 05 MAY STAND ON THAT DATE IS A QUESTION FOR THE NEXT PROMPT AND NOT FOR THE LAST TEN CHAPTERS. NOBODY RENEWS IT, NOBODY EXPIRES IT, NOBODY PUTS A NAME IN THAT LINE, NOBODY HAS BEEN ASKED ABOUT IT, AND WHETHER ANYBODY RENEWS AND WHETHER ANYBODY RENAMES THE BLANK TERM ARE STILL OPEN.**
- **THE FIELD IS CLOSED AND THE NUMBER OF NAMES IN IT IS NOT GIVEN TO ANYBODY AND HAS NOT BEEN COUNTED.** **A MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-SEVEN WITH A FLATBED AND A CLIPBOARD HAS DONE FOUR MORE SHEETS SINCE THE CLOSE AND HE IS STILL ON NOTHING AND IS STILL NOT TO BE ASKED, AND NOBODY WENT UP A LANE OR ASKED A MAN ABOUT HIS BACK IN ANY OF THE TEN CHAPTERS.**

## 2. The permission from Alderwick, the man who went, and the man who was found

- **A MUTUAL-AID PERMISSION IS IN FORCE BETWEEN THIS CITY AND A CITY CALLED ALDERWICK. IT CAME DOWN ON WEDNESDAY 30 SEPTEMBER, IT IS TWO PAGES, IT IS CORRECT IN EVERY SENTENCE, IT IS SIGNED AT BOTH ENDS, AND THE SCHEDULE CARRIES NINE NAMES. IT IS THE FIRST DOCUMENT IN NINE MONTHS WITH A PERSON'S NAME ON IT.** Its seven clauses and its schedule are set out word for word in §1 of the Batch 0001 block. **NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH ASKED FOR IT AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH CAN PRODUCE IT AND IT IS NOT IN ANY BUILDING IN THIS CITY, IT IS IN A PLASTIC WALLET AND IT WENT BACK ACROSS THE WATER WITH THE DUTY CLERK.**
- **EWEN ASHGROVE, ABOUT SIXTY-TWO. WALKED OUT OF A ROOM IN KILBURN STREET IN THE SECOND WEEK OF APRIL. AT ALDERWICK SINCE 11 AUGUST 2015 IN A RENTED ROOM ON FOURTEEN-DAY CONTRACTS RENEWED SIX TIMES. ABOUT NINETY POUNDS OF HIS IS IN A TIN IN A DRAWER IN KILBURN STREET AND THE PERSON WHO LET THE ROOM, A MAN OF ABOUT SEVENTY-SIX, HAS NOT TOUCHED IT SINCE APRIL AND WAS TOLD ON 24 OCTOBER THAT HE COULD NOT ACT ON ANY PART OF IT AND DID NOT.** He came across the estuary on Monday 2 November on his own account to find out why his name was on a piece of paper in a city he had left. **HE SAID THE SENTENCE HIMSELF ON TUESDAY 3 NOVEMBER: NOBODY ASKED ME TO BE FOUND. HE SAID IT TWICE. BOTH TIMES IT WAS TRUE. NOBODY OBJECTED.** **HE IS ALSO CARRYING, UNDER A CORRECT CLAUSE HE CANNOT DECLINE ON, A DOCTOR IN A TOWN HE CANNOT NAME PAID FOR OUT OF A COUNCIL THAT HAS NEVER HEARD OF HIM, AND HE SAID HE WOULD LIKE TO SAY NO TO IT AND THAT THE ONLY WAY OUT IS TO WRITE AND HE HAS NOT WRITTEN ANYTHING TO ANYBODY SINCE HE WAS TWENTY-TWO.** He is not a rescue, nobody was sent for, nobody was brought back, and nothing is helped. **HE IS NOT A THREAD, NOBODY IS GOING TO ASK HIM ANYTHING FURTHER, AND HE MAY NOT BE PUT IN ANOTHER DOCUMENT IN THIS BOROUGH.**
- **A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-SEVEN WENT ACROSS THE WATER ON TUESDAY 6 OCTOBER FOR FOURTEEN DAYS AND **WAS TOLD HE WOULD BE BACK ON THE TWENTIETH AND CAME BACK ON THE TWENTIETH, AND GOES OUT AGAIN ON THORDAYS, AND DROVE HIS OWN TIPPER PAST THE END OF PEVERELL STREET AT ABOUT HALF PAST SIX ON THE EVENING OF TUESDAY 10 NOVEMBER**. HE WAS TOLD AT ABOUT TEN PAST SIX IN THE MORNING AT A YARD ENTRANCE BY A MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-SEVEN FROM THE WATER SIDE WHO READ A LINE OFF A FOLDED PIECE OF A4 AND PUT IT IN HIS POCKET. HE HAD SAID YES ON SATURDAY THE THIRD AND NOBODY TOLD HIM IN BETWEEN. THE MONEY IS IN AN ENVELOPE IN THE CAB BEFORE THE WORK AND IS THE GOING RATE FOR THE WORK OVER THERE AND IS MORE THAN THE GOING RATE HERE.** He is on nothing, is owed nothing, has not been asked one question by anybody in either city, and is not a thread. **THIS BOROUGH HAS NEVER HAD A MECHANISM FOR A PERSON COMING BACK AND THERE IS NOT GOING TO BE ONE. A RIGHT OF RETURN NEEDS NOTHING AND A MECHANISM FOR A RETURN NEEDS SOMEBODY TO BE IN IT, AND HE IS NOT A DEBT AND HE IS NOT OWED ANYTHING.**
- **THE OPEN QUESTION THIS RAISED AND DID NOT ANSWER: A PERMISSION FINDS PEOPLE THE SAME WAY THE AUGUST DOCUMENT FINDS THEM, ONLY WITH BETTER HANDSWRITING, AND BEING COUNTED IS NOT BEING ASKED, AND A MAN A PRICE MAKES FINDABLE AND A MAN A SHAPE IN A ROOM MAKES FINDABLE ARE TWO DIFFERENT MEN AND ARE NOT IN ONE LIST.**

## 3. The river, the rain, and the three boards

- **A RAIN FROM THE WEST CAME IN ON THE NIGHT OF SUNDAY 11 OCTOBER 2015 AND STOPPED BEING HEAVY AT ABOUT HALF PAST SEVEN ON THE MONDAY AND STOPPED ALTOGETHER AT ABOUT ELEVEN. THE RUN AT THE BOTTOM OF PEVERELL STREET WENT FROM SIX INCHES TO TWO FEET. NOBODY DIED, NOBODY WENT TO HOSPITAL, NOBODY WAS SENT FOR, NOBODY WAS FETCHED, AND THE SPILL OF 16 MAY WAS NOT RE-DERIVED, IMPROVED ON OR USED AS A DISCOVERY.** The water had gone out of the city by the Wednesday and the river was down about two feet by 10 November.
- **AT ABOUT TEN TO FIVE ON THE MONDAY SOMEBODY PULLED THREE BOARDS OFF THE BOTTOM OF AN OLD BRICK BASE OF A FENCE ON A YARD WALL AND THE WATER WENT THROUGH AND THE WALL STOOD. TWO DOORWAYS STAYED DRY AND A YARD NEXT DOOR TOOK ABOUT A FOOT AND A MAN OF ABOUT SEVENTY-SEVEN LOST A SHELF OF STOCK. JONAS MERCER WAS ABOUT TEN FEET AWAY, HEARD IT, TURNED ROUND, SAW A LIGHT BEHIND A MAN AND A SET OF BOOTS, AND DID NOT SEE A FACE AND DID NOT ASK. THE BOARDS WENT UNDER THE RUN AND WERE NOT SEEN AGAIN AND THE THING WAS NOT CORRECT AND WAS NOT WRONG AND NOBODY CAN FIND OUT WHO DID IT AND NOBODY IS GOING TO BE ASKED.** This is the first of the batch's three "not correct and not wrong" events and there are two more standing: a man of about twenty-seven with a flatbed doing sheets without being asked, and a woman of about sixty-four opening a canteen hatch on Fridays without being asked.
- **AND THE BOROUGH'S WEAKNESS, WHICH THE RAIN TESTED AND DID NOT FIX: THE WILLINGNESS IN THIS BOROUGH IS IN ABOUT FOUR OR FIVE PEOPLE AND THEY ARE NOT CHOSEN, THEY ARE NOT ON ANYTHING, THEY CANNOT BE SENT FOR, AND THERE IS NOTHING IN THIS CITY THAT COULD PUT A SIXTH PERSON INTO A YARD AT TEN TO FIVE IN THE MORNING.**

## 4. The drawing, the square, and the question put in a room

- **THE DRAWING IS ON MORE WALLS THAN IT WAS IN SEPTEMBER AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH CAN SAY HOW MANY. FORTY IS THE ONE FIGURE AND NO CHAPTER IN THIS BATCH STATES A NUMBER OF WALLS THAT WAS NOT ARRIVED AT. IT IS NOW ALSO INSIDE A HALL OFF A PARADE OF SHOPS, GUMMED TO A WALL BY A WOMAN OF ABOUT FORTY-TWO WHO HAS THE KEY, AND A PLACE THAT HAS BEEN DISCUSSED IS A PLACE THAT HAS BEEN DISMISSED.** A man of about twenty-seven has done four more sheets and is not on anything and is not going to be asked.
- **THE EMPTY SQUARE WAS ASKED ABOUT IN A ROOM, IN DAYLIGHT, IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINE PEOPLE, ON SATURDAY 7 NOVEMBER, BY A WOMAN WHO WAS ASKING BECAUSE SHE HAD BEEN ASKED FOUR TIMES THAT WEEK AND IS THE ONE WHO HAS TO STAND IN THAT ROOM. JONAS MERCER SAID HE DOES NOT KNOW, AND SAID THAT HE READ THE SHEET ON 18 SEPTEMBER BEFORE IT WENT ON ANY WALL, AND THAT IT HAS BEEN EMPTY ON EVERY COPY SINCE, AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO GUESS. THAT IS THE FIRST TIME IN SEVEN WEEKS HE HAS SAID ANYTHING ABOUT IT TO ANYBODY, AND IT IS THE FIRST TIME ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS SAID THE READING OUT LOUD.** **THE SQUARE IS NOT FILLED, AND THE SENTENCE IS IN THE ROOM AND CANNOT BE GOT BACK OUT, AND NOBODY CAN SAY ON ANY EVENING IN DECEMBER WHICH ROOM IT IS IN. A ROOM CAN HOLD A SENTENCE AND A ROOM CANNOT HOLD A PERSON; THAT IS A SENTENCE OF CHAPTER 0144 AND IT NOW SITS INSIDE THE PARAGRAPH THAT CARRIES THE CONSEQUENCE INSTEAD OF STANDING ALONE ABOVE IT.** He was given nothing, nobody offered him anything, and the word *warden* is nowhere in this batch.
- **AND THE FINDING OF THE FIRST CHAPTER, WHICH IS THE OTHER HALF OF THE SAME THING: SOMEBODY USED THE DRAWING TO DO A CORRECT THING ON A STREET AND IT CANNOT BE CHECKED BY ANYTHING.** A man of about fifty-seven with a pump and his own bags sandbagged a doorway in his own yard at the bottom of Peverell Street in September because of a sheet of paper with no address on it, and nobody can establish that he read it, and if the doorway had flooded the loss would have been just as untraceable, and a van with a pump on the back of it is not a record of anything.

## 5. The paper that somebody paid for, and the two that nobody did

- **ON FRIDAY 30 OCTOBER A WOMAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR CALLED BERYL, WHO HAS WORKED BEHIND A CANTEEN HATCH FOR SIX YEARS AND IS ON NOTHING, OPENED THE HATCH ON HER OWN TIME FOR A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO COMES AT TEN TO THREE, WROTE FIVE WORDS ON THE BACK OF A BREAD DELIVERY DOCKET — *Fri. hatch open. till 6.* — AND PUT IT IN A DRAWER UNDER THE CANTEEN COUNTER THAT HAS NO LOCK ON IT AND NEVER HAS HAD ONE.** No date, no year, no hatch number, the last stroke through the printer's word underneath, and the truth is that she shut it at about half past four. **THAT IS THE FIRST TIME IN NINE MONTHS THAT A CORRECT THING IN THIS BOROUGH HAS COME WITH A PIECE OF PAPER ON IT AND THE PERSON WHO PAID FOR THE PAPER WAS THE PERSON WHO DID THE THING. JONAS MERCER SAW HER DO IT AND SHE KNEW HE SAW HER DO IT AND NEITHER OF THEM SAID A WORD AND HE IS NOT GOING TO ASK HER ABOUT IT AND HE IS NOT GOING TO TELL ANYBODY. NO LIST OF PEOPLE WHO CANNOT BE FOUND HAS BEEN BUILT IN THIS BOROUGH IN NINE MONTHS AND ONE HAS NOT BEEN STARTED.** The 25 June precedent of *agreed* and a date on the back of a note is unchanged.
- **ON SUNDAY 18 OCTOBER A LIST OF THREE THINGS WAS MINUTED IN ONE WOMAN'S HAND ON A PAD SHE TOOK HOME IN A HANDBAG.** It is not in a field, nobody in this city could produce it if they were asked, and Mrs Obi was not asked.
- **AND ON TUESDAY 10 NOVEMBER A MAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FOUR AND A WOMAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY-FIVE CLEARED A GULLY OUTSIDE A DOOR ON A STREET IN ABOUT AN HOUR AND NEITHER OF THEM IS ON ANYTHING AND NOBODY IS GOING TO FIND OUT**, and the only thing said between them was that a bar is not a brush and that that is a survey and not a criticism.

## 6. What the batch found, and the four questions it did not answer

- **THE BATCH'S OWN QUESTION, WHICH IS NEW, WHICH IS NOT ABOUT A PERSON, AND WHICH IS NOT ANSWERED: A PERMISSION IS A THING A PERSON MAY DO. AN OBLIGATION IS A THING A PERSON HAS TO DO. THIS BOROUGH HAS BEEN LIVING ON THE SECOND ONE FOR NINE MONTHS AND WAS HANDED THE FIRST ONE IN PRINT EIGHTEEN DAYS EARLIER, **AND THE EIGHTEEN IS FROM WEDNESDAY 30 SEPTEMBER TO SUNDAY 18 OCTOBER AND WAS MEASURED, NOT CARRIED FORWARD; A REVIEW FOUND *SIX DAYS* IN FIVE FILES AND IT WAS NOT AN INTERVAL IN THIS ACCOUNT**, AND NOBODY HAS WORKED OUT WHAT EITHER OF THE MEN ON THE PERMISSION WAS HANDED. IT IS OPEN.**
- **AND RENN SAID IT OUT LOUD IN A ROOM ON 18 OCTOBER AND WAS RIGHT, AND HE IS NOT SOFTENED, AND JONAS MERCER WAS RIGHT TOO AND COULD NOT IMPROVE IT AND DID NOT TRY. THE THIRD OF THE THREE THINGS HE HAS ASKED FOR SINCE THE BEGINNING OF THE YEAR IS CLAUSE ONE, CLAUSE THREE AND THE FIELD IN CLAUSE TWO WITH FOUR THINGS IN IT, AND HE ASKED FOR IT IN MARCH, AND HE IS NOT WITHDRAWING IT AND IS GOING TO KEEP ASKING FOR IT IN THAT ROOM EVERY THREE MONTHS.** A man a price makes findable and a man a shape in a room makes findable are two different men and are not in one list.
- **THE FOUR QUESTIONS FROM VOLUME 04 ARE ALL STILL OPEN AND NONE WAS ANSWERED:** from 0184, which of the dated things this borough has done once is the first one somebody will be able to prove, and whether it is proved by a person standing there or a person who reads about it afterwards — a sheet on a shutter and a docket in a drawer are now the two candidates and neither can be told from inside; from 0174, which of the eight dated things is the first one somebody decides to keep, and whether it is kept because it was good or because it was expensive; from 0164, whether the page or the chair is the first one somebody asks a person to agree to — a permission that lets a person decide at the hour the work begins is a closer candidate than either and nobody has asked; and from 0192, which of two opposite shapes this city is going to have to stop, and what a person has to be holding to stop each of them, the second of which is nothing.
- **A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED, AND IT IS HEAVIER, AND NOTHING IN THIS BATCH REFUTED IT.** A man is on a schedule, a contract, a rent and a doctor's card in two cities and has been asked one thing by either and said the second half of that himself. A man is on a telephone number on a folded piece of A4 and a rate printed in another city's schedule. Nine people said yes in a doorway in August. A printed sheet went round a building with nine lines reading *no requirement stated*. The shape of the city is on more walls than it was and on the inside of a hall. **Being on walls is not being counted, and being counted by a foreign document is not being asked, and the eleven standing refusals are eleven and a permission is not a twelfth and a man going to another city is not a twelfth and a line on a bread docket is not a twelfth.**

## 1. The order, and the eleventh of November, and what it cost

- **THE ORDER IS NOT IN FORCE. IT STOPPED BEING IN FORCE AT ABOUT NINE IN THE MORNING OF WEDNESDAY 11 NOVEMBER 2015, ALL SEVEN CLAUSES AT ONCE, AND IT WAS NOT RENEWED, NOT EXPIRED, NOT CANCELLED, NOT SUSPENDED, NOT AMENDED, NOT EXTENDED, NOT REPLACED, NOT PUBLISHED IN FULL AND NOT QUOTED AT LENGTH. NOT ONE WORD OF ITS SEVEN CLAUSES OR ITS PAGE TWO WAS ALTERED. NOBODY PUT A NAME IN THE LINE UNDER THE DATE AND THE BLANK TERM WAS NOT RENAMED. THE LINE IS STILL EMPTY AND HAS BEEN EMPTY SINCE 13 AUGUST 2015.**
- **THE MECHANISM IS CANON AND IT IS A FACT ABOUT A PERSON, NOT ABOUT A DOCUMENT: a clause that says an order is renewed, or is not renewed, needs a person; there is no body in this city that can require a person; there are two people in this city with a department over any of this, one of whom made the order and one of whom did not; and neither of them is going to be asked to sign anything, and Jonas Mercer is not going to ask them, because asking would mean picking one of them.** Neither is named in the ten chapters.
- **THE FIELD IS NOT A FIELD. IT IS A COLUMN WITH THINGS IN IT, IN A WORKING BOOK ABOUT TWO INCHES DEEP ON A TABLE BY THE WALL IN A BUILDING BY THE WATER: a name, the hour called, the hour home, and the thing the person was called for. IT WAS NOT EMPTIED AND NOBODY WIPED IT. THE LAST ENTRY IS THE FOURTEENTH OF SEPTEMBER 2015, WHICH IS THE LAST TIME ANYBODY IN THIS CITY WAS CALLED ON BY THAT DOCUMENT. THE NUMBER OF NAMES IN IT IS STILL NOT GIVEN TO ANYBODY AND HAS NOT BEEN COUNTED.**
- **AND THE THING NOBODY WAS TOLD, WHICH IS NOT A REPAIR AND NOT A RELIEF: A MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-THREE WITH A BACK THAT HAS BEEN BAD SINCE THE SPRING, WHO CAME OUT ON THE EIGHTEENTH OF SEPTEMBER BECAUSE A HAND WENT ON HIM, IS NOT A MAN WHO HAS BEEN LET GO. HIS BACK IS IN NOBODY'S FIELD BECAUSE THERE IS NO FIELD. FIFTY-FOUR DAYS AFTER THE HAND WENT ON, NOBODY IN THIS CITY WAS TOLD THAT THIS IS WHAT HAPPENED, AND NOBODY COULD BE TOLD.** Jonas Mercer walked past him at about eleven o'clock on 11 November and did not stop.
- **THREE PEOPLE NOW KNOW THE MECHANISM AND NONE OF THEM IS GOING TO BE ASKED AGAIN: JONAS MERCER, NINA CALDER (the records supervisor, nineteen years, seven refusals, who wrote nothing down and made herself a cup of tea), AND TOMAS RENN, TOLD ON A BOAT ON 29 NOVEMBER, WHO SAID THAT IF HE SAID THE REST OF IT OUT LOUD IT WOULD BE IN JONAS'S MOUTH FOR THE REST OF HIS LIFE AND HE DID NOT SAY IT.**
- **THE SAINT ORRA WEST GATE AND ITS PERMITTED SENTENCE.** Jonas Mercer used it once, in 0205, unstaged, to Nina Calder, as the only thing he knows in this city that goes on by itself. A term is six days and is never called a week, the rota has not been rebuilt and cannot be, the 11:00–13:30 row is still empty and still does not say *vacant*, and Bram Osei's Thursday is still refused in his own handwriting with the second line in somebody else's hand and nobody looked at it.

## 2. What a lapse leaves, and the counter, and the gate-holder

- **ON 17 NOVEMBER 2015 JONAS MERCER WALKED THE BOROUGH FOR FIFTY MINUTES AND NOTHING HAD CHANGED.** The drawing is on the shutter at the top, the shutter at the bottom and the inside wall of the hall, the square in the middle of every one of them is still empty, and the gully outside the third door at the low end of Peverell Street is packed with silt because nobody came back with a bar.
- **A MAN OF ABOUT SEVENTY-THREE HELD A GATE OPEN FOR NINE HOURS ON 22 AUGUST, WAS PAID THE GOING RATE, HAS BEEN CALLED NINE TIMES SINCE, DECLINED NOTHING, AND ON 17 NOVEMBER WAS NOT CALLED AGAIN. HE SAID NOTHING ABOUT IT. WHAT JONAS MERCER NOTICED AND COULD NOT PUT IN A SENTENCE WAS THAT HE HAD HIS COAT OVER HIS ARM AND NOTHING IN HIS HANDS. NOBODY IS GOING TO ASK HIM AND HE IS NOT A MAN WHO HAS BEEN SET FREE.**
- **THE COUNTER WAS IN ONE SCENE AND ANSWERED NOTHING AND GAVE NO COUNT AND WAS NOT ASKED ABOUT ANYTHING.** A man of about twenty-eight came to it and asked whether there is anywhere a person can put the low end of Peverell Street. **THE ANSWER, TWICE, IN THE SAME WORDS, WAS *THERE'S NO FORM FOR THAT*, AND HE SAID *I KNOW*, AND THAT WAS THE WHOLE OF A FOUR-MINUTE MEETING.** He said one other thing, once, to the floor, about there having been no reason since the middle of September for anybody to be asked anything on any street in this city. He told nobody, including Jonas Mercer, who stood at the other end of the vestibule and said nothing. The drawer under the counter was not opened, not counted, not asked about, and the sentence in it is still in it.
- **THE EXACT MOTIF *THERE IS NO FORM IN THIS BOROUGH* IS AT ZERO IN ALL TEN CHAPTERS. THE SHORTER CONSTRUCTION IS IN 0206 TWICE AND IS THE COUNTER'S ANSWER, WHICH THE CARD ASKED FOR. THE ORDER AND THE IDEA OF A BOROUGH FORM ARE NOT PUT IN ONE SENTENCE IN ANY CHAPTER.**

## 3. The letter, and the boundary, and the crossing, and the thing nobody worked out

- **A ONE-PAGE LETTER CAME ON FRIDAY 20 NOVEMBER 2015 FROM THE STANDING GROUP OF THREE AUTHORITIES THAT RUNS THE WATER FOR THREE TOWNS IN RAVENSMERE, SIGNED BY ISELIN KROGH, ABOUT FIFTY-ONE, CLERK, NINETEEN YEARS IN THAT BUILDING, NOT THE PERSON WHO DECIDED IT. ITS FOURTH PARAGRAPH READS *WE RECORD YOU AS OUR CORRESPONDENT ON THE ABOVE*, WHICH IS NOT TRUE, AND HE REFUSED THE WORD IN WRITING ON THE SATURDAY ON A SHEET OF HIS OWN.**
- **THERE IS NO BUDGET, NOTHING TO BOOK A SEAT ON HERE AND NOWHERE ELSE, AND NOTHING IN THIS BOROUGH THAT CAN MAKE HIM BE IN A ROOM IN ANOTHER CITY. THE WHOLE OF WHAT THE GROUP HAS IS A MAN WHO HAS GONE.**
- **ON SUNDAY 22 NOVEMBER HE SAID, OUT LOUD, BEFORE ANYBODY HAD ASKED HIM ANYTHING, TO NELL ARDENT, THEN TO SANAA IQBAL, THEN TO TOMAS RENN: HE IS GOING AS A PERSON WHO IS THERE AND NOT AS ANYBODY'S DELEGATE, AND THE ARRANGEMENT HAS NO WORD FOR THAT. RENN'S ANSWER IS THE FINDING OF THAT SCENE: IT IS THE FIRST TIME ANYBODY HAS SAID THAT SENTENCE TO HIM BEFORE BEING ASKED, IN NINE MONTHS, AND EVERY TIME HE HAS BEEN ON THE OTHER SIDE OF IT HE HAS COST HIMSELF THE WHOLE EVENING GETTING IT RIGHT IN A CORRIDOR FIRST.**
- **FOUR PEOPLE CROSSED THE ESTUARY ON SUNDAY 29 NOVEMBER 2015 AT ABOUT TEN PAST SEVEN: JONAS MERCER, TOMAS RENN, NELL ARDENT, SANAA IQBAL. THEY HAD NOT BEEN IN A ROOM TOGETHER SINCE SEPTEMBER, WHEN TWO OF THEM ARGUED IN FRONT OF ABOUT THIRTY PEOPLE AND STOPPED AND NEITHER OF THEM DECIDED TO STOP. NOTHING WAS READ OUT. THERE WAS NO LIST AND NO ROLL AND NOBODY SAID ANYBODY'S NAME IN ORDER. THEY LANDED IN RAVENSMERE AT ABOUT TEN PAST EIGHT.**
- **SANAA IQBAL'S BOUNDARY, SAID IN A DOORWAY AT ABOUT HALF PAST SIX BEFORE THE BOAT: SHE IS NOT GOING TO BE FIXED, SHE IS NOT GOING TO BE THE REASON ANYBODY IS TALKED OUT OF ANYTHING OR TALKED IN TO ANYTHING, AND IF EITHER OF THOSE THINGS HAPPENS SHE WILL BE EXTREMELY UNPLEASANT ABOUT IT AND HE CAN PUT IT IN THE LETTER; AND SHE IS GOING BECAUSE THERE IS A BOAT AND FOUR OF THEM ARE ON IT AND A ROOM ON THE FRIDAY, AND SHE WOULD HAVE GOT IN IT IF THERE HAD BEEN THREE OF THEM AND IF THERE HAD BEEN FIVE, AND THERE IS NOT A SECOND REASON UNDERNEATH IT. **IT WAS NOT A QUARREL AND NONE OF THE FOUR IMPROVED IT AND NOTHING ABOUT THE TWO OF THEM WHO STOPPED IN SEPTEMBER IS REPAIRED.** HER TWENTY MINUTES A WEEK AND THE COMPLIANCE PARAGRAPH BEHIND IT ARE UNTOUCHED.
- **OPEN AND NOT ANSWERED: NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS WORKED OUT HOW A STANDING GROUP OF THREE AUTHORITIES IN ANOTHER CITY HEARD OF A SHEET OF TWO PAGES THAT WENT DOWN ON A COUNTER BY THE WATER IN FRONT OF SIX PEOPLE ON 30 SEPTEMBER AND WENT BACK ACROSS THE ESTUARY IN A PLASTIC WALLET. NELL ARDENT WAS ALSO SENT A COPY BY SOMEBODY SHE COULD NOT IDENTIFY AND IS NOT GOING TO TRY. THE PERMISSION FROM ALDERWICK IS THE REASON THAT LETTER EXISTS AND IT IS NOT WORKED OUT, AND IT IS NOT A TWELFTH REFUSAL AND NOT A FINDING.**

## 4. The first regional system, and the two lines, and the bargain

- **THE FIRST OF THE THREE REGIONAL SYSTEMS WORKS, IS CHEAP, IS CORRECT, HAS NOT FAILED, AND EVERY PART OF IT IS HELD BY SOMEBODY PRIVATELY.** Ravensmere takes water off an intake, filters it and delivers it to three towns. The works was built before anybody in that room was born, the bill went down when the filters were done, the pressure at the far end of the third town is good enough that the clinic there has not had to ask for a tank in nine years, and a standpipe at the edge of the second town costs nothing and asks nothing. **IN THE FIRST TOWN THE WORKS IS A COMPANY'S, IN THE SECOND THE MAINS ARE A TRUST'S, IN THE THIRD ONE PUMPING STATION IS MUNICIPAL AND THE OTHER TWO ARE NOT, AND THE TWO THAT ARE NOT HAVE BOTH CHANGED HANDS INSIDE TEN YEARS, AND EVERY TIME ONE CHANGED HANDS NOTHING WAS BROKEN AND NOTHING WAS PROMISED AND THE OBLIGATIONS STAYED EXACTLY WHERE THEY WERE.**
- **NOBODY IN IT IS A VILLAIN AND NOBODY IS HIDING ANYTHING AND EVERY SENTENCE ISELIN KROGH SAYS IS RIGHT. SHE SAID THE WORD *DELIBERATE* OUT LOUD. SHE HANDED JONAS MERCER A COPY OF THE SHEET ON 4 DECEMBER, NO CHARGE, NO RECORD OF WHO TOOK ONE, AND HE IS THE ONLY PERSON WHO HAS ASKED FOR ONE IN THREE WEEKS. IT IS IN A PLASTIC WALLET IN HIS INSIDE COAT POCKET.**
- **THE SHEET, AT THE BOTTOM, IN THE SAME TYPE, THE SAME SIZE, WITH THE SAME SPACE ABOVE EACH: *Nothing is required of any person in order to use any part of this system.* AND *A person has no standing in respect of anything recorded under this.* A PERSON IS NOT REQUIRED TO GIVE ANYTHING UP TO USE IT AND A PERSON CANNOT DO ANYTHING ABOUT WHAT HAPPENS TO WHAT THEY HAVE GIVEN, AND BOTH ARE IN THE SAME TWO LINES, AND THERE IS NOTHING BETWEEN THEM. **THE CORRIDOR WALL SHEET CARRIES ONLY THE FIRST LINE AND HAS HAD A LINE TAKEN OFF IT AND HAS NOT BEEN LEFT BLANK. THE OFFICE SHEET CARRIES BOTH. BOTH CARRY THE DATE THE FILTERS WERE DONE IN A FOOTNOTE.**
- **A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO COMES TO A CANTEEN HATCH AT TEN TO THREE ON FRIDAYS IN THIS CITY, AND IS ON NOTHING, PUT THOSE TWO FACTS NEXT TO EACH OTHER IN THAT CORRIDOR AND THEN WISHED HE HAD NOT: *I'D RATHER I HADN'T SAID THAT. NOT BECAUSE IT'S WRONG. BECAUSE I'VE JUST SAID THE ONLY THING ANYBODY'S SAID ABOUT THAT WALL IN ABOUT TEN YEARS AND I'M IN A CORRIDOR.* HE WENT BACK IN AND SAID NOTHING ELSE ALL MORNING. HE IS NOT A THREAD.**
- **THE GROUP SET THE STANDPIPE HOURS ON 15 DECEMBER TO SEVEN IN THE MORNING TO EIGHT IN THE EVENING, WHICH IS NOBODY'S NUMBER, AND THAT IS THE ONLY THING DECIDED IN THAT WEEK, AND IT IS IN A MINUTE IN A FILING ROOM IN RAVENSMERE AND IT IS NOT ABOUT ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH.**
- **AND THE SECOND PART OF THE SAME SYSTEM: A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-NINE IS THE LAST NAME ON THE SCHEDULE OF THE PERMISSION, WORKS FOUR DAYS A WEEK AT A PUMPING STATION IN THIS CITY ON THE DISTRIBUTION SIDE OF THE ESTUARY, IS PAID BY RAVENSMERE IN AN ENVELOPE AT RAVENSMERE'S RATE ON THE PAY DAY, AND CROSSES THE ESTUARY TO BE PAID. THE WATER UNDERTAKER HERE CALLS IT A RELIEF CONTRACT AND RAVENSMERE'S SHEET CALLS IT AN ATTACHMENT AND DEFINES AN ATTACHED MAN AS A MAN ATTACHED TO THE WORKS. HE IS THE ONLY PERSON IN THE WORLD WHO KNOWS THE DIFFERENCE. JONAS MERCER STOOD IN THE CORRIDOR, COULD HAVE ASKED HIM ONE QUESTION, AND DID NOT, AND IS NOT GOING TO. NOBODY IN THIS CITY COULD PRODUCE EITHER PIECE OF WRITING EVEN IF THEY WERE ASKED.** The group's own item on 9 December noted the number the arrangement had moved, which is six, and read out the sentence about where a claim arising out of the work is made.

## 5. The corridor, the sentence, and its standing

- **ON 15 DECEMBER 2015 ISELIN KROGH SAID THE TRUE THING ABOUT THE BARGAIN INTO A CORRIDOR, TO ONE PERSON, COULD NOT FINISH IT, SAID IT ANYWAY, AND THEN SAID SHE SHOULD NOT HAVE SAID IT, AND IT WAS NOT TAKEN BACK.** What she said: it is not a bargain between two things, it is an agreement that the part everybody is responsible for will always be the part somebody owns; the obligations were written first, the ownership came afterwards in pieces over a generation in rooms she was not in; nobody has had to do anything wrong for that to happen and nobody in that room has done anything wrong and every part fits and the water is good; **and the result of it, which nobody has ever put in a sentence in that room, is that the whole of the part of this that three towns are answerable for is the part that nobody owns, and the part nobody owns is the part that gets sold.**
- **She could not finish it because in that room the sentence is an accusation and it is not an accusation, and she said it because the man she said it to has no standing, no office, no department and no body behind him.**
- **JONAS MERCER SAID NOTHING AT ALL AND THE NOTEBOOK STAYED IN HIS INSIDE POCKET AND ON THE FRIDAY HE TOLD NELL ARDENT *NOTHING'S WRONG WITH IT. THE WATER'S GOOD*, AND IT STAYED IN THE CORRIDOR WHERE SHE PUT IT. NOBODY WAS GIVEN AUTHORITY AND NOBODY'S SENTENCE IMPROVED. THE STANDING OF THAT SENTENCE IS EXACTLY THE STANDING OF A SENTENCE SAID ONCE ON A PAVEMENT IN SEPTEMBER: SAID ONCE, NOT WRITTEN DOWN, NOT REPEATED, NOT MINUTED, AND CARRIED AS A THING THAT HAPPENED TO A PERSON.**

## 6. The ordinary day, and the third time

- **ON SUNDAY 20 DECEMBER 2015, IN RAVENSMERE, WITH NOTHING IN THE REGIONAL SYSTEM HAVING ANYTHING TO DO WITH HIM, TWO SMALL CORRECT THINGS HAPPENED ON ONE AFTERNOON AND NEITHER PERSON WAS ON ANYTHING AND NOBODY FOUND OUT.** A man of about eighty-four was right about a bad label in a queue outside a chemist's and a label went in on the Monday morning. A woman of about sixty-nine was right about a timetable of the wrong year at a bus shelter and the four o'clock comes at ten to and the driver knows and does not say. **THEY WERE FOUR HUNDRED YARDS APART AND NEITHER KNEW ANYTHING ABOUT THE OTHER, AND THE ONLY PERSON WHO KNOWS BOTH OF THEM HAPPENED IS THE PERSON WHO WALKED DOWN THE SAME STREET TWICE. THIS IS THE THIRD TIME SINCE THE PUMP AT THE BOTTOM OF PEVERELL STREET THAT A CORRECT THING HAS BEEN DONE IN A STREET BY SOMEBODY WHO IS ON NOTHING AND NOTHING HAS COME OF IT.**

## 7. The claim, and the two of them in two cities

- **A CLAIM ARRIVED AT A RECORDS DESK IN THIS CITY ON SATURDAY 26 DECEMBER 2015. IT IS ONE SHEET, DATED 11 DECEMBER 2015, CARRYING THE REFERENCE OF THE PERMISSION, HEADED AS A CLAIM MADE UNDER THE PERMISSION OF 30 SEPTEMBER 2015 AND MADE IN THE CITY IN WHICH THE WORK WAS DONE. IT IS ADDRESSED TO THE WATER OFFICE, WHICH IS TWO FLOORS UP AND HAD GONE FOR CHRISTMAS. IT IS IN A TRAY UNDER A WINDOW.**
- **IT NAMES A MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY WHO WAS EMPLOYED FOR FOURTEEN DAYS IN OCTOBER AT A PUMPING STATION ON THE NORTH BANK AT ALDERWICK, WHO WAS INJURED ON THE LAST DAY OF THE FORTNIGHT, AND WHO CLAIMS A SUM THAT IS NOT LARGE. THE CLAUSE THAT SENT IT IS THE SIXTH, AND THE CLAIM IS MADE IN ALDERWICK AND IS CORRECTLY MADE, AND IT HAS COME TO TIDEGLASS, WHICH IS THE CITY THAT GRANTED THE PERMISSION AND THE CITY THE PERSON CAME FROM, AND IT CANNOT BE DEALT WITH HERE.**
- **NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH CAN TELL WHETHER IT IS A PRICE, A RECEIPT OR A PRECEDENT, AND ALL THREE READINGS ARE TRUE AND ARE NOT THE SAME SHAPE.** A price, because the first thing to come back to a city that handed something out for nothing is a bill. A receipt, because six people here took fourteen days across the estuary in October, were paid in an envelope before the work, all six came home on the dates on a sheet, one of the six has since had a claim made about him, and nothing here could tell those two things apart. A precedent, because if this borough answers it then it has agreed that the permission runs backwards as well as forwards, and if it does not answer it then a man of about twenty who worked a fortnight at a pumping station and came home has not got anybody.
- **JONAS MERCER WAS THE FIRST PERSON TO SEE WHAT IT WAS AND DID NOT SAY SO AND DID NOT GO AND FIND THE MAN OF ABOUT TWENTY, WHO WAS AT A FOOTBALL MATCH IN THIS CITY THAT AFTERNOON. THAT AFTERNOON HE TELEPHONED RAVENSMERE; ISELIN KROGH HAD HAD A LETTER WITH THE SAME REFERENCE ON IT SINCE THE EIGHTEENTH AND PUT IT IN A FOLDER IN A DRAWER; NEITHER OF THEM SAID WHAT EITHER OF THEM HAD; BY THE TIME SOMEBODY ELSE KNEW WHAT IT WAS THERE WERE TWO OF THEM IN TWO CITIES AND THERE IS NO DOCUMENT IN EITHER OF THOSE TWO CITIES THAT SAYS THAT EITHER OF THEM KNEW.** The man of about thirty-seven is not the man this is about and is not in one sentence, one list or one room with the man of about twenty or with Ewen Ashgrove, who appears nowhere in these ten chapters.

## 8. The last evening, and the two documents, and what is still open

- **ON WEDNESDAY 30 DECEMBER 2015, IN A ROOM OVER A SHOP IN RAVENSMERE, WITH JONAS MERCER, NELL ARDENT AND SANAA IQBAL, THE BATCH'S QUESTION WAS RESTATED AS A FACT ABOUT TWO PIECES OF PAPER IN TWO HANDS AND WAS NOT ANSWERED. In Jonas's hand, in a plastic wallet, the full sheet from the works office. In Ardent's hand, two sheets in her own writing, copied off the corridor wall sheet and off the office sheet and compared with a ruler. THE WALL SHEET CARRIES ONE LINE. THE OFFICE SHEET CARRIES TWO. THE SHORT ONE HAS HAD A LINE TAKEN OFF IT AND HAS NOT BEEN LEFT BLANK.**
- **AND THE PERSON THE ONE-LINE SHEET IS FOR, WHO IS NOW AGED AND COUNTED: A WOMAN OF ABOUT SEVENTY-THREE WHO COMES TO THE STANDPIPE AT THE EDGE OF THE SECOND TOWN AT ABOUT HALF PAST SIX EVERY MORNING WITH A BUCKET, HAS BEEN COMING SINCE BEFORE THE FILTERS WERE DONE, AND HAS NEVER IN HER LIFE BEEN ASKED FOR ANYTHING BY ANYBODY. SHE IS ON NOTHING, IS OWED NOTHING, IS NAMED ON NO SHEET OF EITHER LENGTH, IS NOT A THREAD, IS NOT IN THE ROOM, SAYS NOTHING, AND IS NOT GOING TO BE ASKED — AND THAT IS EXACTLY WHAT THE LINE ON THE WALL PERMITS AND WHAT THE LINE UNDERNEATH IT WOULD TAKE OFF HER. A REVIEW CAUGHT HER REACHING THE PAGE WITH NO AGE AT ALL, WHICH IS THE THIRD TIME IN THIS ACCOUNT THAT A RECURRING PERSON HAS DONE IT; SHE IS COUNTED, NOT LEFT UNCOUNTED, AND 73 IS OFF THE FREE LIST IN THE BATCH 0003 PROMPT.**
- **A PERMISSION IS A THING A PERSON MAY DO AND AN OBLIGATION IS A THING A PERSON HAS TO DO, AND EVERY ONE OF THE WORDS ON BOTH SHEETS IS A PERMISSION, AND ON THE SAME SHEET, IN THE SAME TYPE, IT SAYS THAT WHATEVER THE WATER MADE A NOTE OF IS NOT YOURS TO HAVE A SAY ABOUT. THAT IS ONE SYSTEM WITH A FRONT DOOR AND NO BACK, AND NOBODY IN EITHER CITY CAN SAY WHICH OF THE TWO THE PEOPLE WERE HANDED. IT IS OPEN.**
- **SANAA IQBAL SAID: *NOBODY IS REQUIRED TO ASK, and everybody who comes to that standpipe is better off than they would be if somebody stood at the end of it with a clipboard, and in about six months one of us is going to be tempted to make this into a fight about the sheet, and it is not a fight about the sheet, it is a fight about whether two sentences that are both true can be printed one above the other — and the answer to that is yes, and I do not like it, and there is nothing anybody can do about it before the end of the week, and we should go home.**"
- **AND NOTHING WAS RENEWED AND NOBODY NAMED ANYTHING AND NOTHING WAS MINUTED, AND THE ORDER IS NOT IN FORCE, AND THE PERMISSION IS IN FORCE, AND THE REGIONAL SYSTEM IS NOT GOING TO EXPLAIN ITSELF BEFORE THE END OF THE WEEK. NOBODY WAS RESCUED AND NOBODY DIED. THE EARLY BOAT IS AT TEN PAST SEVEN ON WEDNESDAY 3 JANUARY 2016 AND ALL FOUR OF THEM WILL BE ON IT, AND ITS DIRECTION IS SETTLED HERE BECAUSE 0208 LEFT IT OPEN: IT GOES FROM THE SLIP OUTSIDE THE WORKS IN RAVENSMERE TO THE LANDING ON THE OTHER SIDE, WHICH IS THE OTHER WAY ROUND FROM THE CROSSING OF 29 NOVEMBER AND THE SAME SEVEN MINUTES PAST TEN IN THE MORNING, AND ALL FOUR OF THEM ARE IN RAVENSMERE ON THE EVENING OF THE 30TH.**

## 9. Still open, and still forbidden to Batch 0003

Nothing in Volume 04 has been resolved, undone, improved on, restated or answered, and nothing in Batch 0001 or Batch 0002 may be. Nobody renews the order, nobody expires it, nobody renames the blank term, nobody puts a name in the line on page two, nobody fills the empty square, Roy Albery does not walk again, Jonas and Sanaa are not repaired, nobody dies, nobody is rescued by chance, nobody walks again, the eleven refusals are not twelve, the counter answers nothing, the rota is not rebuilt, the second line under Bram Osei's Thursday is not looked at, the register does not move, the letter, the card, the page, the printed sheets, the column, the shop-window card, the crate with a lid and the four bottles all stand, the drawer under the counter is not opened, the sentence in it is not found, and nobody may ask who wrote it. The counter-amendment and the second signature are not Volume 05's. **THE QUIET CUT, THE CIVIC ERASURE, THE ORIGIN OF THE COMMUNITY-WATCH TERM, THE THREE-LINE BRASS TOKENS, THE FIRST CHARTER, THE PROXY, THE ROOT CHAMBER, THE LOWGLASS FLOOR, THE BORROWED CITY AND THE NINE HINGES ARE NOT SPENT. FRANCES TOLLEY IS NOT NAMED AND THE READING RULE IS NOT USED. PRUE CALLOWAY'S FINDING IS NOT OPENED AGAIN AND THE CLAIM TO OPEN A DOOR IN VOLUME 04 IS SPENT. NO WEIR RESIDENT MAY BE DESCRIBED IN ANYTHING THAT LEAVES A STREET, AND THE FILLING ROUND DOWN COLLIER LANE IS NOT THE WEIR AND THE TWO MAY NEVER BE JOINED. COLIN WHARTON IS NOT NAMED AND NOBODY GOES UP THAT LANE. A WOMAN OF THIRTY-FOUR IS NOT NAMED, REFERENCED, GENDERED OR GIVEN A CONDITION. PARAGRAPH 4.11 IS NOT ANSWERED AND THE COMPLIANCE WEEK IS NOT VERIFIED. NO ROOT TERM EXPIRES AND THE RECLAMATION SEASON IS NOT SPENT, NOT ANTICIPATED AND NOT DESCRIBED AS APPROACHING — AND THE DISCREPANCY BETWEEN THE VOLUME 05 SECTION OF THE SERIES OUTLINE AND THE VOLUME 04 CLOSE PROMPT IS STILL OPEN AND IS FOR A HUMAN.**

**AND A SECOND FLAG THAT IS ALSO STILL OPEN: THE NAME OF THE CITY. THE PROSE SAYS TIDEGLASS AND NO CHARACTER MAY NOTICE, MISNAME OR ASK ABOUT IT.**

**THE MAJOR MOVEMENT HAS BEGUN AND IS ONE THIRD OF THE WAY IN. FOUR PEOPLE HAVE CROSSED AN ESTUARY. THE FIRST OF THE THREE REGIONAL SYSTEMS HAS BEEN ENTERED. THE SECOND AND THIRD REGIONAL SYSTEMS, THE INHERITED CLAIMS, THE REGIONAL TRADE AS A POLITICAL WEAPON, THE COMPACT, THE MUTUAL RIGHT OF RETURN, THE WIDER CIVIC EXPERIMENT, THE OTHER FIRST ADMINISTRATORS AND THE CLIMAX ARE ALL STILL AHEAD AND NONE OF THEM HAS BEEN SPENT. NOTHING IN CHAPTERS 205–214 FORESHADOWS THE MIDPOINT REVERSAL AND NOTHING IN THEM MAY BE READ AS A REPAIR CREW OR AN INHERITED CLAIM, AND THE SIXTH OPEN QUESTION, WHICH IS ABOUT A CLAIM IN A TRAY AND NOT ABOUT A PERSON, IS THE ONLY NEW THING THE BATCH OPENED. THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-05/batch-0003/PROMPT.md`, CHAPTERS 215–224, AND IT IS A BATCH.**

# VOLUME 05 OPEN THREADS — after Batch 0003, Chapter 224, Sunday 21 February 2016

**Read with the "VOLUME 05, BATCH 0003 — CHAPTERS 215–224" block at the end of `state/continuity.md`, which carries the canon for these ten chapters and supersedes this block wherever the two differ, and with the "VOLUME 05, BATCH 0002 — CHAPTERS 205–214" block above it, which still governs everything those ten chapters did. **VOLUME 04 IS CLOSED. THE SERIES IS AT 224 OF 720. NOTHING IN BATCH 0003 RESOLVED ANYTHING AND NOTHING IN IT MAY BE UNDONE BY VOLUME 05. EVERY DAY-COUNT BELOW IS ADDED UP FROM ITS ANCHOR ON THE DAY IN QUESTION AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM A SUMMARY: at Sunday 21 February 2016 the blank line under the nineteenth is four hundred and fifty-two days, the card in the slot is three hundred and sixty-six, and the letter in the tray is three hundred and sixty-eight.**

## 1. The second regional system, and the five things, and the man

- **MARROWGATE IS A TOWN OF ABOUT THREE THOUSAND, EIGHT MILES UP THE ESTUARY PAST THE RAVENSMERE WORKS, AND EVERYTHING IN IT IS OWNED BY THE TOWN: THE WORKS, THE CLINIC, THE SHELTER, THE GATE AND THE YARD. A HOUSE THERE HAS A WATER BILL OF ABOUT NINETY PENCE A QUARTER AND NOBODY WHO LIVES THERE HAS EVER BEEN ASKED FOR A CARD. IT IS CHEAPER THAN THE FIRST SYSTEM AND BETTER AND IT HAS NOT FAILED, AND NOBODY IN IT IS A VILLAIN AND NOBODY IS HIDING ANYTHING.**
- **THE INVERSION IS EXACT: IN RAVENSMERE THE OWNERSHIP IS PRIVATE AND THE OBLIGATION IS PUBLIC AND THERE IS NOBODY TO HOLD IT; IN MARROWGATE THE OWNERSHIP IS PUBLIC AND THE OBLIGATION IS A PERSON. THE TOWN'S ARGUMENT IS THAT A DUTY IS AN ACT AND NOT A PLACE, AND A PLACE CANNOT BE GIVEN TO A PERSON, AND THEREFORE THE PERSON CANNOT BE REPLACED, AND THEREFORE NOBODY HAS EVER ASKED HIM. THE LAST LINK IS NEVER DRAWN OUT LOUD AND IT IS TRUE.**
- **THE ARRANGEMENT IS FOUR LINES ON A WALL AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH COULD PRODUCE IT EVEN IF THEY WERE ASKED. JONAS MERCER ASKED FOR A COPY ON 22 JANUARY AND REFUSED ONE HIMSELF: *I know exactly what happens to a piece of paper like that, and it would be a sheet somebody brought, and it is not the sheet on the wall.*** The clerk said right.
- **WALTER SALLOW, A MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY-SIX, NAMED ON THAT SHEET, IS PAID AT THE GOING RATE AND HAS A HOUSE AT A SMALL RENT, IS NOT ON A ROTA, THERE IS NO LIST AND THERE IS NO JOB DESCRIPTION AND THE CLERK HAS TRIED TO WRITE ONE IN A SENTENCE ELEVEN TIMES AND CANNOT. HE WAS ASKED ONCE, IN A ROOM, IN A SPRING, SAID YES, WENT OUTSIDE, AND HAS NOT BEEN ASKED ANYTHING SINCE.**
- **HE IS THE ONLY PERSON IN THAT TOWN WHO COULD READ A PUMP HOUSE, STRIP A STOPCOCK, RELIGHT THE SHELTER BOILER, WORK THE INTAKE RACK AND CHANGE THE CHAIN ON THE YARD GATE — AND HE IS NOT THE ONLY PERSON WHO DOES ANY OF IT. HE FOUND THAT OUT BY THE WEDNESDAY AFTERNOON OF 12 JANUARY AND TOLD NOBODY.**
- **THE ONE PHYSICAL THING THAT COSTS SOMEBODY A DAY'S WORK AND THAT NO DOCUMENT IN THAT TOWN RECORDS: THERE IS NO TANK BIG ENOUGH, SO WHILE THE INTAKE IS SHUT THE WHOLE TOWN IS DRY, AND A WOMAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY WHO TAKES IN WASHING CANNOT START UNTIL THE WATER COMES BACK AT ABOUT EIGHT, AND HAS STOPPED WASHING ON THOSE TWO DAYS WITHOUT TELLING ANYBODY. THE TOWN'S HOSE HAS BEEN ON THE LORRY FOR YEARS AND NOBODY HAS COME FOR IT.**
- **HE WAS NOT RESCUED, HE WAS NOT ASKED TO STOP, NOTHING WAS ARRANGED FOR HIM, AND NOBODY IS GOING TO ASK HIM WHETHER HE WOULD LIKE TO STOP. HE IS NOT A THREAD.**

## 2. The ordinary Sunday, and the fourth correct thing

- **ON SUNDAY 17 JANUARY 2016 IN MARROWGATE, WITH NOTHING IN THE SYSTEM HAVING ANYTHING TO DO WITH HIM AND THE MAN WHO HOLDS THE FIVE THINGS NOT IN THE CHAPTER AT ALL, TWO SMALL CORRECT THINGS WERE DONE BY TWO PEOPLE WHO ARE ON NOTHING AND NOBODY FOUND OUT. A BENCH WAS MOVED BACK A FOOT AND A HALF FROM A PARISH NOTICE BOARD BY A MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY-EIGHT WITH A BARROW ON THE SAYING OF A MAN OF ABOUT NINETY, NEITHER ASKING THE OTHER FOR ANYTHING; AND A HARDWARE SHOP'S DOOR SIGN THAT SAID HALF PAST EIGHT WAS CHANGED TO A QUARTER TO EIGHT BY A WOMAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY-THREE, WITH A MARKER AND THEN A STENCIL, BY A MAN BEHIND THE TILL WHO TOLD NOBODY.**

## 4. The delegation, and the boundary

- **ON 28 JANUARY 2016 JONAS MERCER SAID NO IN A ROOM OVER A SHOP TO A WOMAN WITH A PLASTIC WALLET, IN ABOUT A QUARTER OF A SECOND, AND IT IS THE FIRST TIME HE HAS SAID NO TO A PERSON WHO WANTED SOMETHING OFF HIM. TWO LETTERS ABOUT THE SAME SUNDAY IN FEBRUARY, BOTH BINDING, DATED THE SEVENTH AND THE ELEVENTH OF JANUARY, WERE NOT CANCELLED AND NEITHER CANCELS THE OTHER.**
- **HE HANDED IT TO TOMAS RENN, WHO IS NOT A DEPUTY AND AGREED TO NOTHING, AND SAID OUT LOAD ONCE THAT HE HAD RUN OUT OF A THING HE WAS GOOD AT. RENN ASKED BOTH WRITERS THE SAME QUESTION AND IT WAS NOT *WHICH LETTER IS YOURS*. THE TWO WOMEN CAME TO THE ROOM ON THE THURSDAY BEFORE, UNASKED, AND MADE A RULE BETWEEN THEMSELVES. JONAS MERCER SAID *YOU WERE RIGHT* AND *THERE IS NOTHING I CAN PUT NEXT TO IT*, WHICH IS THE SECOND TIME THAT SENTENCE HAS BEEN SAID IN THIS ACCOUNT. NOBODY GAVE RENN A TITLE, A GRADE, A DEPARTMENT, A CHAIR, A SEAT, A FORM OR A ROW ON ANYTHING, HE IS ON NOTHING, HIS OWN THREE ASKS ARE UNMOVED, AND HE WENT HOME AT ABOUT TEN PAST SIX.**
- **THE LEARNED THING, WHICH IS HALF OF WHAT THIS VOLUME IS FOR, IS THAT A DELEGATED THING GETS DONE DIFFERENTLY AND NOT WORSE.**
- **ON 2 FEBRUARY 2016 AT ABOUT TWENTY TO SEVEN, IN A CORNER BY A RACK OF LEAFLETS IN A ROOM OVER A SHOP, SANAA IQBAL SET A PERSONAL BOUNDARY THAT WAS NOT ABOUT THE WORK: HE HAD BEEN IN THAT ROOM AT ABOUT TEN PAST SIX ON A TUESDAY FOR SIX WEEKS WITH NO ARRANGEMENT AND NO LIST, AND NINE PEOPLE IN THAT BUILDING HAD WORKED OUT THAT THE LAST TEN MINUTES OF THEIR AFTERNOON WAS A THING THAT HAPPENS, AND SHE IS NOT GOING TO BE THE REASON A ROOM OF NINE PEOPLE ARRANGE ANYTHING. HE ACCEPTED IT IN ONE SENTENCE, *Right. I won't come at six*, AND DID NOT ASK WHY, DID NOT APOLOGISE, DID NOT OFFER AN EXPLANATION, AND SAID NOTHING AT ALL FOR THE REST OF THE EVENING.**
- **NEITHER WAS THE REASON THE OTHER WAS IN THE ROOM. IT WAS NOT A REPAIR AND IT WAS NOT A RUPTURE AND IT WAS ALSO NOT NOTHING. THEY LEFT AT ABOUT TEN PAST EIGHT WITHOUT WAITING FOR EACH OTHER. NOTHING ABOUT THE SEPTEMBER ARGUMENT IS SETTLED, HER TWENTY MINUTES A WEEK AND THE COMPLIANCE PARAGRAPH BEHIND IT ARE UNTOUCHED, AND PARAGRAPH 4.11 IS NOT ANSWERED.**

# VOLUME 05 OPEN THREADS — after Batch 0004, Chapter 234, Saturday 16 April 2016

**Read with the "VOLUME 05, BATCH 0004 — CHAPTERS 225–234" block at the end of `state/continuity.md`, which carries the canon for these ten chapters and supersedes this block wherever the two differ, and with the "VOLUME 05, BATCH 0003 — CHAPTERS 215–224" block above it, which still governs everything those ten chapters did. **VOLUME 04 IS CLOSED. THE SERIES IS AT 234 OF 720. NOTHING IN BATCH 0004 RESOLVED ANYTHING AND NOTHING IN IT MAY BE UNDONE BY VOLUME 05. EVERY DAY-COUNT BELOW IS ADDED UP FROM ITS ANCHOR ON THE DAY IN QUESTION AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM A SUMMARY: at Saturday 16 April 2016 the blank line under the nineteenth is five hundred and seven days, the card in the slot is four hundred and twenty-one, and the letter in the tray is four hundred and twenty-three.**

## 1. The third regional system, and the exact shape of its bargain

- **PENHALE IS NOT A TOWN. IT IS A BEND IN A ROAD WITH A BUS STOP, ABOUT FORTY HOUSES AND FOUR VANS, AND A SHARED YARD ON THE FLAT PART. THE THIRD SYSTEM IS THE BEST OF THE THREE, IS NOT HIDDEN, IS NOT RUN BY ANYBODY IN PARTICULAR, IS NOT A SCAM, AND IS NOT FRAUD. NOBODY IN IT IS A VILLAIN AND EVERY SENTENCE ANY OF THEM SAYS IS CORRECT.**
- **THE ONE THING EVERYBODY AGREES ABOUT IS A RULE AND NOT A THING, AND IT IS ONE SENTENCE FRAMED ON A WALL: *Any part of any system in this region may be used by any person.* THE SECOND LINE OF THE SAME RULE EXISTS AND IS NOT SECRET AND IS NOT A DIFFERENT RULE: *A person has no standing in respect of anything recorded under this.* BOTH ARE OFF ONE ORIGINAL, 1987, NEITHER REPRINTED, THE ONE-LINE IS WHAT ANYBODY READS AND THE TWO-LINE IS WHAT ANYBODY SIGNS, AND THE REASON IS THAT A NAME WOULD MAKE THE YARD A BUSINESS.**
- **THE ARRANGEMENT OWNS NOTHING. SHED, RACKING, PIPE, TWO TRUCKS, THE HUT, THE BENCH, THE FENCE AND THE POOL ALL BELONG TO SOMEBODY ELSE. THE MAJOR TURN OF THE WHOLE THIRD SYSTEM AND THE LINE JONAS MERCER BROUGHT AWAY AND SAID TO HIMSELF AND TO NOBODY: *A thing nobody owns can be used by everybody, and so can the paper that comes with it.* THAT IS THE THIRD OF THREE SENTENCES HE HAS SAID TO HIMSELF AND PUT IN NO ROOM.**
- **THE SCHEME PUTS A CREW ON ANY JOB IN THE REGION FOR NOTHING AND THE JOB CARRIES A PIECE OF PAPER AT THE END OF IT AND THE PAPER IS NOT AN INVOICE. IT IS A CLAIM FORM, THE SAME FORM EVERY TIME, AND THE SUM ON IT IS NOT WHAT THE JOB COST. NOBODY IN THAT YARD CAN SAY NO TO ANYBODY AND NOBODY THERE HAS EVER ASKED A CREW WHAT A JOB OUGHT TO COST.**
- **NOBODY'S REQUIRED TO BE ONE OF THE NINE YARDS, AND ABOUT FOUR OF THE NINE HAVE NEVER SENT OR TAKEN A CREW AND PAY IN EXACTLY THE SAME. HALDANE PERRY, A MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY-NINE, KEEPS THE BOOK, REFUSED A WALL SHEET GOING DOWN AND WAS TOLD HE WAS THE BOSS. A WOMAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY-SIX LET THE YARD BE USED IN 1987 WHEN IT WAS A FIELD AND HAS NEVER CHARGED ANYBODY AND IS NOT GOING TO.**

## 2. The crew, the job sheet, and the year

- **ON SATURDAY 12 MARCH 2016 ABBY MARLOW, A WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY-THREE, WENT OUT OF A SHOP ON A PARADE IN THIS BOROUGH AT TEN PAST SEVEN WITH TWO MEN IN THEIR THIRTIES ON A GOOD JOB THEY HAD ALL BEEN ASKED TO DO BY NOBODY, AND CAME BACK, AND THE ONLY PERSON WHO WILL BE TOLD ABOUT IT IS A MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE WHO IS FOUR DAYS A WEEK ON THE FAR SIDE OF THE RIVER AND HAS NOT BEEN RUNG.**
- **ABBY MARLOW SAID *A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED* ON A PAVEMENT, AND ADDED THAT SHE LIKES IT HERE, AND THAT IF ANYBODY TOLD HER SHE HAD BEEN DONE A FAVOUR SHE WOULD NOT HAVE IT. THE BATCH DID NOT REFUTE THAT SENTENCE AND NOTHING IN IT MAY.**
- **JONAS MERCER WAS HANDED A JOB SHEET ON 28 FEBRUARY BY A MAN OF EIGHTY-NINE WHO THOUGHT HE WAS ON A CREW, AND READ IT BEFORE HE SIGNED IT BECAUSE HE SIGNS THINGS, AND SIGNED IT. THE FIGURE WAS £163. THE PRINTED LINE UNDER IT SAYS *the rate for that work as agreed, 1987.* IT WAS IN HIS INSIDE POCKET FOR NINETEEN DAYS AND WENT INTO NELL ARDENT'S HANDBAG ON 18 MARCH.**
- **IT IS AN INHERITED CLAIM AND THE PAPER FOLLOWS THE CREW AND NOT THE JOB. TOMAS RENN WAS ASKED WHAT THE SUM ON THE TOP OF IT WAS AND SAID *THAT'S THE SUM*, TWICE, AND WAS BEING HELPFUL, AND THERE IS NO WAY TO ASK THAT QUESTION SO THAT A PERSON HEARS IT.**

## 3. THE MIDPOINT REVERSAL, IN FULL, AND IT IS NOT FRAUD

- **NOBODY IS BEING CHEATED. THE SUM IS THE SUM THE REGION AGREED IN 1987. THE WORK WAS DONE FOR THAT SUM THEN AND EVERY JOB SINCE HAS BEEN DONE FOR THAT SUM. THE RATE HAS NOT BEEN REVISED BECAUSE NOBODY WHO IS OWED THE DIFFERENCE IS STILL IN ANY OF THE BODIES THAT AGREED IT: TO MOVE A NUMBER YOU NEED A PERSON WHO LOSES, AND THERE IS NO PERSON WHO LOSES. THE PEOPLE IN THE YARD HAVE KNOWN THE YEAR WAS OLD SINCE ABOUT 1994 AND HAVE NEVER BEEN ASKED WHAT HAPPENS WHEN THE YEAR IS OLD, BECAUSE THAT QUESTION IS A QUESTION TO A BODY AND THERE IS NO BODY.**
- **THE CUMULATIVE FIGURE IS ELEVEN CLAIMS AND £1,812, ADDED UP TWICE BY HAND ON THE BACK OF AN ORDER PAD: £140, £152, £163, £171, £168, £174, £112, £180, £166, £190, £196. THE SPAN IS CARRIED AS *SINCE THE SPRING* AND, ON THE SHEET, AS DATES: THE FIRST JOB IS THE WEEK THE WATER WENT DOWN. ***ELEVEN MONTHS* IS A CLOSED FIGURE AND IS NOT PRINTED, AND A WRITER WHO PRINTS IT IS USING A NUMBER THIS BATCH WAS FORBIDDEN.**
- **THE DRAIN IS NOT THE SUM, WHICH IS SMALL, AND NOT THE PAPER, WHICH IS CORRECT. THE DRAIN IS THAT EVERY JOB A CREW FROM THIS BOROUGH DOES IN THAT REGION PUTS A CLAIM AGAINST THIS BOROUGH THAT NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH CAN ANSWER, MEASURE, REFUSE OR PLAN FOR, AND THE ARRANGEMENT IS ENTIRELY VOLUNTARY AND IS STILL JOINED BY PEOPLE WHO WANTED TO JOIN IT. THE LINE ON THE WALL SAYS A PERSON HAS NO STANDING IN RESPECT OF ANYTHING RECORDED UNDER THIS, AND IT IS NOT THERE AS A TRICK; IT IS THERE SO THAT THE YARD CAN NEVER BE A BUSINESS. A BOROUGH THAT SENDS CREWS INTO AN ARRANGEMENT THAT WORKS THAT WAY HAS AGREED, WITHOUT A MEETING, THAT IT HAS NO COUNTERPARTY.**
- **JONAS MERCER IS THE FIRST PERSON IN TWO COUNTRIES TO PUT THE TWO HALVES NEXT TO EACH OTHER AND THE ONLY ONE IN A POSITION TO. HE DID IT IN A CORRIDOR AND A CAR PARK AND HE DID NOT PUT IT IN A ROOM. **NOT ONE PERSON HAS BEEN DISCOVERED AND THE FINDING IS NOT A FINDING ABOUT A PERSON. HE HAD FOUND NOBODY. NOBODY IN TWO COUNTRIES WAS STANDING BEHIND ANY OF IT.**

## 4. The regional trade as a political weapon

- **THE TRADE IS A WEAPON BECAUSE NOBODY HAS TO USE IT AS ONE. A BOROUGH THAT DECIDED ITS CREWS DID NO MORE WORK OVER THE WATER WOULD FIND, IN ABOUT THREE WEEKS, THAT ITS OWN INSURANCE, ITS OWN PARTS, ITS OWN HIRE AND SOMETIMES ITS OWN FUEL WERE MORE EXPENSIVE. NOBODY WOULD HAVE HAD TO DO ANYTHING HOSTILE AND THE PEOPLE WHO WOULD HAVE ENJOYED IT ARE PEOPLE THIS BOROUGH HAS NEVER MET. A REGION CAN BE WEAPONISED BY ARITHMETIC.**
- **THE FOUR ARE CORRANCE SUPPLY, SARN AGGREGATES, LOWDALE HIRE AND MARCEMONT. ALL FOUR SELL INTO THE SHARED YARD, ALL FOUR ARE CORRECT, AND NOT ONE OF THEM HAS EVER BEEN ASKED A QUESTION. THE CORRANCE COUNTER HAS HAD THEM NINE YEARS AS HER BIGGEST SINGLE ACCOUNT AND DOES NOT KNOW THE RATE. THE LOWDALE MAN SAYS HALF HIS TURNOVER IN THIS BOROUGH IS TRADE THAT GOES OUT OF IT AND CALLS IT *A TUESDAY*. THE MARCEMONT FORECOURT IS ABOUT A TENTH ABOVE THE YARD AND HAS BEEN REPAINTED TWICE.**
- **JONAS MERCER'S LINE, SAID OUT LOUD IN A CAR, TO HIMSELF AND TO NOBODY: *They did not do anything to us. They sold us what we asked for.*** **IT IS UNRESOLVED, IT IS NOT A TWELFTH REFUSAL, AND A NODE WITH NO TERM IN FORCE IS NOT A PERSON WITH NO ADDRESS AND HE ALMOST WROTE THAT DOWN AND DID NOT WRITE IT.**

- **THE CLAIM OF 26 DECEMBER 2015 IS STILL IN A DRAWER AT THE END OF A CORRIDOR AND IS STILL A CLAIM FOR MONEY, AND **IT HAS NOW BEEN IN THIS BOROUGH NINETY-SIX DAYS: 28 IN A DRAWER, 30 ON A SHELF BY THE FILING CABINETS, 2 OVER CHRISTMAS IN A TRAY UNDER A WINDOW, THE REST IN THE POST. A TRAY UNDER A WINDOW IS A TRAY UNDER A WINDOW.** THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE WHO HAS THE ROOM HAS SAID OUT LOUD THAT SHE DOES NOT KNOW WHAT IT IS, AND IS RIGHT.
- **AND ON THE SAME DESK THERE IS A SCHEDULE FROM PENHALE RULED INTO ELEVEN LINES UNDER A COLUMN HEADED CLAIM, DATED FROM THE WEEK THE WATER WENT DOWN TO THE FIRST OF APRIL 2016, WITH A DATE, A PLACE, A NAME AND A SUM IN EACH LINE, AND THE YARD HAD GOT ALL ELEVEN SUMS RIGHT. **IT IS THE FIRST THING THAT HAS EVER COME BACK ALONG A DOCUMENT TO THIS BOROUGH THAT NAMES A PERSON BY NAME, BY DATE AND BY PLACE. IT IS CORRECT. IT IS A CARBON AND NOT A DEMAND.** IT IS STILL A DRAWER PROBLEM AND NOT A SUBJECT.
- **ALL ELEVEN CAN BE NAMED AND NONE OF THE ELEVEN WAS ASKED. THE MAN OF ABOUT THIRTY-ONE KNOWS TWO OF THE NAMES AND HAS SAID SO TO NOBODY, AND WAS NOT ASKED, AND WOULD NOT HAVE BEEN ABLE TO SAY WHICH TWO.**

## 6. The borough's own sentence, and the six questions

- **ON 6 APRIL TOMAS RENN SAID *A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED* AND THE FACT THAT THIS BOROUGH HAS PAID NOTHING SINCE THE SPRING AND IS ABOUT TO BE GIVEN A BILL FOR IT BY ANOTHER CITY, AND THE ROOM HEARD IT AS MAKING AN OBJECTION, AND MRS OBI ASKED WHAT HE WAS ASKING FOR, AND HE OPENED HIS MOUTH AND SHUT IT.**
- **AND THE SENTENCE THAT SURVIVED IS THE BOROUGH'S OWN AND IT IS OLD AND IT IS NOT NEW AND IT IS NOT A TWELFTH REFUSAL: A DRAWING ON ABOUT FORTY WALLS, A PUMP IN A DOORWAY, A SHOVEL AND A BROOM, A LABEL ON A SHELF, A BENCH MOVED, A SIGN CHANGED, A HOSE NOBODY CAME FOR, AND A MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTY-SIX RAKING AN INTAKE BY HAND ARE ALL PEOPLE WHO WERE NOT ASKED AND NOT COUNTED, AND THE TOWN THAT OWNS THEM OWNS NOBODY, AND THE REGION THAT WILL SEND THE BILL OWNS NOBODY EITHER, AND THAT IS THE SAME SENTENCE TWICE AND IT IS STILL TRUE.**
- **ON 11 APRIL, IN A METHODIST HALL ON CARROW LANE, ALL SIX QUESTIONS WERE RESTATED IN ORDER AND NONE WAS ANSWERED, AND A MAN IN HIS SIXTIES PUT A VERSION ON THE TABLE THAT WOULD HAVE SETTLED THREE AT ONCE AND IT RESTS ON A DOCUMENT THAT CANNOT BE PRODUCED, AND HE SAID SO HIMSELF, IN FRONT OF NINE PEOPLE, AND SAT DOWN, AND HE NAMED THE TEMPTATION TO ADD A SEVENTH QUESTION AND LEFT IT ALONE. THE BATCH ASKED NO NEW ONE.**
- **THE FIFTH QUESTION IS A THIRD DOCUMENT. THE SIXTH HAS TWO CLAIMS AND A SCHEDULE. THE REGISTER IS AT NINETEEN. NOBODY WAS GIVEN A TITLE, A GRADE, A DEPARTMENT, A CHAIR, A SEAT, A MEMBERSHIP, A FORM OR A ROW ON A ROTA.**

## 7. Still open, and still forbidden to Batch 0005

Nothing in Volume 04 has been resolved, undone, improved on, restated or answered, and nothing in Batch 0001, 0002, 0003 or 0004 may be. Nobody renews the order, nobody expires it, nobody renames the blank term, nobody puts a name in the line on page two, nobody fills the empty square, Roy Albery does not walk again, Jonas and Sanaa are not repaired, nobody dies, nobody is rescued by chance, nobody walks again, the eleven refusals are not twelve, the counter answers nothing and was not entered, the rota is not rebuilt, the second line under Bram Osei's Thursday is not looked at, the register does not move, the letter, the card, the page, the printed sheets, the column, the shop-window card, the crate with a lid and the four bottles all stand, the drawer under the counter is not opened, the sentence in it is not found, and nobody may ask who wrote it. The counter-amendment and the second signature are not Volume 05's. **THE QUIET CUT, THE CIVIC ERASURE, THE ORIGIN OF THE COMMUNITY-WATCH TERM, THE THREE-LINE BRASS TOKENS, THE FIRST CHARTER, THE PROXY, THE ROOT CHAMBER, THE LOWGLASS FLOOR, THE BORROWED CITY AND THE NINE HINGES ARE NOT SPENT. FRANCES TOLLEY IS NOT NAMED AND THE READING RULE IS NOT USED. PRUE CALLOWAY'S FINDING IS NOT OPENED AGAIN AND THE CLAIM TO OPEN A DOOR IS SPENT. NO WEIR RESIDENT MAY BE DESCRIBED IN ANYTHING THAT LEAVES A STREET, AND THE FILLING ROUND DOWN COLLIER LANE IS NOT THE WEIR AND THE TWO MAY NEVER BE JOINED. COLIN WHARTON IS NOT NAMED AND NOBODY GOES UP THAT LANE. A WOMAN OF THIRTY-FOUR IS NOT NAMED, REFERENCED, GENDERED OR GIVEN A CONDITION. PARAGRAPH 4.11 IS NOT ANSWERED AND THE COMPLIANCE WEEK IS NOT VERIFIED. NO ROOT TERM EXPIRES AND THE RECLAMATION SEASON IS NOT SPENT, NOT ANTICIPATED AND NOT DESCRIBED AS APPROACHING — AND THE DISCREPANCY BETWEEN THE VOLUME 05 SECTION OF THE SERIES OUTLINE AND THE VOLUME 04 CLOSE PROMPT IS STILL OPEN AND IS FOR A HUMAN.**

**AND A SECOND FLAG THAT IS ALSO STILL OPEN: THE NAME OF THE CITY. THE PROSE SAYS TIDEGLASS AND NO CHARACTER MAY NOTICE, MISNAME OR ASK ABOUT IT.**

**AND FIGURES THAT MAY NOT BE AGED BY A LATER BATCH, BECAUSE THEY APPEAR WITHOUT ONE ON THE PAGE: HANNA WRAY, WHO KEEPS THE COUNTER MOST WEEKS IN THE SHOP ON THE PARADE, AND EVERY UNAGED FIGURE LISTED IN §11 OF THE BATCH 0004 BLOCK. **INVENTING A FIGURE FOR ONE OF THEM IS INVENTING AN OCCUPIED FIGURE AND IS THE TENTH TIME THAT WOULD HAPPEN IN THIS ACCOUNT. AND A LINE HERE THAT SAID THESE TWO MAY NOT BE AGED BECAUSE THE CENSUS RAN OUT WAS MEASURED AND CORRECTED: THE CENSUS DID NOT RUN OUT, THE FREE FIGURES REMAINING AFTER BATCH 0004 ARE WOMEN 18, 87 AND 90 AND NO MEN, AND A LATER BATCH MAY USE ANY OF THOSE THREE.**

**THE MAJOR MOVEMENT IS COMPLETE. THE MIDPOINT REVERSAL IS SPENT, IN FULL, IN 0229 AND 0230, AND IT IS NOT FRAUD, NOT A VILLAIN, NOT A NEW FINAL ENEMY, NOT A TWELFTH REFUSAL, NOT RESOLVED, AND NOT A FINDING ABOUT ANY PERSON. THE COMPACT, THE MUTUAL RIGHT OF RETURN, THE WIDER CIVIC EXPERIMENT, THE OTHER FIRST ADMINISTRATORS, THE CLIMAX AND THE RESOLUTION ARE ALL STILL AHEAD AND NONE OF THEM HAS BEEN SPENT AND NONE OF THEM HAS BEEN ANTICIPATED IN A DOCUMENT, A LETTER OR A CHARACTER'S INTENTION. THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-05/batch-0005/PROMPT.md`, CHAPTERS 235–244, AND IT IS THE BATCH THE COMPACT BELONGS TO.**

# VOLUME 05 OPEN THREADS — after Batch 0005, Chapter 244, Saturday 4 June 2016

**Read with the "VOLUME 05, BATCH 0005 — CHAPTERS 235–244" block at the end of `state/continuity.md`, which carries the canon for these ten chapters and supersedes this block wherever the two differ, and with the "VOLUME 05, BATCH 0004 — CHAPTERS 225–234" block above it, which still governs everything those ten chapters did. **VOLUME 05 IS NOT CLOSED AND IS NOT DECLARED CLOSED. THE SERIES IS AT 244 OF 720. NOTHING IN BATCH 0005 RESOLVED ANYTHING AND NOTHING IN IT MAY BE UNDONE BY VOLUME 05. EVERY DAY-COUNT BELOW IS ADDED UP FROM ITS ANCHOR ON THE DAY IN QUESTION AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM A SUMMARY: at Saturday 4 June 2016 the blank line under the nineteenth is five hundred and fifty-six days, the card in the slot is four hundred and seventy, and the letter in the tray is four hundred and seventy-two.**

## 1. What this borough is called first for, and why it cannot be put on a table

- **THE PRIORITY IS A PERSON AND A PLACE AND NEITHER CAN BE GIVEN TO ANYBODY.** It is a board about the size of a tea tray in a brick lock-up off a yard, with a strip of card tape and three cards cut with something that was not scissors: BOAT, ENGINE, LOW PEVERELL, and a gap under the third one where a name was.
- **THE MAN AT NUMBER FORTY-ONE IS ON NOTHING, PUT A PUMP IN HIS OWN DOORWAY IN THE WET WEEK OF THE NOVEMBER BEFORE LAST, AND TOOK HIS OWN NAME OFF THAT BOARD ABOUT THREE YEARS AGO BECAUSE *A NAME ON A BOARD IS A THING THAT GETS ASKED ABOUT, AND I'VE BEEN ON ENOUGH LISTS*. THE NAME IS IN A DRAWER IN HIS KITCHEN FOUR DOORS UP THE STREET. HE TOLD HEAR ABOUT IT BECAUSE A MAN IN A SHOP TOLD HIM.** He is the person the sacrifice in 0241 costs and he was not asked about it and was not asked to stop.
- **NELL ARDENT HELD BOTH HALVES AT ONCE, ON THE PAGE, STANDING IN FRONT OF THE BOARD:** a duty can be given to a man who is standing there, and you cannot hand a man to anybody either and you cannot hand a place to anybody. **THE SENTENCE *A DUTY IS AN ACT AND NOT A PLACE* IS NOWHERE IN CHAPTERS 235–244.**
- Jonas Mercer was in the car park and was not in the boathouse. His sum that day was five hundred and twelve.

## 2. The compact, and the four companies, and a right of return

- **A COMPACT CANNOT BE AGREED WITH A YARD, A RULE OR A BOOK. EVERY SENTENCE SAID ABOUT THAT AT PENHALE ON 26 APRIL WAS CORRECT.** A rule is not a party; a rule has not got a mouth; the yard cannot require anybody and therefore nobody in it can be told yes either.
- **A COMPACT IS AGREED WITH PEOPLE ONE AT A TIME AND EACH GIVES SOMETHING THAT IS THEIRS AND NOT SOMETHING A BODY OWNS.** Agreed so far: the woman of about forty-two with the key, with the hall, the notebook, the gas and no money, and a stated price of a Wednesday mothers' group and of becoming a woman running a thing; and a line that is not a claim in the carbon book at Penhale, which is worth nothing because a column is a column.
- **TOMAS RENN SAYS NO IN THREE CHAPTERS — 0236, 0237 AND 0238 — FOR ONE REASON, WHICH HE DOES NOT ADD TO AND WHICH HE IS RIGHT ON, AND NOBODY IN ANY OF THE THREE ROOMS ARGUES HIM OUT OF IT.** He has not said yes and may not be made to. A compact agreed with people one at a time is a list, and he knows what happens to lines.
- **THE FOUR COMPANIES WERE ASKED THE SAME QUESTION AND GAVE THE SAME ANSWER FOUR TIMES, ON SUNDAY 1 MAY: THEY WOULD SELL TO SOMEBODY ELSE.** Corrance Supply, from a room over a shop on a parade, with the rate on a board on a shelf behind her that she has never read. Sarn Aggregates, from a weighbridge: never turned a job away in twenty years, not one, not for a reason. Lowdale Hire, from a cabin, having already said the whole of it once in the February: *that's what a Tuesday is, and nobody sat down and made a Tuesday*. **Marchmont** (the fourth is Marchmont and not Marcement, because Chapter 0230 prints it twice and a chapter governs, and the state files that say Marcement are wrong on the spelling), from a card on a post by a gate, about a tenth above the yard: *nobody is coming here to be loyal to a borough; you want a supplier that's loyal, you want a family*. **NONE OF THE FOUR IS WITHHOLDING ANYTHING, BECAUSE NOT ONE OF THEM HAS EVER BEEN ASKED A QUESTION. A REGION CAN BE MADE TO GO SLOW BY A SUPPLY CHAIN THAT IS BEING POLITE, AND THE ONLY THINGS IT COSTS ARE A NOTEBOOK AND A WEEK ON A TUESDAY.**
- **A RIGHT OF RETURN IS A THING A PERSON MAY DO AND NOBODY IN THE BATCH SAYS THE SUMMATION.** The finding is arrived at by the woman of about fifty-five, who refuses Jonas Mercer's question — asking how many ways a person is allowed to do a thing is the same question about both and gets the same answer — and by rejecting three written drafts: a permission with a bad end on it, a rota, and a duty. **THE THING THAT MAKES A RETURN A RIGHT RATHER THAN A FAVOUR IS SOMEBODY BEING THERE WHEN IT IS NEEDED, AND THAT IS A THING TO BE ASKED FOR AND NOT A THING TO BE PROMISED IN ADVANCE. IT IS NOT A LANE ANYBODY LIVES IN, IT IS NOT A WAY OF MAKING A STREET FINDABLE, AND IT IS NOT IN ANY DOCUMENT, AND TWO PEOPLE REFUSED TO WRITE IT DOWN ON THE PAGE.**

## 3. What it cost him, the public part, and the sacrifice

- **ON WEDNESDAY 11 MAY JONAS MERCER SAID THE ASK OUT LOUD TWICE AND IT WAS THE WRONG MAN BOTH TIMES.** The ask is that this borough agree that if there is a regional emergency, its own crews go out of the borough before anything in the borough gets done. He is the man who has seen all three of the pieces of paper, and in about two years the version will be that the man who read the papers asked the borough to give up its first call. **He was not given his standing back and nobody told him it was borrowed, and he offered the other sentence and it was not true and he let it go past without slowing down for it.**
- **HE HANDED THE ASK TO MRS OBI, WHO IS ABOUT THIRTY-SIX AND IS THE SINGLE PERSON IN THIS BOROUGH WHO HAS EVER PUT TO ANOTHER IN A ROOM WHAT THEY WERE ASKING FOR, AND THEN WAITED.** She did not say no and she did not say yes, and she told him most of the borough would do exactly this and then wonder afterwards why nothing happened.
- **THE PRICE WAS THE NOTEBOOK, WHICH HAD BEEN IN HIS INSIDE POCKET SINCE THE SUMMER AND WAS THE ONLY THING HE HAD, AND IT IS NOT GIVEN BACK. AND FROM THAT AFTERNOON NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH ASKED HIM ANYTHING AT ALL, AND HE FOUND OUT EVERYTHING ELSE BY STANDING IN A SHOP WHEN SOMEBODY SAID IT.** His sum on 11 May was five hundred and thirty-two.
- **ON SUNDAY 15 MAY, IN THE HALL OFF THE PARADE, ABOUT NINE PEOPLE LEARNED IN UNDER FOUR MINUTES THAT A COMPACT IS NOT A THING A ROOM CAN AGREE TO**, and that what a room can do instead is find out who would actually be bound by it, and that the answer is a list of people who have never been asked, longer than the room, which the room was not asked to produce and which the man in his sixties refused to produce and said should be made by the people on it, one at a time, over about six months, and by then it will be a different list. **His sum that day was five hundred and thirty-six and he was in the corridor and did not go in.**
- **ON FRIDAY 20 MAY, IN THE METHODIST HALL ON CARROW LANE, MRS OBI GAVE UP THIS BOROUGH'S FIRST CALL OUT LOUD IN A ROOM AND MEANT IT, AND IT IS A PRIORITY AND NOT THE INSTRUMENT.** The instrument is not in force, was not brought back to life to carry it, was not renewed, and no word of any document was altered and no name went in any line and nothing was written down. **The only reason it could be done is that nobody in either city can require anybody to do anything, and that is the first time in this account that the absence of a body has been any use and also the first time it has cost somebody something, and the card on that wall says both halves and neither was argued out of the other.** The man at number forty-one worked out that it is not the crews that have been lost and that nobody is coming down there now, and he said he would be there anyway, and he declined the key to the lock-up so that nobody would have the padlock to write his name in the gap with.

## 4. What came back, and the three weeks

- **THE PRICE DID NOT ARRIVE BECAUSE OF THE SACRIFICE AND DID NOT ARRIVE AFTER IT. IT ARRIVED ABOUT A WEEK BEFORE THE TWENTIETH, THE WAY A PRICE DOES, AND THE THREE WEEKS OF CREWS GOING OUT OF THE BOROUGH FIRST BEGIN AT THE BEGINNING OF MAY.** Hire, parts, fuel and insurance; twenty per cent on the one that matters; crews in the region that used to be ours and buy where they land.
- **A MAN WHO IS THE ONLY GOOD DRAIN MAN IN FOUR STREETS IS NO LONGER, AT NINE YEARS IN, AND HE SAYS IT IS A TUESDAY.** **A WOMAN IN MARSH LANE HAD A VAN EVERY TUESDAY FOR SIX WEEKS, HIRED OUT OF LOWDALE, AND THE TUESDAY WENT SOMEWHERE ELSE, AND THE SAME VAN OFF THE SAME FORECOURT WENT FROM A HUNDRED AND EIGHTEEN TO A HUNDRED AND THIRTY-SIX ON THE SAME HALF-MILE OF ROAD, AND THERE IS NOBODY IN THE WORLD SHE CAN RING ABOUT IT AND SHE HAS LOOKED.**
- **IT DID NOT LAST AND IT WAS NEVER GOING TO AND THE BATCH MAY NOT TURN IT INTO A TRIUMPH.** Her own prediction is that it comes back to about what it was by the middle of June, and that it happened both ways, and that the price is not a punishment and not a reward.
- **THE ONLY PERSON WHO PREDICTED IT IS JONAS MERCER, WHO SAID IT IN A CAR ON A PARADE ON 27 MARCH IN ABOUT NINE SECONDS, AND BEING RIGHT ABOUT IT HAS NEVER BOUGHT HIM A SINGLE THING IN THIS BOROUGH.**

## 5. The sheet of the eight towns, and the wider civic experiment

- **ON SUNDAY 29 MAY A SHEET OF A4 WAS LEFT FACE UP ON A BUS SHELTER BENCH BY A WOMAN WITH A PLASTIC WALLET OF SOMEBODY ELSE'S PAPERS, WHO SAID IT WAS NOT HERS AND GOT BACK ON THE BUS.** One line of printing across the top in about six inches of type, a column of places under it, no box, no name, no address, no reference, no date, nothing at the bottom, blank back.
- **THE LINE SAYS WORD FOR WORD WHAT THE FRAMED SHEET AT PENHALE SAYS.** Under it, eight towns: **Netherby, Tolforth, Halse Cross, Ansterwick, Kirkmoss, Ellersby, Marleby, Ockbrook.** The woman who has been in the Methodist hall on Carrow Lane about two years knows one of them, and Ockbrook's is on the way to her sister's, and she could not point at it.
- **IT MAY NOT BE SAID THAT IT CARRIES A CHARTER, A PROXY, A ROOT TERM, A HINGE, A SEAL, A DATE OF ORIGIN, A NAME OF A BODY, A MECHANISM OR AN EXPLANATION, AND IT DOES NOT, AND IT IS NOT A DOCUMENT ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH COULD ACT ON.** It is not lost, not mislaid, not stolen, and there is nobody to send it to.
- **THE WIDER CIVIC EXPERIMENT: THE ARRANGEMENT PENHALE RUNS ON WAS NOT THE FIRST OF ITS KIND AND WAS NOT THE ONLY ONE, AND IT IS REVEALED BY A LIST OF PLACE NAMES AND BY NOTHING ELSE.**
- **IT IS IN A DRAWER UNDER THE TRADE COUNTER AT THE BACK OF THE SHOP ON THE PARADE, FACE UP, ON TOP OF A DOCKET, UNDER A LOCK, AND HANNA WRAY HAS THE ONLY KEY AND HAS NEVER GIVEN IT TO ANYBODY. IT CAME OUT ONCE, ON 4 JUNE, ONTO A TABLE IN A METHODIST HALL, AND WENT BACK IN AT TEN PAST FIVE WITH THE LOCK TURNED TWICE. NOBODY HAS OPENED IT FURTHER THAN IT WAS OPENED.** That drawer is not the drawer in the vestibule and has a lock and an invoice spike in it.

## 6. The volume's own new question, asked once and not answered

- **ON SATURDAY 4 JUNE, AT ABOUT TEN TO FIVE, IN A METHODIST HALL ON CARROW LANE, THE MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTEEN WHO WASHES UP IN A KITCHEN AT THE BACK OF THE PARADE, FOUR NIGHTS AND A FRIDAY, ASKED IT IN PLAIN WORDS AND HE HAS NO STANDING TO ASK IT: *Somebody had to be first. Somebody wrote the first one. Somebody set it up and put the towns under it. Where are they now? What became of the first lot?***
- **NOBODY ANSWERED HIM. IT WAS NOT MINUTED. NOBODY ELSE IN THAT ROOM HAD A BOOK OUT.**
- **The shape of it is in the room and nobody put three of the things side by side.** Five words: a permission, an obligation, a claim, a compact, a right of return. Four of them can be pointed at. The fifth, a compact, cannot be pointed at anywhere in this borough or in any of those eight towns, and the reason is on a piece of paper on a table with eight towns on it and not one person's name on it anywhere.
- **THE THREE THINGS THAT EXPLAIN NOTHING: THE FIRST SYSTEM DID NOT EXPLAIN THE WOMAN IN THE CORRIDOR, THE SECOND DID NOT EXPLAIN THE MAN, AND THE SHARED YARD DID NOT EXPLAIN THE YEAR.**

Nothing in Volume 04 has been resolved, undone, improved on, restated or answered, and nothing in Batches 0001 to 0005 may be. Nobody renews the order, nobody expires it, nobody renames the blank term, nobody puts a name in the line on page two, nobody fills the empty square, Roy Albery does not walk again, nobody dies, nobody is rescued by chance, nobody walks again, the eleven refusals are not twelve, the counter in the vestibule answers nothing and was not entered, the rota is not rebuilt, the second line under Bram Osei's Thursday is not looked at, the register does not move, the letter, the card, the page, the printed sheets, the column, the shop-window card, the crate with a lid and the four bottles all stand, the drawer under the counter is not opened, the sentence in it is not found, and nobody may ask who wrote it. **THE QUIET CUT, THE CIVIC ERASURE, THE ORIGIN OF THE COMMUNITY-WATCH TERM, THE THREE-LINE BRASS TOKENS, THE FIRST CHARTER, THE PROXY, THE ROOT CHAMBER, THE LOWGLASS FLOOR, THE BORROWED CITY AND THE NINE HINGES ARE NOT SPENT. FRANCES TOLLEY IS NOT NAMED AND THE READING RULE IS NOT USED. PRUE CALLOWAY'S FINDING IS NOT OPENED AGAIN AND THE CLAIM TO OPEN A DOOR IS SPENT. NO WEIR RESIDENT MAY BE DESCRIBED IN ANYTHING THAT LEAVES A STREET, AND THE FILLING ROUND DOWN COLLIER LANE IS NOT THE WEIR AND THE TWO MAY NEVER BE JOINED. **AND THE THIRD FLAG, STILL OPEN: THE NAME OF THE CITY. THE PROSE SAYS TIDEGLASS AND NO CHARACTER MAY NOTICE, MISNAME OR ASK ABOUT IT, AND NEITHER *NACRE* NOR *RIVER STACKS* APPEARS IN CHAPTERS 235–244.**

WHAT IS AHEAD IS THE RESOLUTION: WHETHER THE PACT HOLDS, AND WHAT THIS CITY DOES WITH A SHEET OF EIGHT TOWNS THAT IT CANNOT ACT ON, AND WHAT A COMPACT THAT CANNOT BE POINTED AT IS DOING IN A BOROUGH THAT HAS GIVEN AWAY ITS FIRST CALL AND AGREED A RIGHT OF RETURN WITH NOBODY.
