# VOLUME 05 OPEN THREADS — after Continuation 0006, Chapter 254, Saturday 23 July 2016

**Read with the "VOLUME 05, CONTINUATION 0006 — CHAPTERS 245–254" block at the end of `state/continuity.md`, which carries the canon for these ten chapters and supersedes this block wherever the two differ, and with the "VOLUME 05, BATCH 0005 — CHAPTERS 235–244" block above it, which still governs everything those ten chapters did. **VOLUME 05 IS NOT CLOSED AND IS NOT DECLARED CLOSED. THE SERIES IS AT 254 OF 720. NOTHING IN CONTINUATION 0006 RESOLVED ANYTHING AND NOTHING IN IT MAY BE UNDONE, IMPROVED ON, RESTATED OR ANSWERED BY VOLUME 05. EVERY DAY-COUNT BELOW IS ADDED UP FROM ITS ANCHOR ON THE DAY IN QUESTION AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM A SUMMARY: at Saturday 23 July 2016 the blank line under the nineteenth is six hundred and five days, the card in the slot is five hundred and nineteen, and the letter in the tray is five hundred and twenty-one.**

## 1. What a borough looks like on an ordinary Tuesday

- **THE BOAT AND THE ENGINE ARE BOTH OUT OF THE BOROUGH ON A TUESDAY AND NEITHER WAS ASKED TO GO, AND IT IS THE FIRST TIME IN THIS ACCOUNT THAT THE SACRIFICE HAS BEEN DESCRIBED AS AN ORDINARY WEEK INSTEAD OF AS A NIGHT.** Tomas Renn put it in about a minute: the thing you get used to isn't the work, it's knowing where two vans are, and right now I know where one is.
- **THE ONLY NEW FACT IN THE WHOLE OF IT: A VAN THAT USED TO COME BACK ON A SUNDAY NOW COMES BACK WHEN IT COMES BACK.** It came in at ten past ten on the Saturday. Nobody on that parade looked up. It used to have a name — my lot said Sunday, the Lowdale lad said Sunday, the man at the bottom house said Sunday because he'd heard the Lowdale lad say it, and none of those three had ever been asked by anybody, and that is the mechanism.
- **JONAS MERCER ASKED ONE QUESTION AND GOT *NOBODY CAN TELL YOU THAT EITHER*, WHICH IS NOT THE SAME AS *NOBODY CAN TELL YOU WHICH DAY*, AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF WHAT SIX WEEKS DID.**
- **THE LOW END OF PEVERELL STREET IS COVERED BY ONE MAN WHO WAS NEVER ASKED AND IS NOT ON ANYTHING.** A woman came out of a back yard for a bucket, he answered her, and nobody asked him a second question. That was the whole of the conversation at the bottom of that street.

## 2. The pact, as a thing rather than as a feeling

- **THE PACT HOLDS, AND IT HOLDS BECAUSE NOBODY IS COUNTING IT, AND THE PROOF IS A COLUMN IN A BOOK THAT HAS BEEN RULED SINCE 1987 WITH A GAP IN IT ABOUT TWO INCHES DEEP, AND A LINE THAT IS NOT A CLAIM IS IN THE GAP.**
- **THE MAN IN HIS FORTIES AT THE PENHALE OFFICE REFUSED A FIGURE AND SAID WHY, AND IT IS THE REASON THE PACT HOLDS: I could tell you how much money went through that shed last year, to the penny, and I have never once been asked, and the day somebody writes a number like that down it stops being a fact about a yard and becomes a figure about a yard, and a figure about a yard is how you get a yard that somebody has to have a good year.**
- **NINE YARDS ON THAT BEND, NONE OF WHICH CAN REFUSE ANYBODY, AND ABOUT FOUR OF THEM HAVE NEVER SENT A CREW OUT AND NEVER TOOK ONE IN AND THEY PUT MONEY IN EXACTLY THE SAME. THERE IS NO LIST ON WHICH THOSE FOUR ARE THE FOUR, BECAUSE THERE IS NO LIST.**
- **HALDANE PERRY REFUSED TO LET THE WALL SHEET DOWN, AND THE SECOND OF HIS TWO REASONS IS THE ONE THAT MATTERS: you'd take it away and come back and ask me what it means, and I've got four men and a woman in this county who could put a bit of paper in front of me and make me say something about it, and you're not one of them and I'd rather you stayed not one of them.**
- **THE SECOND LINE UNDER THE FIRST ON THAT WALL IS STILL NOT EXPLAINED BY ANYBODY AND THAT IS CORRECT.** It is on the page as a line nobody prints. In 0246 a character is asked about it and the man in his forties refuses: he has got a theory and has never once been asked and is not going to stand in a hut and guess at a woman.

## 3. The street, the hose, and one sentence

- **THE SENTENCE, WHICH IS THE ONLY SENTENCE, AND IT IS ABOUT A TUESDAY AND NOT ABOUT ANYTHING ELSE, AND IT WAS SAID TO A MAN ON A LADDER: *If your lot are out on a Tuesday, I'll come down and bring that hose out, and I don't want it on that board.* THE MAN ON THE LADDER SAID *RIGHT* AND WENT BACK UP THE LADDER.**
- **THE HOSE IS THE LENGTH THAT HAS BEEN ON THE WALL AT NUMBER FORTY-ONE SINCE THE WET WEEK IN THE NOVEMBER BEFORE LAST, WITH A GREY LINE OF DIRT BEHIND IT WHERE IT HAS BEEN LYING IN ONE PLACE. A PUMP IS NOT A RESCUE AND A HOSE IS NOT A ROTA ROW AND NEITHER SENTENCE IS SAID OR IMPLIED IN ANY CHAPTER.**
- **THE ROUTE BY WHICH ANYTHING REACHES THE BOTTOM OF PEVERELL STREET IS A MAN FROM A SHOP ON THE PARADE WHO STOPS ON THE STEP AND SAYS THE WATER IS OFF.** It has been that route for the thirty-odd years the man has lived there and it is not a system and nobody could be shown to be running it.
- **NOBODY ASKED HIM A QUESTION.** Jonas Mercer asked nothing on any of three evenings that week and had nothing else to do with them, and his sum that day was five hundred and seventy-three.

## 4. The price, and it is not a triumph

- **THE PRICE CAME BACK DOWN AND IT CAME BACK DOWN FOR THE SAME REASON IT WENT UP, AND NOBODY IS A HERO AND NOBODY IS A VILLAIN, AND THE THING THAT GOES UP AND DOWN IN THIS BOROUGH IS A TUESDAY.** The woman in the second house of Marsh Lane is back to a hundred and eighteen and would rather not be pleased about it: it came down the day the boat came back and I had nothing to do with the boat coming back.
- **THE DRAIN MAN IS BACK TO ABOUT WHAT HE HAD IN FEBRUARY AND IS NOT BACK. FOUR STREETS BECAME TWO AND A HALF IN MAY, AND ONE OF THE TWO WHO CAME IN HAS STOPPED COMING IN, SO HE HAS TWO AND THREE-QUARTER STREETS. BACK WOULD BE FOUR, AND THAT IS THE PART HE DOES NOT GET BACK.**
- **THE FOUR COMPANIES SAID THE SAME THING FOR THE FIFTH TIME AND NONE OF THEM ADDED TO IT.** The Corrance account is down about a sixth on its book and the rate is still on a board on the shelf and will not be read. Sarn has never turned a job away in twenty years. Marchmont's card is about a tenth above the shared yard and nobody has asked the man in the office by the gate since the first of May. Lowdale's man says the plant went out on a Friday and came back on a Sunday and that he could not tell you what he would do differently if they had not come back, and that he has wanted blood in it since March and would charge more, and that he has a diary and not a thing.

## 5. The right of return, asked of a person

- **THE ASK WAS PUT BY THE MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTEEN, OUT LOUD, ONE PERSON AT A TIME, IN A DOORWAY, WITH NOTHING WRITTEN DOWN. HE DID NOT MAKE A LIST AND THE REASON IS NOT A PRINCIPLE: he has nobody's names except the ones he already knew from standing in rooms.**
- **SOMEBODY SAID YES WHO IS NOT THE MAN WITH THE PUMP, AND WHAT THEY SAID YES TO WAS A DAY, AND THE FINDING IS THAT A RIGHT OF RETURN IN THIS BOROUGH IS CURRENTLY THE SIZE OF ONE TUESDAY: one Tuesday and a man who finishes at two, and the Tuesday after that he's in Leeds till Wednesday, and the Tuesday after that nobody knows.**
- **THE ASK IS NOT RECIPROCAL AND WAS NOT SOLD AS RECIPROCAL, AND THAT WAS PUT TO HIM IN A ROAD AND HE AGREED WITH IT: if you pretend it is reciprocal this becomes a thing with two halves, and a thing with two halves gets a name, and the name gets a person under it, and then it's the other thing.**
- **THE ONLY PERSON WHO HAD EVER BEEN RELIABLY THERE WAS NOT ASKED, AND THAT IS ARITHMETIC AND NOT SENTIMENT: his own name is in a drawer in his kitchen four doors up the street, and he would have been the fourth name on anybody's list and was on nobody's.**

## 6. The six, and a wrong answer left standing

- **THE FIRST TIME ANYWHERE IN THIS ACCOUNT THAT A WRONG ANSWER HAS BEEN PUT INTO A ROOM AND LEFT THERE. THE MAN IN HIS SIXTIES SAID THAT THE REGISTER IS THE DATED THING THAT WILL BE THE FIRST ONE PROVABLE, SAID SO BEFORE HE PUT HIS HAND UP, SAID IT IS WRONG, AND THE ROOM DID NOT CORRECT HIM.**
- **WHY IT IS WRONG: the register is at nineteen, the last entry is a bulb, the line under it goes down the page and stops about two inches short of the bottom, and everything this borough has done since is not in it, because it was not written down, deliberately, by about nine people in this room over about seven weeks.**
- **WHY HE PUT IT THERE: somebody will say it anyway, and if nobody in the room can take it apart then the room goes home with a wrong thing in it that everybody can repeat, and by August it is the sort of thing a man repeats at a kerb. And: I am not allowed to correct it, because if I correct it I have to say the right one, and I don't have the right one.**
- **THE FINDING, NAMED BY THE YOUNG WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY AND NOT BY HIM: you are allowed to leave a wrong thing in a room if you can put the right one where it was. He can't. None of us can. So it stays and it goes out of here. And that is not the same as the room believing it.**
- **BY WEDNESDAY IT HAD GOT ITSELF INTO A CORRIDOR BY ITSELF, BY A MOUTH THAT HAD NEVER HEARD HIM SAY IT AND WOULD NEVER KNOW HE HAD ARRANGED IT. THE WRONG ANSWER IS IN A BOROUGH NOW AND NOBODY PUT IT THERE.**

## 7. The sheet of eight towns, a second time, and eight names

- **THE SHEET CAME OUT OF THE DRAWER UNDER THE TRADE COUNTER FOR THE SECOND TIME IN THIS BOROUGH, ON FRIDAY 8 JULY, IN FRONT OF ABOUT NINE PEOPLE WHO HAD HEARD OF NONE OF THE EIGHT PLACES.**
- **THE KEY IS HANNA WRAY'S, CAME IN HER HANDBAG AND WENT BACK IN HER HANDBAG, AND SHE HAS NEVER ONCE GIVEN IT TO ANYBODY IN NINE YEARS, AND THE REASON IS NOT THE PAPER AND IS NOT THE ROOM: if somebody wanted to look at it in March I'd have said no in March.**
- **NOBODY CAN ACT ON IT AND THAT IS CORRECT, AND THE ONE THING THAT CAN BE DONE WITH IT IS NOT AN ACT: EIGHT PLACES READ OUT LOUD TWICE, AND EIGHT PEOPLE SAYING THEIR OWN NAMES OUT ONCE, IN NO ORDER, IN WHATEVER ORDER THEY GOT UP IN.**
- **THE EIGHT PLACES WERE READ OUT THREE TIMES IN TOTAL AND ONE HAND WENT UP OUT OF NINE, FOR OCKBROOK, AND THE WOMAN WHO RAISED IT COULD NOT POINT AT IT.** The man in his sixties wanted that written down in a room somewhere, and it was not, and he said that was fine, because a thing you cannot write down is a thing you cannot be proved wrong about later.
- **NOTHING WAS WRITTEN DOWN BECAUSE THERE WAS NOTHING ON THE TABLE TO WRITE ON AND NOBODY HAD BROUGHT ANYTHING. THE MAN AT NUMBER FORTY-ONE'S NAME IS NOT ONE OF THE EIGHT AND NOBODY SUGGESTED ANOTHER NAME.**
- **IT STILL CARRIES NO CHARTER, NO PROXY, NO ROOT TERM, NO HINGE, NO SEAL, NO DATE OF ORIGIN, NO NAME OF A BODY, NO MECHANISM AND NO EXPLANATION, AND 0251 GIVES IT NONE.**

## 8. What two yeses and one no built, which is nothing they can point at

- **THE COMPACT IS THE THING MRS OBI SAID IT WOULD NOT BE, WHICH IS A LIST, AND NOBODY HAS MADE ONE, AND THE ONLY EVIDENCE IT EXISTS IS THAT SEVERAL PEOPLE DID SOMETHING SMALL ON THE SAME DAY WITHOUT ARRANGING IT.** Mrs Obi says it herself and then refuses the next three sentences: I do not know what that means, and anybody in that room who tells you they know what a compact that isn't a list is, is selling you something.
- **WHAT EXISTS INSTEAD: TWELVE PENCIL TICKS IN A BOOK THE WOMAN WITH THE KEY HAS KEPT FOR NINE YEARS, TWELVE ROOMS WITH ABOUT NINE PEOPLE IN THEM AT SOME POINT, AND ONE LINE IN A COLUMN HEADED WITH NOTHING AT ALL AT THE BOTTOM OF A HILL. THAT IS THE ENTIRE OUTPUT OF SIX WEEKS.**
- **THE EVIDENCE, WHICH IS FOUR THINGS ON TUESDAY THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF JUNE AND IS NOT A LIST AND IS NOT MINUTED: A BATH OUT OF A SECOND-FLOOR LANDING IN MARSH LANE, DONE BY A MAN OFF THE RIVER WHO TOLD NOBODY IN ADVANCE AND NOBODY AFTERWARDS · FOUR FEET OF PIPE AND A FITTING LEFT ON A KERB BY THE BOAT CREW WHO TOLD NOBODY · A POUND IN A METER PUT IN BY SOMEBODY NOBODY SAW · A LENGTH OF CHAIN BOUGHT BY THE MAN AT NUMBER FORTY-ONE WHO WOULD NOT SAY WHICH GATE IT WAS FOR.**
- **NOBODY COULD CONNECT THE FOUR OF THEM. THE ONLY THING THAT MADE THEM THE SAME DAY IS THAT ONE MAN SAID A DATE OUT LOUD IN A STREET ON A WEDNESDAY MORNING TO SOMEBODY WASHING UP IN A KITCHEN AND DID NOT PUT IT IN A LIST AND WENT BACK TO WORK.**
- **IT IS NOT A TWELFTH REFUSAL, IT IS NOT A VICTORY, AND NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH CONTAINS A THANKS OF ANY KIND.**

## 9. A question put to the region, and it comes back in the wrong direction

- **A QUESTION WENT OUT OF THIS BOROUGH INSIDE SOMEBODY ELSE'S FOLDER, ON THE BACK OF THE LAST DOCKET IN A BOAT'S JOB WALLET, IN A HAND THAT WAS NOT A TRADESMAN'S HAND: *Who printed the first one, and are they still doing it.* NO NAME, NO ADDRESS, NO COMPANY, NO DATE, NO REFERENCE. THE BOATMAN SAID *THAT'S A TERRIBLE WAY TO DO A THING* AND PUT IT IN THE WALLET AND DID THE DAY'S WORK.**
- **WHAT CAME BACK WAS CORRECT, AND IT IS NOT A BILL AND NOT A DEMAND AND NOT A CLAIM, AND IT IS A SHEET: ONE LINE OF PRINTING ACROSS THE TOP IN ABOUT SIX INCHES OF TYPE, *Any part of any system in this region may be used by any person*, AND UNDER IT ONE PLACE, CENTRED, ON ITS OWN. **AND THE PLACE IS THIS BOROUGH.** THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE READ IT OVER HANNA WRAY'S SHOULDER AND SAID ONE WORD.**
- **AND IT IS NOT ABOUT US AT ALL, IT IS ABOUT A PLACE, AND THAT IS THE NEW PART OF THE FINDING: IT SAYS A PLACE IS ONE OF THE PLACES FROM WHICH A THING MAY BE USED, AND IT DOES NOT SAY ANYBODY IS COMING, IT DOES NOT SAY ANYTHING IS OWED, AND IT DOES NOT SAY THAT A PERSON WHO LIVES THERE HAS BEEN COUNTED OR HEARD FROM OR WRITTEN DOWN ANYWHERE. IT IS A PRINTED PLACE NAME UNDER A PRINTED SENTENCE, AND A PRINTED PLACE NAME IS A FACT ABOUT PRINTING.**
- **AND IT DOES NOT ANSWER THE QUESTION. A LAD WROTE TWO QUESTIONS ABOUT A PERSON AND SOMEBODY READ TWO QUESTIONS ABOUT A PERSON AND ANSWERED WITH A PLACE, AND IT IS A PERFECTLY GOOD ANSWER TO A QUESTION NOBODY ASKED. NOBODY ON EITHER SIDE OF THAT WATER HAS DONE ANYTHING WRONG OR ANYTHING CLEVER OR ANYTHING HOSTILE.**
- **NOT ONE PERSON HAS BEEN DISCOVERED. NOT ONE PERSON IS A PERPETRATOR. NOTHING IS A FRAUD AND NOBODY IS A VILLAIN. THE FOUR COMPANIES SUPPLIED THAT WEEK AT THE SAME RATE WHETHER IT HAPPENED OR NOT.**
- **IT IS IN THE DRAWER UNDER THE TRADE COUNTER, FACE UP, ON TOP OF THE OTHER SHEET, AND THE LOCK WENT ROUND TWICE. THAT DRAWER IS NOT THE DRAWER UNDER THE COUNTER IN THE VESTIBULE IN THE BUILDING BY THE WATER, AND NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH HAS MENTIONED THAT DRAWER AT ALL.**

## 10. An ordinary Saturday, and the line under the nineteenth

- **THE REGISTER IS AT NINETEEN, THE NINETEENTH IS A YARD LIGHT WITH A BULB IN A MAINTENANCE COLUMN AND THE BULB IS OUT, AND THE LINE UNDER IT IS SIX HUNDRED AND FIVE DAYS AND IS STILL BLANK.** Nina Calder said it was the same as last month because it was on her list.
- **A MAN FROM THE PARADE PUT THE WEEK'S ACTUAL SHAPE INTO A ROOM: this is the fifth time I have not known where a van in this borough was, and it is five Tuesdays and not a fortnight, and I have stopped minding, and I have not made my peace with it, and those are two different things, and the difference is the whole of it.**
- **ONE SMALL THING HAPPENED TO JONAS MERCER ON THAT PARADE AND IT IS THE ONLY THING THAT HAPPENED TO HIM IN SEVEN WEEKS: A MAN BEHIND A TILL ASKED HIM WHAT A TWO-INCH SWEPT BEND WENT FOR IN THE CATALOGUE, AND HE ANSWERED, AND IT TOOK ABOUT FOUR SECONDS, AND IT WAS CORRECT. IT IS THE FIRST THING ANYBODY IN THIS BOROUGH HAS ASKED HIM SINCE THE ELEVENTH OF MAY AND IT IS A PRICE. HE IS NOT ASKED ANYTHING ELSE.**
- **THE NEW QUESTION IS STILL A QUESTION AND NOBODY HAS ANSWERED IT, AND IT IS DELIVERED BY AN EXCHANGE AND NOT BY A PARAGRAPH: the woman of about forty-two with the key asks the man of about eighteen whether he is still after that thing he asked; he says he is; she asks whether anybody said anything to him about it; he says no; she says *right*, and puts the key in her bag, and shuts the bag.** THE BAG IS HERS AND IS NOT A DRAWER, AND THE ACCOUNT HAS THREE KEY-HOLDING WOMEN AND TWO LOCKED DRAWERS, SO *THE DRAWER* MAY NOT STAND WITHOUT BEING NAMED.
- **THE HALL OFF A PARADE ON THAT SATURDAY AFTERNOON HAD NINE PEOPLE IN IT AND EVERY ONE OF THEM WAS ON NOTHING, AND THE ENTIRE MINUTE OF IT WAS A MAN ASKING WHETHER ANYBODY WAS GOING TO WRITE ANY OF IT DOWN.**

**AND A REVIEW WAS RUN OVER CHAPTERS 245–254 AND SIX FINDINGS WERE REPAIRED AT THE SOURCE, AND NONE OF THEM CHANGED AN OPEN THREAD: SEVEN CLOSING ALL-CAPS BLOCKS THAT RESTATED THEIR OWN OPENINGS WERE REWRITTEN AS CONSEQUENCE, *A TUESDAY* WAS CUT BACK TO ITS CAP IN FIVE CHAPTERS, TWO SELF-EXPLAINING PARAGRAPHS AND TWO SPEECH TAILS WERE CUT IN THREE CHAPTERS, ALL TEN ENTRIES IN `state/chapter-summaries.md` WERE REWRITTEN SO THAT THE FILE NO LONGER SUPPLIES A CHAPTER WITH ITS OWN SENTENCES, ONE MISATTRIBUTED LINE ABOUT THE WORD *US* WAS CORRECTED IN `state/character-state.md`, AND THE UNANCHORED *THE DRAWER* AT THE END OF 0254 IS NOW HER BAG, WHICH IS WHY CARD 0260 OF THE NEXT PROMPT HAS BEEN RE-STATED. NO CARD, DATE, DAY-COUNT, FIGURE, NAME, MAJOR TURN, STANDING, FLAG OR OPEN THREAD WAS ADDED, REMOVED OR ALTERED. THE FULL RECORD IS §12A OF THE CONTINUATION 0006 BLOCK IN `state/continuity.md`, AND EVERY FIGURE IN THIS BLOCK IS TO BE RE-MEASURED FROM THE CHAPTERS AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM THIS FILE.**

## 11. Still open, and still forbidden to Continuation 0007

Nothing in Volume 04 has been resolved, undone, improved on, restated or answered, and nothing in Batches 0001 to 0005 or in Continuation 0006 may be. Nobody renews the order, nobody expires it, nobody renames the blank term, nobody puts a name in the line on page two, nobody fills the empty square, Roy Albery does not walk again, nobody dies, nobody is rescued by chance, nobody walks again, the eleven refusals are not twelve, the counter in the vestibule answers nothing and was not entered, the rota is not rebuilt, the second line under Bram Osei's Thursday is not looked at, the register does not move, the letter, the card, the page, the printed sheets, the column, the shop-window card, the crate with a lid and the four bottles all stand, the drawer under the counter in the vestibule is not opened, is not counted, is not asked about and is not mentioned, the sentence in it is not found, and nobody may ask who wrote it. **THE QUIET CUT, THE CIVIC ERASURE, THE ORIGIN OF THE COMMUNITY-WATCH TERM, THE THREE-LINE BRASS TOKENS, THE FIRST CHARTER, THE PROXY, THE ROOT CHAMBER, THE LOWGLASS FLOOR, THE BORROWED CITY AND THE NINE HINGES ARE NOT SPENT. FRANCES TOLLEY IS NOT NAMED AND THE READING RULE IS NOT USED. PRUE CALLOWAY'S FINDING IS NOT OPENED AGAIN AND THE CLAIM TO OPEN A DOOR IS SPENT. NO WEIR RESIDENT MAY BE DESCRIBED IN ANYTHING THAT LEAVES A STREET, AND THE FILLING ROUND DOWN COLLIER LANE IS NOT THE WEIR AND THE TWO MAY NEVER BE JOINED. COLIN WHARTON IS NOT NAMED AND NOBODY GOES UP THAT LANE. A WOMAN OF THIRTY-FOUR IS NOT NAMED, REFERENCED, GENDERED OR GIVEN A CONDITION. PARAGRAPH 4.11 IS NOT ANSWERED AND THE COMPLIANCE WEEK IS NOT VERIFIED. NO ROOT TERM EXPIRES AND THE RECLAMATION SEASON IS NOT SPENT, NOT ANTICIPATED AND NOT DESCRIBED AS APPROACHING — AND THE DISCREPANCY BETWEEN THE VOLUME 05 SECTION OF THE SERIES OUTLINE AND THE VOLUME 04 CLOSE PROMPT IS STILL OPEN AND IS FOR A HUMAN. **THE PERMITTED SAINT ORRA GATE SENTENCE WAS NOT USED IN CONTINUATION 0006 AND MAY NOT BE USED TWICE IN THE NEXT BATCH, AND NO TERM AFTER THE TWENTY-THIRD OF MAY MAY BE NAMED OR DATED.**

**AND THE FLAGS, ALL STILL OPEN AND NONE OF THEM A WRITER'S TO CLOSE. FIRST: THE NAME OF THE CITY. THE PROSE SAYS TIDEGLASS AND NO CHARACTER MAY NOTICE, MISNAME OR ASK ABOUT IT, AND NEITHER *NACRE* NOR *RIVER STACKS* APPEARS IN CHAPTERS 245–254. SECOND: THE THIRD OF THE EIGHT PLACES IS PRINTED *Halsey Cross* IN CHAPTERS 0243, 0244 AND 0251 AND *Halse Cross* IN EVERY STATE FILE, AND A HUMAN MAY RULE EITHER WAY AND A WRITER DID NOT. THIRD: WHAT VOLUME 05 IS CENTRALLY. FOURTH: THE BOUNDARY — CHAPTERS 243 THROUGH 254 WERE WRITTEN AS THE TABLES DATED THEM AND BELONG TO NO VOLUME, NO VOLUME 06 OUTLINE WAS WRITTEN, NO VOLUME-CLOSE PROMPT WAS WRITTEN, AND CARD 0254 IS NOT A CLOSE.**

**AND FIGURES THAT MAY NOT BE AGED BY A LATER BATCH: HANNA WRAY, WHOSE AGE IS NOT STATED ANYWHERE IN THIS ACCOUNT, AND EVERY UNAGED FIGURE LISTED IN §14 OF THE CONTINUATION 0006 BLOCK IN `state/continuity.md`, WHICH INCLUDES THE MAN AT NUMBER FORTY-ONE, THE WOMAN WITH THE KEY TO THE LOCK-UP, THE BOAT CREW, THE ENGINE CREW, THE MAN ON THE WEIGHBRIDGE, THE WOMAN ON THE CORRANCE COUNTER, THE MAN IN THE MARCHMONT OFFICE, THE MAN IN THE LOWDALE CABIN, THE DRAIN MAN, THE WOMAN WITH THE VAN, THE MAN FROM THE WORKS ON THE FAR SIDE OF THE RIVER, THE MAN IN HIS FORTIES AT THE PENHALE OFFICE, THE MAN IN HIS SIXTIES WHO IS THE BUILDING ESTIMATOR, THE WOMAN WHO HAS BEEN IN THE METHODIST HALL ABOUT TWO YEARS, A MAN FROM THE PARADE, THE VAN DRIVER, THE WOMAN AT NUMBER THIRTY-EIGHT, THE MAN ON THE STEPLADDER, THE MAN WHO SAID ASK ME ON THE DAY, THE BOATMAN WITH THE WALLET, AND THE TWO PEOPLE WHO SAID THEIR OWN NAMES QUIETLY IN A HALL IN JULY. **NO NEW FIGURE IN CONTINUATION 0006 WAS GIVEN AN AGE AT ALL, AND THAT IS THE BLESSED ANSWER AND NOT A CLOSURE. THE FREE FIGURES REMAINING ARE WOMEN 18, 87 AND 90 AND NO MEN, AND ALL THREE ARE UNSPENT.**

**WHAT IS AHEAD IS NOT A RESOLUTION AND IT IS NOT A CLOSE. WHAT IS AHEAD IS A BOROUGH IN WHICH A PACT IS HOLDING BECAUSE NOBODY IS COUNTING IT, A COMPACT CANNOT BE POINTED AT, A RIGHT OF RETURN IS THE SIZE OF ONE TUESDAY, A PRICE HAS COME BACK DOWN FOR THE SAME REASON IT WENT UP, A WRONG ANSWER ABOUT A REGISTER IS IN TWO ROOMS AND A CORRIDOR, EIGHT PEOPLE HAVE SAID THEIR OWN NAMES OUT ONCE IN A ROOM AND NOTHING WAS WRITTEN DOWN, A CORRECT SHEET WITH THE NAME OF THIS BOROUGH ON IT IS IN A LOCKED DRAWER UNDER A TRADE COUNTER, SIX HUNDRED AND FIVE DAYS IS UNDER A BLANK LINE, AND SOMEBODY HAD TO BE FIRST AND NOBODY HAS ANSWERED IT. THE NEXT PHASE IS `workspace/volume-05/continuation-0007/PROMPT.md`, CHAPTERS 255 ONWARD, AND IT IS A CONTINUATION BATCH AND NOT A VOLUME CLOSE, BECAUSE THE BOUNDARY IS A HUMAN'S AND A WRITER MAY NOT DECIDE IT. IT MAY NOT BE DATED BEFORE SATURDAY THE TWENTY-THIRD OF JULY 2016.**

# VOLUME 05 OPEN THREADS — after Continuation 0007, Chapter 264

**Read with the "VOLUME 05, CONTINUATION 0007 — CHAPTERS 255–264" block at the end of `state/continuity.md`, which carries the canon for these ten chapters and supersedes this block wherever the two differ, and with the "VOLUME 05, CONTINUATION 0006 — CHAPTERS 245–254" block above it, which still governs everything those ten chapters did, and with the "VOLUME 05, BATCH 0005 — CHAPTERS 235–244" block above that. **VOLUME 05 IS NOT CLOSED AND IS NOT DECLARED CLOSED. THE SERIES IS AT 264 OF 720. NOTHING IN CONTINUATION 0007 RESOLVED ANYTHING AND NOTHING IN IT MAY BE UNDONE, IMPROVED ON, RESTATED OR ANSWERED BY VOLUME 05. EVERY DAY-COUNT BELOW IS ADDED UP FROM ITS ANCHOR ON THE DAY IN QUESTION AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM A SUMMARY: at Saturday 17 September 2016 the blank line under the nineteenth is six hundred and sixty-one days, the card in the slot is five hundred and seventy-five, and the letter in the tray is five hundred and seventy-seven.****

## 1. A true detail accreted onto a wrong answer

- **THE WRONG ANSWER ABOUT THE REGISTER NOW CARRIES A DETAIL THAT NOBODY GAVE IT, AND THE DETAIL IS TRUE, AND A TRUE DETAIL CANNOT BE TAKEN BACK OUT.** A woman buying post-fix at a till on 30 July said her mother, who did the dinners at the school over the road, kept the copy before that one, and that there was a line at the bottom of the page where they stopped writing, and called it the only honest thing in the building. **The line is real. It is the line under the nineteenth entry. It is the most reliable thing in this borough's records and it means that about nine people in a room in April decided to write nothing down and left it showing.**
- **THE MAN WHO INVENTED THE WRONG ANSWER CANNOT SAY WHICH PART IS HIS AND IS NOT GOING TO TRY.** His own account of it: he can be told the line is real and he has said so, and he cannot deny it without denying the whole thing, and denying the whole thing would mean saying what the register does prove, which is nothing, and he has never been able to say that. Asked which half is his, he said he could not unpick it in a fortnight because it is in three trades and a school and a queue by then.
- **IT TRAVELS AS ORDINARY TRADE, NOT CONSPIRACY.** A man off a flatbed of a firm of three lorries says it in a queue, his own firm hears it within three hours, one of the three is a different firm, and he says *I haven't* when a man accuses him of having been in a room. **He has not been in a room. Nobody in that queue knew it and nobody asked.**
- **THE CARD ON THE WALL IN THE METHODIST HALL ON CARROW LANE IS QUOTED ONCE, IN 0255, IN HIS MOUTH, AND ONLY ONE OF ITS TWO SENTENCES IS SAID. NEITHER HALF IS ARGUED OUT OF THE OTHER.**
- **JONAS MERCER HEARD ALL OF IT, DID THE SUM, GOT SIX HUNDRED AND TWELVE, WROTE IT ON A PIECE OF PAPER, PUT THE PAPER BACK IN HIS COAT, AND WAS NOT ASKED ANYTHING BY ANYBODY THAT DAY.**

## 2. A locked drawer, a good reason, and nine years

- **A PRINTED FORM FROM A COUNTY OFFICE WANTS A BODY.** It has a reference, three boxes and a return-by date, and it asks what material is held at an address, when it arrived, and whether the person answering intends to keep it. **There is no body. It went back in the post with one of the three boxes answered and the other two left blank, which is what the woman who brought it had said would happen when she asked.**
- **THE GOOD REASON, AND IT IS A REAL ONE: a printed sheet from the firm stripping the first-floor corridor of the building by the water says anything left on a sill, a rail, a shelf or the floor between the tenth and the fourteenth of September will be cleared, and one of the two pieces of A4 in the handbag in that corridor has this borough's name printed under a line.** She wanted one piece of it somewhere a man with a stripper would not find.
- **THE MAJOR TURN: SHE SAID SOMETHING ABOUT THE KEY THAT NOBODY EXPECTED AND THAT IS NOT A REASON FOR OPENING IT, AND THE DRAWER UNDER THE TRADE COUNTER WAS NOT OPENED, AND NOBODY IN THAT BACK ROOM WAS ANGRY.** Asked whether she would ever hand the key to anybody, not today and not this year but ever, Hanna Wray said she did not know, and said it was the first time in nine years anybody had asked her about the key instead of about the paper. Then she said why she carries it: the alarm in that shop has been changed three times in nine years and each time she was the one who had to be there before the man came, and it is not on a hook behind the till and has never been on a hook behind the till.
- **The drawer under the trade counter has a lock on it and an invoice spike in it and two sheets on top of each other in it and was not opened, and none of them has been out since 19 July. The drawer under the counter in the vestibule in the building by the water is not mentioned in any chapter of this batch. The three key-holding women are not in one sentence and not in one list anywhere in these ten chapters.**

## 3. Four accounts, and the one that does not match

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THE FOUR ACCOUNTS DO NOT MATCH AND THE MISMATCH IS THE FINDING, AND NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH IS A LIAR, AND THE ONE THAT DOES NOT MATCH IS THE ONE THAT WAS REPORTED BY SOMEBODY WHO WAS NOT THERE.** They were put to four people in persons' fronts on 8 August and nothing was written down but four headings on the back of an envelope.
- **THE BATH HOLDS.** The woman in the second house of Marsh Lane was there: an hour, under the stairs, a man off the river gone before four — and she insists, unasked and twice, that it was hers, because she had been saying since March that she could do it and could not, and she said the date first.
- **THE PIPE HOLDS.** Four foot and a bit and an elbow, on the kerb outside the shop that shuts of a Sunday, Friday to Monday, gone by the Wednesday morning, never asked for, still under a bit of roofing felt in the yard of the house behind and available to whoever wants it.
- **THE CHAIN HOLDS AS AN ACCOUNT AND NOT AS A FACT.** The woman who has been in the Methodist hall about two years asked him on the Sunday and got *a gate*, and nobody has gone and asked him since.
- **AND THE POUND IN THE METER DOES NOT HOLD, AND IT WAS ALWAYS HERS.** The woman of about forty-two with the key to the hall off the parade said she put it in, has put money in that meter every week since the February, nobody has ever seen her do it, nobody else has ever done it, and if somebody had she would have known because she locks up. **There is no date: she does it on a Monday or a Thursday depending what day she has remembered it, and it has never been written down by anybody.**
- **THE ACCOUNT THAT WENT ROUND FOUR ROOMS CAME FROM HER, SECOND-HAND, OUT OF A ROOM SHE WAS NOT IN, IN A WEEK SHE WAS NOT IN.** Nobody corrected anybody. A woman said *that's what you told me* and a woman said *there's nowhere for that to go*.
- **THE COMPACT'S EVIDENCE IS NOW THREE THINGS AND NOT FOUR, AND NOBODY HAS REPLACED THE FOURTH WITH ANYTHING.** The day itself is still the only date anybody in this borough ever agreed on, and it was agreed by a man in a street and not by a room.

## 4. A barrow, and a man nobody in that corridor had mentioned

- **THE MAJOR TURN: HE DOES A JOB AND SAYS ONE SENTENCE THAT IS NOT A REASON AND NOT A REFUSAL, AND THE SENTENCE IS ABOUT A PERSON NOBODY IN THAT ROOM HAD MENTIONED.** A sack barrow, out of a hardware shop four doors down, on a Saturday morning, asked for by a woman and the young woman of about twenty who could not get four cardboard boxes down two flights of a narrow staircase. He said yes in one word, carried the third box up himself, put it on the barrow, went down, came back for the fourth, and warned them about a step in the light.
- **THE COUNT OF HIS REFUSALS IN THIS ACCOUNT IS THREE AND IT IS STILL THREE.** His no in April was about a compact that is a list and was not a refusal of a barrow, and the chapter says so out loud, and the young woman of about twenty puts it as *a barrow* and he puts it back as *it's a barrow*.
- **THE SENTENCE IS ABOUT A MAN WHO SWEEPS THAT PARADE AT ABOUT HALF FIVE IN THE MORNING AND GOES DOWN THE MIDDLE OF IT AND DOES PEVERELL STREET AFTERWARDS, AND WHO IS NOT ON ANYTHING, AND WHOSE NAME TOMAS RENN COULD NOT TELL YOU.** He has never once seen anybody from this borough say one word to him — not a good morning, not a nod, nothing — and he has never asked him anything and is not going to now.
- **AND IT IS NOT MADE INTO ANYTHING.** He said it on a kerb and not in a room, and his reason is in his mouth: in a room it becomes a thing, and then somebody comes and asks the man whether he wants the hours changed, and he has had a conversation he did not ask for and has to have an answer, and he has not got one and does not need one. **Nothing is arranged for that man, nobody goes down that street at half five, and nobody rings him.**

## 5. Which gate, found out without asking him

- **THE MAJOR TURN: SOMEBODY FINDS OUT WHICH GATE WITHOUT ASKING HIM, AND IT IS NOT THE GATE ANYBODY WOULD HAVE GUESSED.** **IT IS THE FIELD GATE AT THE BOTTOM OF PEVERELL STREET, PAST THE LAST HOUSE, ON THE SIDE OF THE BANK.**
- **IT WAS FOUND OUT BY A GIRL ON A COLLEGE BUS AND NOT BY A SEARCH.** The bus goes down that street and stops past the field gate. The chain is about eight feet, new, bright, hooked over the latch and not fastened, and the padlock on the gate hangs open with nothing in it. She had to unhook it to get through to the stop and she hooked it back exactly as she found it.
- **SHE ELIMINATED IT BY LOOKING, NOT BY ASKING.** Number forty-one's own back gate has a five-lever padlock on it as thick as a wrist and has had one the whole time she has been coming down there, and number thirty-eight's side gate is a wooden gate that has hung crooked since before she was born.
- **NOBODY WENT DOWN THERE AFTER HER AND NOBODY ASKED HIM ANYTHING — NOT ABOUT THE CHAIN, NOT ABOUT THE GATE, AND NOT WHETHER HE WOULD LIKE SOMEBODY TO STOP.** The man of about eighteen said he has about half an hour of his own thinking to do before he could open his mouth on any of it, and that it is not his business. **A man who is there is not a man who has been set free, and no chapter says he has.**
- **He is not rescued, he is not asked whether he would like to stop, nothing is arranged for him, the pump is still in the doorway and the stopcock is still off, and his own name is still in a drawer in his kitchen four doors up the street.** The chapter does not say what the chain is for and 0257 did not ask.

## 6. A lamp, ten pounds, and no question asked

- **THE MAJOR TURN: HE IS ASKED TO DO ONE SMALL ORDINARY THING BY SOMEBODY WHO NEEDS IT DONE AND HE DOES IT, AND NOBODY MAKES IT INTO ANYTHING, AND IT IS THE ONLY THING THAT HAPPENS AND IT IS ENOUGH FOR THE CHAPTER. HE DOES NOT ASK HIS QUESTION AGAIN.**
- **THE THING WAS A LAMP IN THE FIRST-FLOOR CORRIDOR OF THE BUILDING BY THE WATER**, which went out about a fortnight before, where the works say it is not their light, where there is a three-tread kitchen step under the window and the fitting is over the fire door at the other end. He put the step on the top landing of the stairs to get the extra foot, and **it took twenty minutes instead of two, because the box in her room had the old sort of bulb in it** and he had to run down the parade and back, and a man behind a till counted four out of a box behind the till and said they would want the money for them.
- **SHE PAID HIM AND HE TOOK IT AND NEITHER OF THEM SAID ANYTHING ABOUT IT, AND THERE ARE NO THANKS OF ANY KIND IN THIS BATCH.** She told him, in a corridor, that she had been thinking of asking him something since the summer and had decided not to, and why, and he said *that was June*.
- **ON THE PARADE THE WOMAN HE WORKS FOR ASKED HIM WHERE HE WENT AND HE SAID HE DONE A LIGHT AND SHE SAID *WHO FOR* AND HE SAID *THE WOMAN AT THE END OF THE CORRIDOR* AND SHE SAID *RIGHT* AND WENT BACK TO THE SINK. NOBODY PUT IT ON ANYTHING.**
- **He is on the dishes, on nothing, and has been given no title, grade, department, chair, seat, membership, form, row on a rota or way of being thanked. The bag in the July exchange is hers and is not a drawer, and no drawer is mentioned in this chapter at all.**

## 7. The argument of 11 April, heard back in September

- **THE MAJOR TURN: HE DOES NOT DISOWN IT AND HE DOES NOT OWN IT, AND THE THIRD THING IS SAID BY SOMEBODY ELSE.** The argument of 11 April, which rested on a piece of paper nobody in this borough could put on a table, and which he told the room in May he could not unmake, was said that evening in the Methodist hall on Carrow Lane **by a man from the parade who had heard it in a corridor in July, who had not been told whose it was, and who assumed that a thing said in a corridor in July came out of somebody who had earned it.**
- **WHAT HE SAID ABOUT IT IS TWO TRUE HALVES:** it is his in the sense that it came out of his head in April, and it is not his in the sense that it came out of another man's mouth, and he will not take a mouth that is not his and will not say it did not come from his head either. He was asked why he did not say that in July and said he could have, that he said nothing in July, and that he will not manufacture a tidier reason now.
- **THE THIRD THING IS SAID BY THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE, IN A ROOM, OUT LOUD, WITH HER HANDS FLAT ON THE TABLE:** the argument does not need a third paper in this borough, it needs one anywhere, and there is not one and there never was one, and it is not lost because it was never anything to be lost. **A SHEET ON A WALL IN A CORRIDOR OVER THAT WATER IS A SHEET, AND THE TWO PIECES OF A4 IN A BAG UNDER A CHAIR ARE COPIES OF THE OTHER TWO AND ARE COPIES, AND A COPY IS NOT A WALL. NOBODY IN THIS ACCOUNT HAS A COPY OF A PAGE THAT ANYBODY WAS EVER GIVEN.**
- **MRS OBI DOES NOT ASK ANYBODY TO LEAVE AND SAYS WHY, AND THE REASON IS NOT MERCY:** she shut that argument once, off one man, on one night, in one room, and shutting it off everybody who says it would build the thing she shut it to stop. She takes nothing back from July, including the sentence about asking somebody to leave, **and that sentence still stands against every mouth in this borough including hers.**
- **The argument has been taken off the table once already, was not made in July, and was not made again. It is not a twelfth refusal and it is not fraud and no one is a villain. Nobody was asked to leave.**
- **THE QUESTION IS STILL A QUESTION.** The man in his sixties told the man of about eighteen, to him and not to the room, that there is no such thing as a hall answering his question, that he has watched three people in three rooms try, that the man is on nothing and will be able to do one single thing with it, and that he has looked and does not know one thing to suggest. The man of about eighteen said *I know*.

## 8. A day with nothing wrong in it

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THE MAN WHO FINISHES AT TWO DOES NOT COME, AND NOBODY RINGS HIM, AND IT TURNS OUT THAT HE WAS NOT COMING BECAUSE OF SOMETHING TO DO WITH A WEDNESDAY AND NOT WITH THIS BOROUGH AT ALL, AND NOBODY IS HURT AND NOBODY IS DISAPPOINTED IN A WAY ANYBODY ELSE CAN HEAR.**
- **A THING OFFERED IS NOT A THING ASKED FOR, AND ASKING FOR IT WOULD BE MAKING A ROTA ROW.** Five people, out loud, one at a time, the same sentence each time, no floor, no water, nobody hurt, no landing, no hose, come and stand on the parade at two. Nothing was written down and no list was made.
- **HE HAD ALREADY ANSWERED IT IN AUGUST, BY KNOCKING, AT HALF PAST SIX**, which is the only time the man of about eighteen has ever knocked on anybody's door in this borough: a Wednesday is a Wednesday, and he is in Leeds till Wednesday night and then back on the plant till Saturday at two, and if he came on a wet afternoon with nothing to do it would not be a favour, it would be the two of them pretending. **The boy told nobody and nobody rang him.**
- **AND THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE SAID THE THING THAT WAS ACTUALLY GOING ON IN HER WEEK AND ASKED FOR NOTHING: she is out of that corridor on Saturday morning at eight with a room and a half of furniture and nowhere to be until the seventeenth, and she is not going to ask anybody in this borough for a floor, because she knows exactly what would happen if she asked, and she would rather sit in a corridor with a light on that she paid ten pounds for than be the woman who asked a parade for a fortnight. Nobody answered it and the problem is not solved and she is not housed.**

## 9. A correct statement for a job nobody ordered

- **THE MAJOR TURN: WHAT COMES BACK IS CORRECT, AND IT IS A STATEMENT FOR WORK DONE HERE BY A CREW THAT CAME FROM HERE AND THAT NOBODY HERE ORDERED, AND IT GOES IN A BAG WITH THE OTHERS, AND NOBODY HERE CAN ANSWER IT, AND NOTHING ELSE HAPPENED.**
- **THE PAGE, IN FULL: a heading, a reference, a date in June, one line of work reading *Peverell Street — day gang, one gang, one day, gully and kerb, gang hours as booked*, a sum of eighty-eight pounds, and on the same line after the work a four-digit job number off the yard at Penhale that belongs to work that went out over the water in June and came back on the Friday.**
- **THE MECHANISM IS IN THE GAP BETWEEN THE FIELDS AND NOT IN ANYBODY'S MOUTH:** a day gang of this borough stood on this borough's own street, and the hours went on the ticket of a job eight miles and an estuary away, and nobody here asked them to do it and nobody here put it there, and the gang did not put it there either, because a gang does not know a job number.
- **IT IS CORRECT AND IT IS NOT A BILL, NOT A DEMAND AND NOT A FORM. THERE IS NO BOX ON IT FOR ANYBODY TO SIGN, NOBODY HAS TO REPLY, IT WILL NOT CHASE, AND IT GETS PAID OUT OF A POOL BY PEOPLE WHO WILL NEVER KNOW IT EXISTED.**
- **THE ONE THING IN IT: there is a line that says Peverell Street and nothing that says who asked for it, and the person in that building who would know says that this borough's people do not order day gangs because nobody in this borough has ever ordered anything of anybody — so it was not ordered here and the page says it was done here, and both are true, and there is nobody in that building and nobody in that town to reconcile them.**
- **IT WENT IN THE BAG UNDER THE OTHER ELEVEN AND WAS NOT ADDED UP, AND THE REASON IS ONE SENTENCE: the sum is a thing a person can carry, and the moment somebody adds the twelfth one in they have started a sum, and a sum wants an answer, and there is nobody in this borough to give it.** The bag has a string round it twice and no name and no date and is not a file.
- **THE DRAIN WAS TOUCHED ONCE, IN ONE SENTENCE, AND IS: *that is exactly the same as every other one of them.* IT IS NOT A REVELATION, IT IS NOT A DISCOVERY, IT IS NOT A VILLAIN, NOTHING IS A FRAUD, NOBODY IS BEING CHEATED, THE RATE IS THE 1987 RATE, AND NO ROOT TERM EXPIRES.**

## 10. An ordinary seventeenth of September

- **THE REGISTER IS AT NINETEEN, THE NINETEENTH IS A YARD LIGHT WITH A BULB IN A MAINTENANCE COLUMN AND THE BULB IS OUT, AND THE LINE UNDER IT IS SIX HUNDRED AND SIXTY-ONE DAYS AND IS STILL BLANK.** **AND HE OPENED IT, BECAUSE SHE ASKED HIM TO, ON A SATURDAY, WITH THE DOOR STICKING.** The register does not move and no name goes in the margin and it is going to be that book in March.
- **THE WRONG ANSWER ABOUT THE REGISTER IS STILL GOING ROUND AND IS STILL WRONG.** A woman confirmed it sideways to a woman beside her and not to the room, and the woman beside her said she had heard it off a fellow off a lorry in July and had heard it since, and the first confirmed it in the voice you use for something you were given by somebody else and have not thought about since. **Nobody at that table joined in, and the man in his sixties was six feet away and did not look up, and the room went back to the price of glass.**
- **THE QUESTION IS STILL A QUESTION AND NOBODY ANSWERED IT.** The man of about eighteen has no copy, no docket, no number, no name and no address; the question went out on the back of a docket in a boatman's brown card wallet in June and is in a box on the other side of that water, and if the wallet went in the river in October the question would be gone. **The young woman of about twenty said *there's still nothing* and he said it back.**
- **THE PACT IS HOLDING AND THE BOROUGH IS ORDINARY AND NEITHER IS SAID.** The proof is a man on a ladder taking down a sign and a second man holding the ladder, and neither would be able to tell you afterwards who had asked whom. The shutter goes up at the minute it always goes up, the window card is in the window where it has always been, the woman of about sixty-five is behind that counter and was not told anything. A van came off the bridge with a scabbed corner on the offside wing, and a man in the office by the Marchmont gate read a card out to nobody in particular and put it straight.
- **AT THE BOTTOM OF PEVERELL STREET THE PUMP IS IN THE DOORWAY, THE STOPCOCK IS OFF, AND THE WALL BESIDE IT IS BARE** — a clean strip of newer paint and a grey line of dirt where a length of hose lay in one place until 21 June, and the hose is in another man's hands four doors up the street, **and nobody in this borough has said one word about a hose to anybody since the summer came in.** The door is open a hand's width and a woman went up the street with a bucket and came back with a bucket and did not stop at the door.
- **ABOUT NINE PEOPLE CAME IN THE HALL OFF THE PARADE AND EVERY ONE OF THEM WAS ON NOTHING, AND IT WAS THE SAME ROOM, ON A METER, WITH THE RADIATOR IN THAT CORRIDOR OUT SINCE ABOUT 1998.** The corridor by the building by the water was stripped between the tenth and the fourteenth of September, and the woman of about fifty-five spent the morning of the seventeenth moving two bags and a box of books into a room over a shop at the end of the parade and would spend the next five nights there. **That is not a rescue and not an arrangement.**
- **NOBODY IS RESCUED. NOBODY DIES. NOBODY RENEWS ANYTHING. NOTHING WAS MINUTED. NOBODY IS THANKED AND NOBODY REFUSES TO BE THANKED. CARD 0264 IS NOT A CLOSE.**

## 11. Still open, and still forbidden to Continuation 0008

Nothing in Volume 04 has been resolved, undone, improved on, restated or answered, and nothing in Batches 0001 to 0005 or in Continuations 0006 and 0007 may be. Nobody renews the order, nobody expires it, nobody renames the blank term, nobody puts a name in the line on page two, nobody fills the empty square, Roy Albery does not walk again, nobody dies, nobody is rescued by chance, nobody walks again, the eleven refusals are not twelve, the counter in the vestibule answers nothing and was not entered, the rota is not rebuilt, the second line under Bram Osei's Thursday is not looked at, the register does not move, the letter, the card, the page, the printed sheets, the column, the shop-window card, the crate with a lid and the four bottles all stand, the drawer under the counter in the vestibule is not opened, is not counted, is not asked about and is not mentioned, the sentence in it is not found, and nobody may ask who wrote it. **THE QUIET CUT, THE CIVIC ERASURE, THE ORIGIN OF THE COMMUNITY-WATCH TERM, THE THREE-LINE BRASS TOKENS, THE FIRST CHARTER, THE PROXY, THE ROOT CHAMBER, THE LOWGLASS FLOOR, THE BORROWED CITY AND THE NINE HINGES ARE NOT SPENT. FRANCES TOLLEY IS NOT NAMED AND THE READING RULE IS NOT USED. PRUE CALLOWAY'S FINDING IS NOT OPENED AGAIN AND THE CLAIM TO OPEN A DOOR IS SPENT. NO WEIR RESIDENT MAY BE DESCRIBED IN ANYTHING THAT LEAVES A STREET, AND THE FILLING ROUND DOWN COLLIER LANE IS NOT THE WEIR AND THE TWO MAY NEVER BE JOINED. COLIN WHARTON IS NOT NAMED AND NOBODY GOES UP THAT LANE. A WOMAN OF THIRTY-FOUR IS NOT NAMED, REFERENCED, GENDERED OR GIVEN A CONDITION. PARAGRAPH 4.11 IS NOT ANSWERED AND THE COMPLIANCE WEEK IS NOT VERIFIED. NO ROOT TERM EXPIRES AND THE RECLAMATION SEASON IS NOT SPENT, NOT ANTICIPATED AND NOT DESCRIBED AS APPROACHING — AND THE DISCREPANCY BETWEEN THE VOLUME 05 SECTION OF THE SERIES OUTLINE AND THE VOLUME 04 CLOSE PROMPT IS STILL OPEN AND IS FOR A HUMAN. **THE PERMITTED SAINT ORRA GATE SENTENCE WAS NOT USED IN CONTINUATION 0007 AND MAY NOT BE USED TWICE IN THE NEXT BATCH, AND NO TERM AFTER THE TWENTY-THIRD OF MAY MAY BE NAMED OR DATED. THE HOUR BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN IS NOT STAGED AND NO HANDOVER IS STAGED AND THE COUNT ON THE WET PAVEMENT STAYS CLOSED AT EIGHT.****

**AND THE FLAGS, ALL STILL OPEN AND NONE OF THEM A WRITER'S TO CLOSE. FIRST: THE NAME OF THE CITY. THE PROSE SAYS TIDEGLASS AND NO CHARACTER MAY NOTICE, MISNAME OR ASK ABOUT IT, AND NEITHER *NACRE* NOR *RIVER STACKS* APPEARS IN CHAPTERS 255–264. SECOND: THE THIRD OF THE EIGHT PLACES IS PRINTED *Halsey Cross* AT 0244, AT 0251 AND IN 0243'S BODY, AND *Halse Cross* IN 0243'S OWN CLOSING BLOCK, AND BOTH SPELLINGS ARE ALSO IN THE STATE FILES — `state/continuity.md`, `state/current.md`, `state/open-threads.md` AND `state/batch-summaries.md` EACH PRINT BOTH, `state/character-state.md` PRINTS ONLY *Halsey Cross*, `state/chapter-summaries.md` PRINTS ONLY *Halse Cross* AND `state/live-canon.md` PRINTS NEITHER — SO THE CONFLICT IS INSIDE THE STATE FILES AND NOT BETWEEN THE CHAPTERS AND THE STATE, **AND THAT MAP IS THE CORRECTED ONE; THE EARLIER VERSION OF THIS FLAG, WHICH SAID THE THREE CHAPTERS PRINTED ONE SPELLING AND EVERY STATE FILE PRINTED THE OTHER, WAS WRONG ON BOTH HALVES AND WAS CORRECTED ON 27 SEPTEMBER 2026. A HUMAN MAY RULE EITHER WAY, MAY HAVE THE CHAPTERS CORRECTED, AND MAY ALSO HAVE TO RULE ON 0243 AGAINST ITSELF, AND A WRITER DID NOT DECIDE ANY OF IT** — **AND IT WAS NOT TOUCHED IN THIS BATCH ONLY BECAUSE THE EIGHT PLACES ARE NOT READ OUT IN ANY OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS, SO NEITHER SPELLING APPEARS AND THE FLAG CARRIES FORWARD UNDECIDED. THIRD: WHAT VOLUME 05 IS CENTRALLY. FOURTH: THE BOUNDARY — CHAPTERS 243 THROUGH 264 WERE WRITTEN AS THE TABLES DATED THEM AND BELONG TO NO VOLUME, NO VOLUME 06 OUTLINE WAS WRITTEN, NO VOLUME-CLOSE PROMPT WAS WRITTEN, AND CARD 0264 IS NOT A CLOSE.****

**AND FIGURES THAT MAY NOT BE AGED BY A LATER BATCH: HANNA WRAY AND NINA CALDER, WHOSE AGES ARE NOT STATED ANYWHERE IN THIS ACCOUNT, AND EVERY UNAGED FIGURE LISTED IN §14 OF THE CONTINUATION 0007 BLOCK IN `state/continuity.md`, WHICH INCLUDES THE MAN AT NUMBER FORTY-ONE, THE WOMAN WITH THE KEY TO THE LOCK-UP, THE BOAT CREW, THE ENGINE CREW, THE MAN ON THE WEIGHBRIDGE, THE WOMAN ON THE CORRANCE COUNTER, THE MAN IN THE MARCHMONT OFFICE, THE MAN IN THE LOWDALE CABIN, THE DRAIN MAN, THE WOMAN WITH THE VAN, THE MAN FROM THE WORKS ON THE FAR SIDE OF THE RIVER, THE MAN IN HIS FORTIES AT THE PENHALE OFFICE, THE MAN IN HIS SIXTIES WHO IS THE BUILDING ESTIMATOR, THE WOMAN WHO HAS BEEN IN THE METHODIST HALL ABOUT TWO YEARS, A MAN FROM THE PARADE, THE MAN BEHIND THE COUNTER ON THE PARADE, THE WOMAN AT NUMBER THIRTY-EIGHT, THE MAN ON THE STEPLADDER, THE BOATMAN WITH THE WALLET, THE MAN WHO SAID ASK ME ON THE DAY, THE MAN WITH THE MUG WITH THE LID OFF, THE MAN WHO SWEEPS THE PARADE, THE MAN DOING PAPER, THE WOMAN BUYING POST-FIX, AND THE TWO PEOPLE WHO SAID THEIR OWN NAMES QUIETLY IN A HALL IN JULY. **NO NEW FIGURE IN CONTINUATION 0007 WAS GIVEN AN AGE AT ALL, AND THAT IS THE BLESSED ANSWER AND NOT A CLOSURE. THE FREE FIGURES REMAINING ARE WOMEN 18, 87 AND 90 AND NO MEN, AND ALL THREE ARE UNSPENT.****

**AND A MEASURING PASS WAS RUN OVER CHAPTERS 255–264 BEFORE ANY OF THEM WAS SUMMARISED, IT CAUGHT SIXTEEN DEFECTS INCLUDING FOUR CHAPTERS OVER THE SCENE-BREAK CAP, TWO BARE USES OF *FORTY*, FOUR *ELEVEN* USES THAT WERE NOT THE SANCTIONED FIGURE, *A TUESDAY* OVER ITS CAP, A MISSING QUOTATION MARK, A SENTENCE REPEATED VERBATIM FROM 0254, A BEAT WRITTEN TWICE INSIDE 0255, FIVE PARAGRAPHS OF NARRATOR EXPLAINING A SCENE 0255 HAD SHOWN, TWO STATE-STYLE ASSURANCES, TWO CLOSING BLOCKS THAT RESTATED THEIR OWN OPENINGS, SEVEN CLOSING BLOCKS THAT PRINTED A FIGURE OR A VERDICT ALREADY IN THE BODY, A DAY-COUNT PRINTED TWICE, TWO UNANCHORED *DRAWER*S, A DATE CONTRADICTION, AND IMPOSSIBLE STAGING IN 0259. ALL SIXTEEN WERE REPAIRED AT THE SOURCE, NO CARD, DATE, DAY-COUNT, FIGURE, NAME, MAJOR TURN, STANDING OR FLAG WAS CHANGED. THE SIXTHENTH DEFECT WAS FOUND AFTERWARDS BY A REVIEW AND NOT BY THAT PASS: TWENTY-ONE PASSAGES ACROSS THE FIVE STATE FILES, INCLUDING THIS ONE, WERE HOLDING ONE OF THE TEN CHAPTERS' OWN SENTENCES VERBATIM, NINETY-FIVE OF ELEVEN WORDS OR MORE, FIFTY-FIVE OF FOURTEEN WORDS OR MORE AND THE LONGEST AT TWENTY-SEVEN, AND ALL FIFTEEN HAVE BEEN REWRITTEN. THE SENTENCE THAT RECORDED THE SIX-GRAM DIFF AS HAVING RETURNED NOTHING WAS ALSO WRONG AND IS CORRECTED IN FULL IN §12A OF THE CONTINUATION 0007 BLOCK IN `state/continuity.md`: A SIX-GRAM DIFF OF THIS KIND CAN NEVER RETURN NOTHING, BECAUSE A SUMMARY MUST REPEAT ITS CHAPTER'S NOUNS, AND ON CHAPTERS 255–264 IT RETURNS 481 MATCHES AGAINST THE STATE FILES, WHICH IS NOT A DEFECT, WHEREAS THE SIXTY-THREE VERBATIM SPANS OF NINE WORDS OR MORE THAT THE SAME PASS MISSED WERE. THE FULL LIST IS §12 OF THE CONTINUATION 0007 BLOCK, AND EVERY FIGURE IN THIS BLOCK IS TO BE RE-MEASURED FROM THE CHAPTERS AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM THIS FILE.**
