# VOLUME 05 OPEN THREADS — after Continuation 0008, Chapter 274

**Read with the "VOLUME 05, CONTINUATION 0008 — CHAPTERS 265–274" block at the end of `state/continuity.md`, which carries the canon for these ten chapters and supersedes this block wherever the two differ, and with the "VOLUME 05, CONTINUATION 0007 — CHAPTERS 255–264" block above it, which still governs everything those ten chapters did. **VOLUME 05 IS NOT CLOSED AND IS NOT DECLARED CLOSED. THE SERIES IS AT 274 OF 720. NOTHING IN CONTINUATION 0008 RESOLVED ANYTHING AND NOTHING IN IT MAY BE UNDONE, IMPROVED ON, RESTATED OR ANSWERED BY VOLUME 05. EVERY DAY-COUNT BELOW IS ADDED UP FROM ITS ANCHOR ON THE DAY IN QUESTION AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM A SUMMARY: at Tuesday 15 November 2016 the blank line under the nineteenth is seven hundred and twenty days, the card in the slot is six hundred and thirty-four, and the letter in the tray is six hundred and thirty-six.****

## 1. A second day with nothing wrong in it

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THE SECOND DAY IS SMALLER THAN THE FIRST AND IT IS ALSO BETTER, AND THE ONLY REASON IT IS BETTER IS THAT NOBODY SAID THEY COULD COME, AND IT ARRIVES IN ONE SENTENCE IN A YOUNG WOMAN'S MOUTH: *Nobody said we can.* THAT'S IT, THAT'S ALL OF IT, THAT'S THE WHOLE THING I HAD.** The first was five people in rain on a parade for an hour and a half who mostly did not know who else was coming. This one is two people and a bus and a walk to the end of the parade and back.
- **THE BOY IS ON THE DISHES IN THE KITCHEN AT THE BACK OF A PARADE AND IS NOT RUNNING ANYTHING AND NOBODY ASKED HIM TO.** He was told nothing and told nobody, and the woman he works for went home at six and left him the rest of it, and there are about six things in the sink and the hot water goes off at eight because a timer on the machine has never been reset.
- **THE TWO ARE THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE, WHO HAS A ROOM OVER A SHOP UNTIL MONDAY, AND THE YOUNG WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY, OFF THE COLLEGE BUS.** They go out separately without having told each other, meet at the top of the parade by accident, walk down it and back, and tell nobody.
- **THE REASON GIVEN IS THAT IF ANYBODY IS TOLD THEN IT IS A THING, AND THEN SOMEBODY DOES IT TWICE, AND TWO TIMES IS A HABIT, AND THEN SOMEBODY ASKS HOW MANY OF THEM THERE WERE, AND THERE ISN'T A NUMBER. THERE IS NOBODY'S LIST, NO FORM OFF ANYBODY'S COUNTER, AND NOBODY WHO HAS TO DO ANY SINGLE THING ABOUT IT.**
- **IT IS NOT A ROTA ROW AND NOT A COMPACT AND NOT AN ANSWER TO ANYTHING, AND THE FIRST DAY'S FIGURE WAS FIVE AND THIS ONE'S IS TWO. NOBODY IS HURT. NOBODY IS TOLD SHE MAY COME. NOTHING IS BUILT, AND THE CHAPTER IS DATED THE SAME DAY AS 0264 AND RUNS FROM THE EVENING OF IT, AND NOTHING IN EITHER CHAPTER IS SHOWN TWICE.**

## 2. A step, two men, and a word nobody can explain

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THEY FINISH IT, AND THE ONLY THING THAT SURVIVES THE MORNING IS A WORD ONE OF THEM USED THAT THE OTHER HAS NOT HEARD BEFORE AND CANNOT EXPLAIN AND IS NOT ASKED TO. THE WORD IS *ARRIS*, AND IT IS ABOUT THE FRONT CORNER OF A COURSE OF STONE THAT HAS GONE SOFT.** The man in his sixties says the word back twice, cannot put it to anything at all, says *I've no idea, he said it* when a woman asks him what one is, and does not ask again. The man in his thirties does not explain it and is not asked to.
- **THE JOB IS A CRACKED SLAB ON THE FRONT STEP OF THE METHODIST HALL ON CARROW LANE, AND THE DISAGREEMENT IS ONLY ABOUT THE JOB: take it all up and relay it, which needs a number and a book and a good few hundred pounds, or cut it back and re-bed the top course, which holds for thirty years and has no number on it.**
- **NEITHER MAN WILL PUT ANYTHING IN WRITING. ONE OF THEM WILL NOT DISCUSS IT IN A SHOP, AND THE REASON IS ABOUT A SHOP FLOOR AND NOT A MORAL POSITION: a barrow can go out of a shop in front of anybody and a job has a name on it.**
- **THE MAN IN HIS SIXTIES ASKED FOR ONE THING TO BE SAID OUT LOUD IN A ROOM SOMEBODY HEARS, WHICH WAS THAT HE WAS NOT ASKING THE OTHER MAN TO DO IT, AND THE OTHER LET HIM SAY IT. THAT IS THE WHOLE OF THE ACCOMMODATION ANYBODY MADE.**
- **THE MAN WHO FIRST SAID ANYTHING ABOUT THE STEP SAID IT IN A DOORWAY ON THE PARADE IN SEPTEMBER. HE WAS ABSENT ON THE SATURDAY AND NOBODY SOUGHT HIM OUT, AND IT WENT UNWRITTEN THEN AS WELL.**
- **THE SLAB STAYS AGAINST THE WALL BY THE DOOR UNDER A COAT UNTIL WEDNESDAY BECAUSE THE HALL'S DOORS OPEN ON A WEDNESDAY MEETING, AND THE MAN IN HIS THIRTIES SAYS HE WILL COME BACK ON WEDNESDAY, WHICH MAKES IT A THING HE IS DOING ON A WEDNESDAY AND NOT A THING HE DID ON A SATURDAY.** The disagreement was never turned into a disagreement about the order, the pact or a right of return, and no duty was used as a reason why a crew may go.

## 3. A tin of paint with a name on the lid

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THE OBJECT WAS HANDLED BY SOMEBODY WHO DID NOT KNOW WHAT IT WAS, AND THAT IS ALL, AND IT IS NOT A DISCOVERY AND NOT A CONSPIRACY AND NOBODY IS CHEATED AND NO PERPETRATOR EXISTS.** A part-used tin in the top drawer of a kitchen unit in a flat on a street off Corrance Lane, with a surname and an initial chinagraphed on a strip of masking tape folded under the rim of the lid. It came back on the Friday with a thumb's worth gone out of it and a dent in the surface of the paint.
- **THE SURNAME ON THE TAPE IS NEVER READ ALOUD IN THE CHAPTER, BY ANYBODY, AND NOBODY GOES LOOKING FOR ANYBODY, AND NOBODY IS FOUND OUT BY BEING NAMED.**
- **AND THE THREE NAMES ARE IN THREE PLACES AND ARE NEVER PUT IN ONE SENTENCE OR ONE LIST, WHICH IS THE POINT OF THE CHAPTER: the surname on the tape, in a kitchen · a man's name in the back of a stock book in the lock-up behind the counter of the shop that shuts early, put there in July by a man who left a bag of leads and never came back · a list of eleven names with dates and places and sums on it in a book at the back of a room at the end of a corridor on the first floor of a building by the water, which nobody has had out since the summer and which one person knows about because a woman read the outside of it out loud once on the step of that building.** None of the three is resolved, none is chased, and a name is not a person and a person is not a name.
- **THE MAN WHO DID ODD JOBS READ A NAME BECAUSE OF THE INITIAL AT THE FRONT OF IT, DID NOT KNOW WHAT THE TIN WAS, USED A BIT ON A GATEPOST BARE SINCE APRIL BECAUSE THERE WAS NOTHING ELSE TO HAND, PUT THE LID BACK ON, AND SAID SO.**
- **IT WAS FOLLOWED FOR ONE WEEK AND NOT FOR A FORTNIGHT, AND THE POINT OF THE CARD'S FORTNIGHT WAS THE CEILING AND NOT THE LENGTH.**

## 4. An ordinary weekday in October

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THE WEEK IS ORDINARY AND SOMEBODY IN IT SAYS, UNPROMPTED, THAT IT IS THE FIRST ONE SHE CAN REMEMBER BEING BORED IN, AND NOBODY DISAGREES, AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF THE CHAPTER.** Said by the woman of about forty-two with the key to the hall off the parade, in the doorway, on her way to the meter, and she gives her own reason, which is that she has had the key nine years. The man from the parade says it is not a lot of a thing, comfortably, without looking up.
- **WHAT THE WEEK WAS MADE OF, SHOWN AND NOT SUMMED: a gang of four off a firm on the parade went out on the Monday to a house in Corrance Lane for a chimney, a flue and a stretch of wall, and came back on the Thursday, and their hours went on a sheet into a folder in a van and out of that folder on the other side of that water, and nobody in this borough has ever seen that sheet · a van came back midweek with no near mirror on it and a roll of tape on the dash · a firm of heating engineers put twenty pounds on the figure for coming out and left a line about a level of service under it and no name on it anywhere · the hall off the parade is opened on a Friday afternoon now, because it is on a meter and warm, and about five people are in it and nobody is asked anything when they come in or when they go.**
- **THEIR FOREMAN IS A MAN WHO IS NOT ON ANYTHING AND WAITED IN A FRONT ROOM FOR A MAN TO COME AND SIGN A SHEET AND SAID NOTHING ABOUT THE WEEK AND WAS ASKED NOTHING ABOUT THE WEEK AND THEN DROVE THE VAN HOME WITH A BROKEN NEARSIDE MIRROR. THE WOMAN WHO DOES THE BOOKS AT THAT FIRM IS NOT ON ANYTHING EITHER AND IS THE ONLY PERSON IN THE FIRM WHO KNEW WHAT THE FOUR MEN HAD DONE ALL WEEK, AND SHE PUT THE SHEET INTO A SPREADSHEET AND SHUT THE LAPTOP. THERE IS NO LIST ANYWHERE IN THIS BOROUGH WITH A NAME ON IT.**
- **NOTHING IN THE WEEK IS A FINDING AND NO NARRATOR SAYS THAT ANYTHING IN IT IS A FINDING, AND THE HALL, THE PRICE, THE VAN AND THE CREW ARE NOT A TWELFTH OF ANYTHING. A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED, AND A VAN THAT CAME BACK IS NOT A RESCUE.**

## 5. A better wrong answer about what will be proved

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THE NEW WRONG ANSWER IS BETTER THAN THE OLD ONE AND EVERYONE IN THE ROOM PREFERS IT, AND THE FINDING IS THAT A BOROUGH CAN BE GIVEN A BETTER WRONG ANSWER AND WILL TAKE IT, AND THE MAN WHO PUT THE FIRST ONE IN CANNOT FIX IT AND WAS NOT ASKED TO.**
- **THE NEW ANSWER IS NOT A PAPER. IT IS THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF JUNE ITSELF**, given by the young woman of about twenty, who is the only person in that room who was in a room in April. Her reason, and the only reason in the room, is that a date is the only thing this borough has ever agreed on, and a date cannot be lost where a docket can, and there is no paper in this borough anybody will be able to lay a hand on in ten years' time.
- **AND THE DATE IS WRONG ON PURPOSE AND MAY NOT BE CORRECTED BY ANYBODY. ON THE PAGE THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF JUNE IS ONE MAN AND A BATH OUT OF A SECOND-FLOOR LANDING IN MARSH LANE (0252, 0257) AND THE FIVE PEOPLE IN THE RAIN FOR AN HOUR AND A HALF IS THE SEVENTH OF SEPTEMBER (0262), WHICH THE SAME YOUNG WOMAN PUTS ON THE PAGE HERSELF IN 0265 — *ON THE SEVENTH, THERE WAS FIVE OF US STOOD IN THE RAIN.* THE COUNT OF FIVE IS CANON AND IS CORRECT; THE DATE IS NOT, AND THE CHAPTER SAYS OUT LOUD THAT THE ANSWER IS NOT BETTER *BECAUSE IT'S MORE TRUE*, AND IN 0274 IT COMES OUT OF A TABLE AS *THAT'S WHAT THEY'RE ALL SAYING*. **A BOROUGH HAS GIVEN A BETTER WRONG ANSWER A DATE THAT A ROOM AGREED WITH, AND NOBODY WHO AGREED WAS THERE, AND NOBODY MAY CORRECT IT, EXPLAIN IT OR IMPROVE ON IT.** A REVIEW ON 27 SEPTEMBER 2026 RAISED THIS AS A POSSIBLE ERROR AND IT IS NOT ONE; IT IS THE BATCH'S OWN SUBJECT.
- **IT IS TAKEN IN ABOUT ELEVEN SECONDS BY PEOPLE WHO HAVE NEVER HEARD THE OTHER ANSWER. THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE SAYS OUT LOUD THAT IT IS A GREAT DEAL BETTER THAN THE REGISTER, AND A WOMAN WHO WORKS IN A SHOP AGREES, AND THE MAN IN HIS FORTIES SAYS HE THOUGHT IT DID, AND A MAN OFF A LATE AGREES WITH IT.**
- **THE MAN IN HIS SIXTIES IS NOT IN THAT ROOM, DID NOT ARRANGE IT, IS NOT TOLD, MUST NOT BE TOLD, AND THE CHAPTER SAYS SO IN HER MOUTH: HE TOLD A SHOPKEEPER ON THE THIRTIETH OF JULY THAT HE COULD NOT GET THE TWO HALVES OF HIS OWN ANSWER APART, HE WAS STANDING IN A STREET WHEN HE SAID IT, AND NOT ONE OF THAT ROOM WAS IN THAT STREET, AND HE HAS NOT BEEN TOLD THIS AND IS NEVER GOING TO BE, AND THAT IS THE CORRECT OUTCOME.**
- **NOBODY IN THE ROOM CAN CORRECT IT AND NOBODY IS ASKED TO, AND A WRONG ANSWER LEFT IN A ROOM BY SOMEBODY WHO HAS NOT BEEN TOLD IT IS WRONG IS NOT A NEW WRONG ANSWER AND NOT A SECOND ONE OF THE SAME.**
- **AND A MAN WITH A CLIPBOARD STOOD IN THE DOOR AT TWENTY TO NINE WITH A SMALL JOB AND A PRICE, AND THE YOUNG WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY WENT DOWN AND TOLD HIM THE TRUTH, AND HE SAID HE WOULD COME BACK AND DID NOT COME BACK, AND ABOUT THREE WEEKS LATER HE TOLD A MAN HE WAS WORKING FOR THAT HE HAD TRIED A ROOM AT THE END OF A PARADE AND BEEN TURNED AWAY, AND THE MAN SAID THEY WON'T, AND NEITHER SAID ANOTHER WORD.**

## 6. A water bill that went up for a takeover

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THE PRICE GOES UP FOR A REASON THAT HAS NOTHING TO DO WITH ANYTHING DONE IN THIS BOROUGH, AND SHE SAYS THE SENTENCE THAT IS THE WHOLE OF THE CHAPTER, AND SHE SAYS IT TO HER OWN KITCHEN TABLE, AND ONE MAN HEARS IT: *That's nowt to do with us and it's still got my name on it.*** She is the woman in the second house of Marsh Lane, who has been right once, and who says out loud that she would rather be wrong this time.
- **THE REASON IS A TAKEOVER, WHICH IS CORRECT AND IS NOT FRAUD: FOUR COUNTIES OVER THE WATER ON ONE LETTERHEAD SINCE ABOUT JUNE, FIGURES UP ONE AT A TIME, A PRINTED LINE ON THE BILL THAT WAS NOT ON THE LAST ONE, A CROSS REFERENCE TO A PAGE, AND A FREE TELEPHONE NUMBER AT THE FOOT FOR ANYBODY WHO WISHES TO QUERY IT. A WOMAN IN SARN HAD THE SAME LETTER IN AUGUST AND BROUGHT IT ROUND TO SHOW A TAP FITTER BECAUSE SHE HAD NOBODY ELSE TO SHOW IT TO.**
- **AND SHE ASKS, AND IS TOLD, AND BELIEVES, THAT THE TRADE RATE ON A JOB OUT OF THE PENHALE YARD HAS NOT MOVED SINCE ABOUT NINETEEN EIGHTY-SEVEN AND IS NOT GOING TO, AND THAT IT IS A DIFFERENT SHEET OF PAPER WITH A DIFFERENT NAME ON IT AND NOTHING TO DO WITH HER.**
- **AT THE END SHE ASKS WHETHER SHE SHOULD RING THE NUMBER AND IS ASKED WHO SHE WOULD RING, AND SAYS SHE DOES NOT KNOW, AND SAYS THAT IN FIVE MONTHS THE TELEPHONE IN THAT KITCHEN HAS HAD ONE CALL ON IT AND SHE SAID NO TO THAT ONE AS WELL. THE BILL IS ON THE TABLE WHEN HE LEAVES AND IT IS ON THE TABLE AT TWO AND IT IS ON THE TABLE AT SIX AND IT DID NOT GO INTO THE FOLDER WITH THE OTHERS.**
- **THE DRAIN IS TOUCHED ONCE, IN ONE SENTENCE, BY HER: A MAN UP THIS STREET WHO DID THE DRAINS HAS ABOUT THE LEGS HE HAD IN FEBRUARY AND IS NOT GETTING THEM BACK, AND BACK WOULD BE FOUR, AND HE KNOWS IT AND SO DOES EVERYBODY ON THAT STREET, AND THERE WAS NOBODY TO RING ABOUT THAT EITHER. IT IS NOT ELABORATED ON. NOBODY IN THIS BOROUGH IS A HERO AND NOBODY IS A VILLAIN, THE FOUR COMPANIES ARE CORRECT, THE RATE HAS NOT MOVED, AND NONE OF IT IS FRAUD.**

## 7. Two people in one shop on one afternoon in August

- **THE MAJOR TURN: SOMEBODY DISCOVERS THAT TWO OF THE PEOPLE IN THE PARADE DID SOMETHING ON THE SAME DAY IN AUGUST AND NEVER TOLD EACH OTHER OR ANYBODY, AND THE DISCOVERY IS MADE BY ACCIDENT, IN A QUEUE, OUT OF THE MOUTH OF A THIRD PERSON WHO WAS TOLD BY ONE OF THEM AND IS NOT ON ANYTHING. **THE TWO ARE THE MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTEEN AND THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE, THE DAY IS THURSDAY THE TWENTY-FIFTH OF AUGUST, AND THE SENTENCE THEY SAID IN THE SAME SHOP AN HOUR APART IS *I HAVEN'T GOT ANYBODY*.****
- **WHAT EACH WAS GIVEN OFF A SHELF WAS **NOTHING. NOTHING WAS GIVEN, AND THE STATE FILES SAID OTHERWISE FOR ONE BATCH AND WERE WRONG, AND THE CHAPTER GOVERNS.** ON THE PAGE IN 0271 THE WOMAN WHO RUNS THE SHOP THAT SHUTS EARLY SAYS THE HASP, THE STAPLE AND THE SCREW EYE ARE IN A BAG IN THE BACK AND HAVE BEEN SINCE ABOUT THE SUMMER, THAT SHE HAS NOT PUT THEM IN BECAUSE THAT IS NOT HER DOOR, THAT SHE IS NOT GIVING THEM AWAY, AND THAT THE YOUNG WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY IS TO TAKE THEM OFF A SHELF IN A SHOP THAT SHUTS AT ONE AND PAY FOR A BAG OF POST-FIX WITH HER OWN MONEY — AND THE YOUNG WOMAN PAYS AND GOES OUT AND THE SHUTTER COMES DOWN BEHIND HER AT TWENTY TO FOUR. **THE ACCOUNT THAT WENT ROUND IS THE MAN BEHIND THE COUNTER ON THE PARADE'S, TOLD IN A QUEUE THE SAME AFTERNOON AND REPEATED IN THE KITCHEN THAT NIGHT BY BOTH THE YOUNG WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY AND THE MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTEEN, AND WHAT IS TRUE IN IT IS THAT SOMETHING WENT OFF A SHELF IN THAT SHOP ON THE TWENTY-FIFTH OF AUGUST AND WHAT IS NOT TRUE IS THAT ANYBODY WAS GIVEN ANYTHING FOR NOTHING. THE TORCH IN THAT ACCOUNT IS NO EVIDENCE OF A GIFT EITHER: IN 0260 THE MAN BEHIND THE TILL COUNTED FOUR LAMPS OUT OF A BOX BEHIND THE TILL AND SAID THEY'D WANT THE MONEY, AND THE MONEY FOR THE FOUR WAS TAKEN ON THE FRIDAY. THE ACCOUNT IS NOT CORRECTED BY ANYBODY IN ANY CHAPTER AND IS NOT TO BE CORRECTED BY A LATER ONE; IT IS WHAT WENT ROUND, AND SHE TOLD ONE MAN FOR NO REASON AT ALL, AND HE TOLD A QUEUE.**
- **THE DISCOVERER IS THE YOUNG WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY, WHO HAD BEEN INTO THAT SHOP EARLIER THE SAME AFTERNOON AND ASKED ABOUT THE DOOR OF A ROOM OVER A SHOP **BECAUSE THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE HAD SAID, ON THE THURSDAY BEFORE LAST, IN A ROOM OVER A SHOP THAT IS NOT HERS, THAT THE DOOR OF THE ROOM SHE HAD TAKEN DOES NOT SHUT** — IT IS HER OWN DOOR AND NOT THE DOOR OF THE MAN IN HIS THIRTIES WHOSE ROOM SHE WAS SITTING IN, AND A CHAPTER NOW SAYS SO — AND DID NOT SAY WHY SHE WAS ASKING, AND WAS TOLD, CORRECTLY, THAT IT WAS NOT THE SHOPKEEPER'S DOOR.**
- **THE MAN OF ABOUT EIGHTEEN HAS NOT ASKED ABOUT IT AND WILL NOT, AND SAYS THE THREE THINGS THAT ARE TRUE ABOUT IT, WHICH ARE THAT THERE WAS NO ARRANGEMENT, NO LIST AND NOBODY WHO THOUGHT IT UP, AND THAT HE IS NOT GOING TO TELL THE MAN WHO WAS TOLD, BECAUSE THE MAN WOULD HAVE TO SAY SOMETHING BACK AND HAS NOT GOT ANYTHING TO SAY BACK ABOUT A HASP AND A STAPLE.**
- **HE NAMES THE THREE THINGS THAT WENT NOWHERE IN THE SAME BREATH AND MAKES NOTHING OF ANY OF THEM: A LAMP IN A CORRIDOR SHE PAID HIM TEN POUNDS FOR IN AUGUST AND HE TOLD NOBODY · FOUR BOXES UP TWO FLIGHTS OF A NARROW STAIRCASE ON A BARROW WALKED OUT OF A SHOP IN DAYLIGHT · A CHAIN ON A GATE AT THE BOTTOM OF A STREET THAT NOBODY HAS WALKED DOWN TO LOOK AT. NOTHING IN THIS CHAPTER SAYS WHAT THE CHAIN IS FOR.**
- **NOBODY GOES AND TELLS ANYBODY, NOBODY GOES AND THANKS ANYBODY, NOTHING IS ARRANGED, NOTHING IS A STRUCTURE AND NOTHING IS A PRECEDENT, AND THE FACT THAT IT HAPPENED IS NOT A STRUCTURE. A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED, AND HE IS STILL ON THE DISHES AND IS WRITTEN ON NOWHERE.**

## 8. A hundred and eighty pounds and a boiler nobody owns

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THE ROOM WORKS OUT WHO OWNS IT AND FINDS THAT NOBODY OWNS IT, AND SAYS SO, AND THE SAYING IS NOT A CLAIM AND NOT A REFUSAL AND NOT AN OFFER, AND SOMEBODY CARRIES IT HOME ON A TROLLEY AND IT IS NOT ARRANGED. **THE OBJECT IS A BOXED BOILER IN THE METER-HIGH CUPBOARD UNDER THE STAIRS OF THE HALL OFF THE PARADE, BOXED AND WRAPPED IN BUILDING PAPER IN 2004 BY THE MAN WHO CAME TO PUT THE NEW ONE IN AND WAS NEVER ASKED TO COME BACK FOR IT.****
- **THE COST IS A PRINTED SHEET FROM THE FIRM OF HEATING ENGINEERS WITH A LAMINATED CARD IN ITS WINDOW TWO DOORS OFF THE PARADE: A HEADING, A REFERENCE, A DATE IN OCTOBER, A FIGURE OF A HUNDRED AND EIGHTY POUNDS FOR REMOVAL, A LINE SAYING A SURVEY WAS CARRIED OUT ON THE SEVENTH, AND AN EMPTY SPACE AT THE FOOT FOR A NAME AND AN ACCOUNT. NOBODY ASKED FOR A SURVEY AND THERE WAS NO SUCH THING AS ANYBODY TO ASK.**
- **AND THE ROOM GETS THERE IN THE TIME IT TAKES TO MAKE A CUP OF TEA: THE FREEHOLDER IS A COMPANY OVER THE WATER, WHOSE LETTERHEAD CARRIES NO NUMBER ANYBODY HERE HAS RUNG, THE HALL WAS LEFT TO A TRUST THAT WAS WOUND UP IN ABOUT 1991 AND THE MAN IN HIS FORTIES FOUND THAT OUT OF A BOOK IN AN OFFICE IN ABOUT 2006 AND TOLD NOBODY AT THE TIME, THE GAS IS ON A METER THE WOMAN HOLDING THAT HALL'S KEY PAYS OUT OF HER OWN POCKET, AND THE NEW BOILER IS THE FIRM'S AND WORKS.**
- **THE MAN IN HIS FORTIES SAYS THE SENTENCE THREE TIMES, AND ON THE SECOND AND THIRD OCCASIONS HE IS ASKED WHETHER HE IS CLAIMING IT AND WHETHER HE IS REFUSING IT AND ANSWERS NO TO BOTH, AND ASKS THE ROOM TO HEAR BOTH, BECAUSE IN ABOUT AN HOUR ONE OF THEM WILL ASK HIM WHICH ONE HE MEANT. THE SHEET LIES FACE UP ON THE TABLE UNDER A COAT UNTIL THE LIGHTS GO OFF AND THE CUP COMES OFF IT AND GOES INTO A SINK.**
- **AND WHAT HAPPENS IS NOT ARRANGED: A WOMAN PUTS HER HAND ON THE CUPBOARD TO GET PAST A DOOR AND THE DOOR COMES OFF ITS HINGE ON THE SIDE AWAY FROM HER, AND A BOILER THAT HAD BEEN IN A CUPBOARD SINCE 2004 IS SUDDENLY COMING OUT OR STAYING IN, AND THE MAN IN HIS SIXTIES HAS A FOLDING PLATFORM TROLLEY IN HIS BOOT THAT HE HAS NEVER USED FOR ANYTHING HE WAS PAID FOR, AND HE SAYS HE WAS GOING THAT WAY, AND NOBODY ASKED HIM TO AND HE HAD NOT SAID HE WOULD.**
- **AND THE BOILER IN THE METHODIST HALL ON CARROW LANE, WHICH ANYBODY COULD HAVE HAD FOR NOTHING SINCE ABOUT FEBRUARY, IS STILL THERE AND STILL FREE AND IS NOT A TWELFTH OF ANYTHING. A THING OFFERED WHEN NOTHING IS WRONG IS A DIFFERENT THING FROM A THING ASKED FOR, AND ASKING FOR IT WOULD BE MAKING A ROTA ROW, AND NOBODY ASKED FOR ANYTHING.**

## 9. Eleven people, two things, and something kind

- **THE MAJOR TURN: HE DOES TWO SMALL ORDINARY THINGS THAT SOMEBODY NEEDED DONE AND HE DOES THEM IN FRONT OF ELEVEN PEOPLE AND NOBODY MAKES ANYTHING OF EITHER, AND ONE OF THE ELEVEN SAYS SOMETHING KIND TO HIM AND HE DOES NOT KNOW WHAT TO DO WITH IT AND GOES HOME.**
- **THE TWO THINGS: A TRESTLE TABLE LEG THAT CAME OUT OF ITS BRACKET, WITH THE PLATE AND ABOUT HALF THE TEAS ON THAT TABLE GOING OVER, WHICH HE FIXES IN ABOUT FOUR SECONDS WITH A RING SPANNER HE WAS CARRYING FOR A TAP ON THE COLD FEED IN HIS OWN KITCHEN · AND A PAPER BAG OF SHOPPING THAT WENT OUT OF THE DOOR IN THE DRAUGHT ONTO A WET PARADE, WHICH HE GOES OUT AND BRINGS BACK BY THE STRING, AND ON THE WAY IN HE PUTS A HAND ON THE DOOR FRAME AND THE DRAUGHT STOPS AND STAYS STOPPED AND NOBODY SAYS ANYTHING ABOUT THAT EITHER.**
- **AND THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FORTY-TWO HAS THE KEY TO *THAT* HALL AS WELL AS THE KEY TO THE ONE OFF THE PARADE, WHICH 0273 NOW SAYS AND WHICH WAS NOT ESTABLISHED ANYWHERE BEFORE 27 SEPTEMBER 2026. SHE HAS BOTH, SHE DOES THE METER AND THE PADLOCK AT BOTH, AND 0240'S *BECAUSE IT IS HER HALL* IS THE HALL OFF THE PARADE. A LATER WRITER MAY NOT PUT EITHER HALL'S GAS ON ANYBODY ELSE.**
- **THE KIND THING IS SAID BY THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FORTY-TWO WITH THE KEY TO THAT HALL, IN THE DOORWAY, ON HER WAY TO THE METER: *You've done that before.* — *No.* — *Somebody taught you.* — *No. There's nobody to teach me. I watch a thing and then I do it wrong about nine times.* — AND SHE LAUGHS AND SAYS, *Somebody did.* IT IS NOT A THANKS, IT IS NOT AN OFFER, IT IS NOT A JOB AND IT IS NOT A STANDING.**
- **HE DOES NOT KNOW WHAT TO DO WITH IT AND STAYS UNTIL THE CHAIRS GO UP AND CARRIES FOUR OF THEM, AND GOES OUT INTO THE RAIN AND STANDS ON THE STEP BECAUSE THE STEP IS THE WIDTH OF THE HALL. THE SPANNER GOES BACK IN HIS POCKET AND THE TABLE LEG IS STILL WHERE IT WAS PUT, THREE DAYS LATER AND AGAIN AT THE START OF THE FOLLOWING WEEK.**
- **AND NOBODY ASKS HIM ONE THING ALL NIGHT THAT WAS A QUESTION ABOUT HIM, NOBODY OFFERS HIM ONE THING AT ALL, NOTHING IS WRITTEN DOWN, AND THE THING HE ASKED IN JUNE IS STILL THE THING HE ASKED IN JUNE AND WAS NOT ASKED AND WAS NOT ANSWERED.** Being asked twice is not a standing, being useful is not a job, and being in a room is not being a member. He has no title, grade, department, chair, seat, membership, form, row on a rota or any way of being thanked, and the word *thank* is at zero in the chapter.

## 10. The last of an autumn, with everything exactly as it was

- **THE REGISTER IS AT NINETEEN, THE NINETEENTH IS A YARD LIGHT WITH A BULB IN A MAINTENANCE COLUMN AND THE BULB IS OUT, AND THE LINE UNDER IT IS SEVEN HUNDRED AND TWENTY DAYS AND IS STILL BLANK.** **Nell Ardent, who is thirty-nine and copies things out in her own hand, opened it on the Monday for a line she wanted on a form, and opened it again because she does that, and worked it out on the corner of a form with the end of her pen. She put it back in the cupboard it came out of and the register did not move and there is no name in that margin.**
- **THE WRONG ANSWER ABOUT THE REGISTER IS STILL GOING ROUND AND IS STILL WRONG, AND THERE IS NOW A BETTER WRONG ANSWER GOING ROUND WITH IT. AT A TABLE BY THE WALL ONE WOMAN HAS IT OFF AN AUNT WHO HAD IT OFF A FELLOW OFF A LORRY IN JULY, AND THE OTHER ONE, UNASKED, GIVES HER THE DAY, AND GIVES IT ACROSS THE TABLE THE WAY YOU GIVE SOMEBODY A THING THAT IS BETTER THAN THE THING THEY HAD, AND NEITHER SAYS WHERE IT CAME FROM, AND NOBODY AT THAT TABLE JOINS IN, AND THE MAN IN HIS SIXTIES IS FOUR FEET AWAY WITH A CUP OF TEA AND DOES NOT LOOK UP.**
- **AND THE QUESTION IS STILL A QUESTION: THE MAN FROM THE PARADE, WHO HAS PUT HIS HAND UP IN THAT ROOM TWICE SINCE THE SUMMER, DID NOT PUT IT UP, AND NOBODY NOTICED THAT EITHER, AND HE SAT THERE UNTIL TEN WITH HIS CUP IN BOTH HANDS.**
- **AND THE BOROUGH IS ORDINARY AND THE PACT IS HOLDING AND NEITHER IS SAID: a man comes out of the door of the building by the water with a piece of paper in his coat and a biro he is not using and nobody in the street says anything to him · the shutter goes up at about half past eight and the window card with that shop's own number on it and nothing else on it is in the window where it has always been, and the woman of about sixty-five is behind that counter and is not told anything · a van comes off the bridge with pipe in the back of it and two men stand three lengths of it against a shopfront, and one asks the other who said they would do it and is told *You*, and neither of them minds · at the bottom of Peverell Street the pump is in the doorway, the stopcock is off, the wall beside it is bare with a clean strip of newer paint and a grey line of dirt above it, and a woman goes up with a bucket and comes back with a bucket and a man comes down with a dog, and neither stops at a door · the first floor of the building by the water is being emptied again, of a bundle of post addressed to a man who left in the spring and an umbrella stand with nothing in it, and neither of the two people carrying them out says the other's name · and the woman of about fifty-five is in a second room in the same building, on a month, and its door shuts because somebody put a hasp on it at the end of October, and she has said nothing about it and would say nothing if she were asked.**
- **NOBODY IS RESCUED. NOBODY DIES. NOBODY RENEWS ANYTHING. NOTHING WAS MINUTED. NOBODY IS THANKED AND NOBODY REFUSES TO BE THANKED. THE ELEVEN STANDING REFUSALS ARE ELEVEN. CARD 0274 IS NOT A CLOSE.**

## 11. Still open, and still forbidden to Continuation 0009

Nothing in Volume 04 has been resolved, undone, improved on, restated or answered, and nothing in Batches 0001 to 0005 or in Continuations 0006, 0007 or 0008 may be. Nobody renews the order, nobody expires it, nobody renames the blank term, nobody puts a name in the line on page two, nobody fills the empty square, Roy Albery does not walk again, nobody dies, nobody is rescued by chance, nobody walks again, the eleven refusals are not twelve, the counter in the vestibule answers nothing and was not entered, the rota is not rebuilt, the second line under Bram Osei's Thursday is not looked at, the register does not move, the letter, the card, the page, the printed sheets, the column, the shop-window card, the crate with a lid and the four bottles all stand, the drawer under the counter in the vestibule is not opened, is not counted, is not asked about and is not mentioned, the sentence in it is not found, and nobody may ask who wrote it. **THE QUIET CUT, THE CIVIC ERASURE, THE ORIGIN OF THE COMMUNITY-WATCH TERM, THE THREE-LINE BRASS TOKENS, THE FIRST CHARTER, THE PROXY, THE ROOT CHAMBER, THE LOWGLASS FLOOR, THE BORROWED CITY AND THE NINE HINGES ARE NOT SPENT. FRANCES TOLLEY IS NOT NAMED AND THE READING RULE IS NOT USED. PRUE CALLOWAY'S FINDING IS NOT OPENED AGAIN AND THE CLAIM TO OPEN A DOOR IS SPENT. NO WEIR RESIDENT MAY BE DESCRIBED IN ANYTHING THAT LEAVES A STREET, AND THE FILLING ROUND DOWN COLLIER LANE IS NOT THE WEIR AND THE TWO MAY NEVER BE JOINED. COLIN WHARTON IS NOT NAMED AND NOBODY GOES UP THAT LANE. A WOMAN OF THIRTY-FOUR IS NOT NAMED, REFERENCED, GENDERED OR GIVEN A CONDITION. PARAGRAPH 4.11 IS NOT ANSWERED AND THE COMPLIANCE WEEK IS NOT VERIFIED. NO ROOT TERM EXPIRES AND THE RECLAMATION SEASON IS NOT SPENT, NOT ANTICIPATED AND NOT DESCRIBED AS APPROACHING — AND THE DISCREPANCY BETWEEN THE VOLUME 05 SECTION OF THE SERIES OUTLINE AND THE VOLUME 04 CLOSE PROMPT IS STILL OPEN AND IS FOR A HUMAN. **THE PERMITTED SAINT ORRA GATE SENTENCE WAS NOT USED IN CONTINUATION 0008 AND MAY BE USED AT MOST ONCE IN A LATER BATCH, UNSTAGED, IN THAT FORM, AND NO TERM AFTER THE TWENTY-THIRD OF MAY MAY BE NAMED OR DATED. EZRA MBIKI AND TREVOR NUNN ARE NOT IN A ROOM. NO HANDOVER IS STAGED. THE HOUR BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN IS NOT STAGED. NO STAND IS ADDED TO THE WET PAVEMENT AND THE COUNT STAYS CLOSED AT EIGHT. THE EMPTY SQUARE IS STILL EMPTY AND WAS NOT MENTIONED IN CHAPTERS 265–274. THE THREE-DIMENSIONAL FIGURE SEVEN HUNDRED AND TWENTY IS BOTH A DAY-COUNT AND THE LENGTH OF THIS SERIES AND THE TWO ARE UNRELATED; A WRITER WHO MEETS IT MUST CHECK WHICH.**

**AND THE FOUR FLAGS, ALL STILL OPEN AND NONE OF THEM A WRITER'S TO CLOSE. FIRST: THE NAME OF THE CITY. THE PROSE SAYS TIDEGLASS, ONCE IN EACH OF CHAPTERS 265–274 AND IN EVERY DATELINE, AND NO CHARACTER MAY NOTICE, MISNAME OR ASK ABOUT IT, AND NEITHER *NACRE* NOR *RIVER STACKS* APPEARS IN THOSE TEN CHAPTERS. SECOND: THE THIRD OF THE EIGHT PLACES IS PRINTED *Halsey Cross* AT 0244, AT 0251 AND IN 0243'S BODY, AND *Halse Cross* IN 0243'S OWN CLOSING BLOCK, AND BOTH SPELLINGS ARE ALSO IN THE STATE FILES — `state/continuity.md`, `state/current.md`, `state/open-threads.md` AND `state/batch-summaries.md` EACH PRINT BOTH, `state/character-state.md` PRINTS ONLY *Halsey Cross*, `state/chapter-summaries.md` PRINTS ONLY *Halse Cross* AND `state/live-canon.md` PRINTS NEITHER — **SO THE CONFLICT IS INSIDE THE STATE FILES AND NOT BETWEEN THE CHAPTERS AND THE STATE, AND A CHAPTER GOVERNS, A WRITER DID NOT DECIDE ANY OF IT, AND IT WAS NOT TOUCHED IN CONTINUATION 0008 ONLY BECAUSE THE EIGHT PLACES ARE NOT READ OUT IN ANY OF THOSE TEN CHAPTERS, SO NEITHER SPELLING APPEARS AND THE FLAG CARRIES FORWARD UNDECIDED. IF A LATER BATCH READS THE EIGHT PLACES ALOUD, IT PRINTS *Halsey Cross*, WHICH IS THE SPELLING ALREADY ON THE PAGE IN 0244, IN 0251 AND IN 0243'S BODY.**** THIRD: WHAT VOLUME 05 IS CENTRALLY. FOURTH: THE BOUNDARY — CHAPTERS 243 THROUGH 274 WERE WRITTEN AS THE TABLES DATED THEM AND BELONG TO NO VOLUME, NO OUTLINE WAS REWRITTEN, NO VOLUME 06 OUTLINE WAS WRITTEN, NO VOLUME-CLOSE PROMPT WAS WRITTEN, AND CARD 0274 IS NOT A CLOSE. **AND THE FOURTH IS NOW ESCALATED RATHER THAN REPEATED, BECAUSE IT HAS BEEN DEFERRED THREE TIMES AND EVERY PHASE SINCE HAS BEEN A CONTINUATION FOR THE SAME REASON. THREE ANSWERS EXIST AND A HUMAN MAY PICK ANY OF THEM IN ONE LINE AND A WRITER MAY NOT PICK: (a) THE BOUNDARY IS WHERE THE PAGE PUTS IT, `outline/volume-05.md` AND `outline/series.md` ARE CORRECTED TO 195–274, AND CHAPTER 275 IS A VOLUME CLOSE, WHICH IS THE ONLY ONE OF THE THREE THAT ENDS THE LOOP; (b) VOLUME 06 IS DEFINED FROM 243, GIVING IT THIRTY-TWO CHAPTERS AND LEAVING VOLUME 05 AT FORTY-EIGHT; (c) A FIFTH BLOCK IS NAMED AND 243–274 ARE GIVEN TO IT, WHICH COSTS AN OUTLINE NOBODY HAS WRITTEN. UNTIL A HUMAN RULES, EVERY PHASE IS A CONTINUATION.**

**AND THE REVIEW REPAIR OF 27 SEPTEMBER 2026, WHICH §12B OF THE CONTINUATION 0008 BLOCK IN `state/continuity.md` SETS OUT IN FULL AND WHICH A WRITER OF CONTINUATION 0009 INHERITS AS FOLLOWS. **A HASP WENT ON THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE'S SECOND ROOM AT THE END OF OCTOBER AND NOT IN THE SUMMER, AND EVERY PLACE THAT SAID THE SUMMER IS CORRECTED. **NOTHING WAS GIVEN OFF ANY SHELF: THE SHOPKEEPER REFUSED AND THE YOUNG WOMAN PAID, AND WHAT WENT ROUND IS THE MAN BEHIND THE COUNTER ON THE PARADE'S ACCOUNT, WHICH NOBODY CORRECTS AND WHICH IS NOT TO BE CORRECTED. **SHE HAS THE KEY TO BOTH HALLS. **THE DOOR WITH THE HASP IS HER OWN AND NOT THE MAN IN HIS THIRTIES', AND THAT MAN'S ROOM HAS NO HASP ON IT. **THE REGISTER WAS OPENED ON TUESDAY THE FIFTEENTH, WHICH IS ITS OWN CHAPTER'S OWN DATE AND THE DAY *SEVEN HUNDRED AND TWENTY* BELONGS TO. **THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF JUNE IS A DELIBERATE DRIFT AND IS NOT TO BE CORRECTED. **AND THE BATCH'S OWN VERBATIM-SPAN FIGURE IS RESTATED AS SCOPED, WITH THE BATCH-INTERNAL PASS RUN AND RECORDED AT SIX NINE-GRAMS, ONE OF TEN WORDS, ALL OF THEM A DATELINE, A RECURRING LOCATION OR A FIGURE'S OWN BY-NAME DESCRIPTION.**

**AND FIGURES THAT MAY NOT BE AGED BY A LATER BATCH: HANNA WRAY AND NINA CALDER, WHOSE AGES ARE NOT STATED ANYWHERE IN THIS ACCOUNT, AND EVERY UNAGED FIGURE LISTED IN §14 OF THE CONTINUATION 0008 BLOCK IN `state/continuity.md`. **NO NEW FIGURE IN CONTINUATION 0008 WAS GIVEN AN AGE AT ALL, AND TWO THAT A DRAFT HAD AGED WERE DE-AGED AT THE SOURCE. THE FREE FIGURES REMAINING ARE WOMEN 18, 87 AND 90 AND NO MEN, AND ALL THREE ARE UNSPENT.****

**AND A MEASURING PASS WAS RUN OVER CHAPTERS 265–274 BEFORE ANY OF THEM WAS SUMMARISED. IT CAUGHT EIGHTEEN DEFECTS: FIVE CHAPTERS OVER THE SCENE-BREAK CAP, A BARE *FORTY* FOR A COUNT OF PEOPLE, A BARE *THE DRAWER* AND *ELEVEN YEARS* IN ONE SENTENCE, *ABOUT ELEVEN SECONDS* USED AS A DURATION, *THE NINTH* USED AS A DATE, A SENTENCE REPEATED WORD FOR WORD FROM 0264, THREE BRIDGES DUPLICATING THE FOLLOWING PARAGRAPH AFTER A MECHANICAL MERGE, A CONTRADICTION OF THE CANON COUNT FOR THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF JUNE, TWO FIGURES GIVEN AN AGE NOBODY HAD ANY RIGHT TO GIVE THEM, TWO LENGTHS AND A HEAD-COUNT AGAINST FIGURES ALREADY IN THE ACCOUNT, A CONSTRUCTION ONE WORD FROM A BANNED MOTIF, A STATE ASSURANCE IN A LAD'S MOUTH, A CLOSING BLOCK RESTATING ITS OWN OPENING, AND TWO CLOSING BLOCKS THAT PRINTED A FIGURE OR A SUBJECT ALREADY IN THE BODY OR IN THE COLD OPEN. ALL EIGHTEEN WERE REPAIRED AT THE SOURCE, AND NO CARD, DATE, DAY-COUNT, FIGURE, NAME, MAJOR TURN, STANDING OR FLAG WAS CHANGED BY ANY OF THEM. THE VERBATIM-SPAN CHECK WAS RUN BEFORE ANY CHAPTER WAS SUMMARISED AND RETURNED **FOUR SPANS OF NINE WORDS OR MORE AND NONE OF TEN WORDS OR MORE** ON THE CHAPTERS ALONE **— WHICH WAS SCOPED TO THE CHAPTERS AGAINST EVERY OTHER FILE IN THE REPOSITORY AND NOT AGAINST EACH OTHER, WHICH A REVIEW ON 27 SEPTEMBER 2026 CAUGHT, AND WHICH IS CORRECTED AND RESTATED AS SCOPED IN §12B, WHERE THE BATCH-INTERNAL PASS IS ALSO RUN AND RECORDED,** AND WAS RUN AGAIN AT THE END ACROSS EVERY FILE WITH THE STATE FILES WRITTEN, WHERE IT STANDS AT **FOURTEEN SPANS OF NINE WORDS OR MORE, TWO OF TEN AND NONE OF ELEVEN**; THE TEN-WORD SURVIVORS ARE THIS BATCH'S OWN RECORD OF A REWORD AND A DESCRIPTION OF A TABLE LEG, AND THE NINE-WORD SURVIVORS ARE A CARD'S OWN PHRASING AND BY-NAME DESCRIPTIONS OF FIGURES AND STANDING OBJECTS. THE FULL LIST IS §12 OF THE CONTINUATION 0008 BLOCK, AND EVERY FIGURE IN THIS BLOCK IS TO BE RE-MEASURED FROM THE CHAPTERS AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM THIS FILE.**

# VOLUME 05 OPEN THREADS — after Continuation 0009, Chapter 284, Friday 30 December 2016

**Read with the "VOLUME 05, CONTINUATION 0009 — CHAPTERS 275–284" block at the end of `state/continuity.md`, which carries the canon for these ten chapters and supersedes this block wherever the two differ, and with the "VOLUME 05, CONTINUATION 0008 — CHAPTERS 265–274" block above it, which still governs everything those ten chapters did. **VOLUME 05 IS NOT CLOSED AND IS NOT DECLARED CLOSED. THE SERIES IS AT 284 OF 720. NOTHING IN CONTINUATION 0009 RESOLVED ANYTHING AND NOTHING IN IT MAY BE UNDONE, IMPROVED ON, RESTATED OR ANSWERED BY VOLUME 05. EVERY DAY-COUNT BELOW IS ADDED UP FROM ITS ANCHOR ON THE DAY IN QUESTION AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM A SUMMARY: at Friday 30 December 2016 the blank line under the nineteenth is seven hundred and sixty-five days, the card in the slot is six hundred and seventy-nine, and the letter in the tray is six hundred and eighty-one.****

## 1. A piece of post and a door with nobody behind it

- **THE MAJOR TURN: AN ITEM WAS ADDRESSED TO A TOP FLAT ON MARSH LANE THAT HAS BEEN EMPTY SINCE THE SUMMER, AND THE POSTMAN PUT IT DOWN IN THE RECESS OF THE DOOR RATHER THAN TAKE IT BACK, AND NOBODY WENT UP AND NOBODY TOLD ANYBODY AND HE WAS ASKED NOTHING ALL DAY.** The door has three bells on a small board and no names beside any of them, and he put his thumb on the top one and did not push it.
- **NOBODY'S NAME WENT ON ANYTHING AND THE ENVELOPE IS NOT OPENED ON THE PAGE AND ITS CONTENT IS NOWHERE IN THIS ACCOUNT.** He carries no card and has no book on him, and there is nothing to write it in.
- **THE OTHER HALF IS A BIN DAY AND TWO WOMEN, ONE ANNOYED AND ONE NOT, AND THE SENTENCE IS THAT THE POST IS THE ONE THING IN THAT TOWN WITH SOMEBODY TO SIGN FOR IT, AND THE ANSWER GIVEN BACK IS THAT POST KNOCKS AND POST DOES NOT GET AN ANSWER.** The lorry came once and did not come back, and the shopkeeper went out and got her own bin at about half past nine.
- **THE CARD IN THE POST SLOT AND THE LETTER IN THE TRAY WERE NOT MENTIONED AND ARE NOT IN THIS CHAPTER, AND THE ENVELOPE IS A THIRD OBJECT AGAIN AND IS FOR A DIFFERENT BUILDING ON A DIFFERENT STREET FROM THE ONE THE SEALED LETTER IS IN, WHICH IS NOT STATED ANYWHERE IN THIS ACCOUNT AND MAY NOT BE STATED BY A WRITER.**

## 2. Two men, one job, and a word for a ticket

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THE WORK GOT DONE AND THE HOURS WENT ON A TICKET THAT BELONGS TO SOMETHING ELSE, AND ONE MAN LEARNED A WORD FOR IT AND WAS NOT TOLD WHY THE WORD IS THE WORD. THE WORD IS *RECHARGE*.**
- **TWO MEN BOTH WANTED A LEANING STACK TAKEN DOWN AND NEITHER TOLD THE OTHER HE WAS COMING, AND THEY DISAGREED ONLY ABOUT THE JOB, AND THE ONE WHO HAD BEEN UP THE FLUE TWICE WAS RIGHT AND SAID SO.** A cap put on in a hurry in the summer, a top course gone over in the last week of October, a lorry's worth of brick in the gutter, an open flue in a roof with the rain going straight down it.
- **ALL THREE PEOPLE ARE RIGHT AND NOBODY IS A VILLAIN AND NOBODY WAS DISCOVERED: the householder who rang in June and was waited on, the tradesman eight miles off and over an estuary who took the job off his own book in August, and the man in this borough who did the work.** A ticket is raised on whatever is open at the time by somebody who is not paid for writing it down, and the office would not unpick it by hand because the day it does a correction made by hand ceases to be a ticket.
- **HE USED THE WORD TWICE IN THE FORTNIGHT AFTERWARDS, ONCE IN THE WRONG PLACE IN A SHOP ABOUT A KETTLE SOMEBODY ELSE HAD PAID FOR AND ONCE IN THE RIGHT PLACE TO A MAN QUOTING FOR WORK THAT HAD BEEN DONE AND INVOICED BY ANOTHER FIRM IN AUGUST.** A credit note came on the Friday, headed with the amount charged on the second of June, with a line at its foot that says the same thing twice over, and neither man would put anything in writing.
- **THE DRAIN IS NOWHERE IN THIS CHAPTER AND WAS NOT TOUCHED, THE ELEVEN CLAIMS WERE NOT ADDED UP, THE RATE HAS NOT MOVED SINCE 1987 AND THE CARD FORBIDDING ALL THREE WAS KEPT.**

## 3. Five doors on a landing

- **THE MAJOR TURN, AND IT IS NOT THE DOOR CLOSING: THE DOOR SHUT AND NOTHING ELSE HAPPENED, AND THE NEXT DOOR ALONG, COUNTING PAST THE FIRE DOOR, DID NOT, AND NOBODY JOINED THE TWO UP.**
- **A LANDLORD'S AGENT WENT ALONG THE LANDING WITH FOUR MATTERS ON HIS MIND AND MANAGED TO SETTLE NONE OF THEM, AND HE ASKED NOBODY ANYTHING AND WROTE NOTHING DOWN.** A fire door coming back too fast, a flickering stair light, letterboxes promised in June, and the fronts of the doors, which is a thing you do and not a thing you write down.
- **THE HASP ON THE THIRD DOOR WENT ON AT THE END OF OCTOBER AND WAS NOT RE-RUN: WHILE HE WAS STANDING THERE IT SHUT IN HER FACE, HARD ENOUGH TO SHIFT THE CATCH, AND SHE HAD TO LIFT IT AGAIN TO GET HER ELBOW PAST THE FRAME, AND HE ASKED HER NOTHING ABOUT THE FITTING ON THE INSIDE OF IT, AND SHE TOLD HIM NOTHING.**
- **THE FOURTH DOOR STOOD OPEN THREE INCHES WITH A CHAIR AGAINST IT AND A DRAUGHT UNDER IT THAT MOVED A RUG WHENEVER A DOOR OPENED ANYWHERE ON THAT FLOOR, AND HE DID NOT GO IN AND DID NOT ASK AGAIN, AND SHE SAID, AND WHOSE ENTIRE ANSWER, SET DOWN AS IT WAS SPOKEN, IS *I know it is*.**
- **ON THE THURSDAY SOMEBODY WEDGED IT, AND HE CAME BACK WITH A WEDGE HE HAD TAKEN OFF A VAN AND ASKED NOBODY ANYTHING, AND IT IS STILL WORK THAT BELONGS TO SOMEBODY.**
- **THE SHOPKEEPER IS IN IT FOR ONE PARAGRAPH. THE HASP, THE STAPLE AND THE SCREW EYE ARE STILL IN A BAG IN THE BACK OF THAT SHOP AND HAVE BEEN SINCE ABOUT THE SUMMER, NOTHING WAS GIVEN, THE YOUNG WOMAN PAID FOR HER OWN POST-FIX IN OCTOBER, AND NO GRATITUDE IS SHOWN, DESCRIBED OR REPORTED. THE YOUNG WOMAN WENT BACK UP THE PARADE ON THE SIDE AWAY FROM THE WATER, WAITED AT THE CORNER BY THE BANK UNTIL A BUS HAD GONE THE OTHER WAY, PUT THE BAG IN A DRAWER UPSTAIRS, ON TOP OF A SCARF, AND TOLD NOBODY. **SHE WENT BACK UP THE PARADE ON THE SIDE AWAY FROM THE WATER AND PUT THE BAG IN A DRAWER UPSTAIRS WHEN SHE GOT IN, AND NO CONNECTION IS MADE BY ANYBODY, BECAUSE A REVIEW OF 27 SEPTEMBER 2026 CUT FOUR STACKED NEGATIVES OUT OF THAT PARAGRAPH AND THE CONNECTION SHE DOES NOT MAKE IS BETTER LEFT UNDRAWN.****

## 4. An ordinary cold week in the first fortnight of December

- **THE MAJOR TURN, AND IT IS THE WHOLE OF THE CHAPTER: THE WEEK IS ORDINARY AND SOMEBODY IN IT SAYS THAT SHE CANNOT REMEMBER AN EARLIER WINTER IN THIS BOROUGH, AND NOBODY ARGUES.** Said at half past ten in a bus shelter by a woman with a shopping bag to a man in a fleece. Her only reason is that her father had one the like of it and had not been on that road a year and was on the Marshmont side.
- **THE OTHER HALF IS A MAN WHO WAS NOT CALLED. HE LIVES AT NUMBER TWO IN THAT LANE, DOES GAS AND HEATING, HAS A VAN WITH A SIGN ON IT, AND TWO OTHER HOUSES ON THAT LANE HAVE TAKEN A SHEET OFF HIM.** The boiler at number six went on the Thursday morning, the firm said Friday after four for ninety-four pounds and came on the Friday. He went by her door at half past eight with a bag of tools, said good morning, kept to the far side of the road and did not turn his head towards her house. She did not ask him, and it occurred to her that she could have put a tenner under his door, and she did not, and nobody in that lane discussed it.
- **THE WEEK WAS GRITTING ONCE, A PARENTS' EVENING ON THE PARADE, A COLLEGE BUS THAT DID NOT COME TWICE, A MAN WITH A BUCKET AND A MOP, A STANDPIPE AND A QUEUE FILLING BOTTLES, AND A FIGURE THAT IS HIGHER AFTER FOUR THAN BEFORE IT.**
- **NOTHING IN THE WEEK IS A FINDING AND NO NARRATOR SAYS THAT ANYTHING IN IT IS A FINDING, AND A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO WAS HELPED: SHE HAD A FIRE IN A GRATE, A HOT WATER BOTTLE, A GAS FIRE IN THE OTHER ROOM AND A LINE OF WASHING DRIED ON A CLOTHES HORSE, AND THAT WAS THE MENU OF THE WEEK.**

## 5. A third wrong answer about what will be proved

- **THE MAJOR TURN: A ROOM THAT HAD ALREADY SETTLED ON AN ANSWER IT LIKED WAS PUT A DIFFERENT QUESTION AND PRODUCED A THIRD, AND THE THIRD THING IS WORSE THAN EITHER OF THE OTHER TWO, AND NOBODY IN THAT ROOM CAN COMPARE THE THREE.** The first is the register, off an aunt off a man off a lorry. The second is the day, the twenty-eighth of June. The third is that somebody over the water will open a drawer in a building none of them has ever been inside, and find a sheet carrying this borough's postcode.
- **THE YOUNG WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY, WHO GAVE THAT ROOM THE DAY IN OCTOBER, IS IN IT, AND SAYS THE DAY IS BETTER, AND SAYS SHE IS NOT GOING TO SAY IT A THIRD TIME TO MAKE IT GO ROUND AGAIN.** A familiar wrong answer is not a confirmed one, not a weakened one and not a second one of the same, and nobody corrects the third one.
- **THE FINDING ARRIVES IN TWO SENTENCES IN A DOORWAY AND NOT AS A LABEL: THE YOUNG WOMAN TELLS THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE SHE HAS HEARD TWO, AND IS TOLD SHE HAS HEARD THREE AND KEPT ONE, AND IS ANSWERED, YOU AS WELL.**
- **THE MAN IN HIS SIXTIES IS NOT IN THAT ROOM, DID NOT ARRANGE IT, IS NOT TOLD AND MUST NOT BE TOLD, AND THE CHAPTER PUTS IT INTO HER MOUTH THAT HE WAS ON A PAVEMENT IN THAT TOWN IN JULY AND COULD NOT GET AT BOTH HALVES OF HIS OWN ANSWER, AND THAT NOT ONE PERSON IN THAT HALL HAD EVER STOOD THERE.**
- **THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF JUNE MAY NOT BE PUT BACK TO FOUR AND WAS NOT: FIVE PEOPLE WENT OUT ON THE SEVENTH OF SEPTEMBER, AND THE DATE IS WRONG ON PURPOSE, AND NO CHAPTER IN THIS BATCH CORRECTED IT, EXPLAINED IT OR IMPROVED ON IT.**

## 6. A gate, a padlock and the only sentence available

- **THE MAJOR TURN AND IT IS ALL THAT HAPPENS: THE TWO OF THEM WALK PAST IT, AND ONE OF THEM SAYS THE ONLY SENTENCE THAT IS AVAILABLE, WHICH IS THAT IT IS A GATE, AND THE OTHER DOES NOT WRITE IT DOWN.**
- **THE TWO ARE THE YOUNG WOMAN OF ABOUT TWENTY, WHO CAME OFF A BUS ON THE THURSDAY NIGHT BECAUSE THE COLLEGE BUS DOES NOT RUN IN THE VACATION, AND THE MAN IN HIS THIRTIES, WHO WAS THERE FOR A PIECE OF BATTEN OFF A SKIP AND NOT FOR HER.**
- **THE GATE IS A FIVE-BAR ONE SET ON A SQUARE POST, WITH A CHAIN ROUND THE TOP HINGE BAR AND A PADLOCK HANGING OPEN IN THE HASPIED SIDE WITH THE KEY STILL IN IT, AND IT HAS STOOD THAT WAY SINCE THE SUMMER CAME. NOTHING IN THIS CHAPTER SAYS WHAT THE CHAIN IS FOR AND NOTHING IN THIS BATCH DOES.** There is an empty trough in about nine acres of grass and the gate is shut.
- **THE NUMBER IS STENCILLED ON THE TOP RAIL IN WHITE PAINT AND IS NO LONGER A NUMBER: THREE FIGURES, THE FIRST GONE AT THE CORNER, THE SECOND GONE DOWN THE MIDDLE, AND WHAT SURVIVES OF THE THREE IS A SINGLE MARK AND A PIECE OF A FOOT, AND NEITHER PERSON USES IT.** He had a pad of work notes open on a wing mirror with a pencil stub in his hand and bit the pencil stub, and closed the pad.
- **THE MAN AT NUMBER FORTY-ONE IS PASSED AND NOT STOPPED AT. THE PUMP IS IN HIS DOORWAY, THE STOPCOCK IS OFF, THE WALL BESIDE IT IS BARE WITH A CLEAN STRIP OF NEWER PAINT AND A GREY LINE ABOVE IT, THERE IS A LIGHT ON IN HIS FRONT ROOM, AND NOBODY KNOCKS ON THAT DOOR, NOBODY RINGS HIM, HE IS NOT RESCUED, HE HAS NOT BEEN SET FREE AND NOBODY SAYS THAT HE HAS.**

## 7. Two loads of sand done in July and found out about in December

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THE SAME MAN DID TWO THINGS IN ONE WEEK IN JULY AND TOLD A DIFFERENT PERSON ABOUT EACH, THE TWO PEOPLE HE TOLD ARE NOT ON ANYTHING AND HAVE NEVER MET, AND THE SECOND WAS FOUND OUT ABOUT BY ACCIDENT, IN A SHOP, OUT OF THE MOUTH OF A THIRD PERSON WHO HAD BEEN TOLD BY ONE OF THE OTHER TWO, WHO DID NOT KNOW HE WAS STANDING THERE.**
- **THE TWO THINGS ARE THREE BARROWLOADS INTO A SOFT PATCH AT THE BACK OF THE SHOP THAT SHUTS EARLY, WHERE A LORRY HAD BEEN IN AND TURNED WITH A LOAD ON, ON A THURSDAY; AND SIX BARROWLOADS INTO THE GAP UNDER A LIFTING SLAB AT THE TOP OF MARSH LANE ON THE WEDNESDAY OF THE SAME WEEK.** The first was told to the woman who runs that shop, who has said nothing since July. The second was told in September, in a kitchen, to a woman called Julie who works in a shop in a place off Corrance Lane that has a name.
- **THE MOUTH IT CAME OUT OF IS A WOMAN WHO WORKS AT THE YARD AT SARN WHO KNEW IT ONLY BECAUSE JULIE TOLD HER IN ABOUT NOVEMBER.** The shopkeeper told her he was standing right behind her with a bread knife in his hand, then sold her a pound of small onions and turned the till towards her, and said ninety-one pounds, and said nothing else.
- **HE ADMITS ONE OF THE TWO THINGS AND NOT THE OTHER, IN HIS OWN MOUTH: HE TOLD JULIE IN SEPTEMBER BECAUSE IT CAME UP IN HER KITCHEN, AND NOT IN JULY BECAUSE HE DID NOT KNOW HOW SHE WOULD TAKE IT, AND HE CALLS THAT A BAD REASON TWICE.**
- **NOTHING WAS GIVEN, NOTHING WAS ASKED FOR, NOBODY WENT AND TOLD ANYBODY, NOBODY WENT AND THANKED ANYBODY, NOTHING IS ARRANGED, NOTHING IS A STRUCTURE, NOTHING IS A PRECEDENT, A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED, AND THE LAD WHO WASHES UP IN THAT PARADE'S KITCHEN CAME OUT OF THAT KITCHEN WITH THE LAST OF A TRAY ON IT AND WAS QUESTIONED ABOUT NONE OF IT.**

## 8. A second piece of paper and a cupboard door on a trolley

- **THE MAJOR TURN: THE SECOND SHEET WAS HANDLED BY SOMEBODY WHO HAD LEARNED SOMETHING FROM THE FIRST ONE, AND WHAT SHE LEARNED WAS THAT A SAYING IS NOT A CLAIM, AND THE SECOND COST ALSO WENT NOWHERE, AND IT IS NOT BETTER AND IT IS NOT WORSE.** The first sheet was a hundred and eighty pounds for removing a boiler nobody owns, and the boiler went down a back lane on a trolley in November.
- **THE SECOND IS TWO HUNDRED AND SIXTY POUNDS FROM A FIRM OF ELECTRICAL CONTRACTORS FOR A FIXED WIRE TEST AND THE MAKING GOOD, WITH A LINE SAYING A TEST CANNOT BE BOOKED UNTIL IT IS CONFIRMED IN WRITING, AND THERE IS NOTHING IN THAT ROOM CAN SERVE AS THE WRITTEN CONFIRMATION IT ASKS FOR.** Nobody had asked for a test.
- **A LETTING AGENT CAME IN BECAUSE OF A SHOP TWO DOORS ALONG THAT THE SAME COMPANY DOES OWN, AND SAID HE COULD PUT THE HALL ON IN A FORTNIGHT, AND THAT HE COULD NOT LET A ROOM TO ANYBODY WITHOUT THAT TEST, AND THAT HE COULD NOT SIGN A LEASE HIMSELF EITHER.**
- **THE WOMAN WHO HAS THE KEY SAID IN THE ROOM, IN HER OWN WORDS, THAT SHE IS NOT HAVING THAT TEST DONE AND IS NOT REFUSING IT, AND ASKED THE ROOM TO HEAR BOTH.** The man in his forties, who said the same sentence in November and was asked twice which of two he meant, pointed out that she has said it backwards. She told him the bin is a firm with a job number on it and it is not the bin.
- **SOMEBODY CARRIES SOMETHING OUT ON A TROLLEY AND IT IS STILL NOT ARRANGED: THE CUPBOARD DOOR THAT HAS BEEN LYING FACE DOWN ON THE BOARDS UNDER THAT STAIR SINCE NOVEMBER WENT DOWN A BACK LANE ON THE FOLDING PLATFORM TROLLEY AND WAS LEFT STANDING AGAINST THE OUTSIDE WALL UNDER THE PIPE, BECAUSE NOBODY HAD TOLD HIM WHERE IT OUGHT TO GO. IT CAME OFF THE WALL AND DOWN ONTO THE PAVING SLABS IN A LADLE OF RAIN ON THE THURSDAY.** The boiler in the Methodist hall on Carrow Lane, which anybody could have had for nothing since about February, is still there and still free and is not a twelfth of anything. A thing offered when nothing is wrong is a different thing from a thing asked for, and a boiler is not a twelfth thing.

## 9. Five rooms and a pair of gloves

- **THE MAJOR TURN: HE WAS IN FIVE ROOMS AND OUT OF ONE OF THOSE FIVE A SMALL OBJECT WAS PUSHED TOWARDS HIM, AND HE COULD NOT USE IT, AND HE DID NOT GIVE IT BACK, AND HE DID NOT ASK ANYBODY WHAT IT WAS FOR, AND THE PERSON WHO GAVE IT IS NOT A PERSON WHO OWNS ANYTHING.**
- **THE THING IS A PAIR OF WOOLLEN GLOVES WITH NO FINGERS IN THEM, GREY, SHINY AT THE THUMB AND THE HEEL, WITH THE PAPER BAND STILL ON ONE.** The giver is the young woman of about twenty, who put them on a table beside his cup without saying anything about them, then said they were no use on a dish and that he would have had a bad winter, and then said where the proper ones were to be had on that parade and that she had been in twice asking and had not asked twice in the one shop.
- **HE CANNOT USE THEM BECAUSE HE NEEDS HIS FINGERS TO GRIP AND TO FEEL AND HE WASHES UP IN HOT WATER. HE PUT THEM IN THE LEFT POCKET OF HIS CARDIGAN AND PUT A HAND IN THAT POCKET TO CHECK IT BEFORE HE WENT, AND FOUND A RECEIPT AND A LOLLY STICK IN THERE, AND LEFT THE GLOVES WHERE THEY WERE. ON THE THURSDAY HE PUT THEM ON AT THE TOP OF A STAIR FOR ABOUT AS LONG AS A CUP OF TEA TAKES, THEN TOOK THEM OFF AGAIN, AND BY FRIDAY THEY WERE BACK IN THE POCKET AND DRY THROUGH.**
- **THE FIVE ROOMS START WITH THE KITCHEN AT THE BACK OF A PARADE IN THE MORNING, THE FLOOR OF THE SHOP THAT SHUTS EARLY AT TEN, A KITCHEN IN A HOUSE IN CORRANCE LANE AT HALF PAST ONE WHERE HE IS BEING PAID THE SAME FIGURE AS THE WOMAN WHO USUALLY COMES, A HALL OFF A PARADE IN THE EVENING, AND A ROOM OVER A SHOP AT THE END OF A PARADE WHERE SIX PEOPLE PLAY CARDS.**
- **HE IS ON THE DISHES. HE HAS NO TITLE, GRADE, DEPARTMENT, CHAIR, SEAT, MEMBERSHIP, FORM, ROW ON A ROTA OR ANY WAY OF BEING SAID ANYTHING ABOUT, BEING ASKED WHETHER HE WAS DOING ANYTHING ON FRIDAY NIGHT IS NOT A STANDING, BEING USEFUL IS NOT A JOB AND BEING IN A ROOM IS NOT BEING A MEMBER, AND THE THING HE ASKED ON THE FOURTH OF JUNE IS STILL THE THING HE ASKED IN JUNE AND WAS NOT ASKED AND WAS NOT ANSWERED. THE WORD *THANK* IS AT ZERO IN THE CHAPTER.**

## 10. An ordinary thirtieth of December with everything exactly as it was

- **THE REGISTER IS AT NINETEEN, THE LINE UNDER IT IS SEVEN HUNDRED AND SIXTY-FIVE DAYS AND IS STILL BLANK, AND THAT IS THE ONE DAY-COUNT IN THE PROSE OF THESE TEN CHAPTERS.** **Nell Ardent, thirty-nine, went in at about ten past ten for a line she wanted for a form of her own, wrote it out, shut the book, opened it again because she does that, put her finger down the column as far as the writing goes, and found the last thing in the book is a yard light with its bulb in a column headed maintenance and that the bulb is out. A ruled line runs on from under it towards the foot of the page and stops about two inches short of the baseboard. She did the sum on the corner of the form with the butt end of a pen. She shelved the book. The register did not move and there is no name in the margin.**
- **AND THE BOROUGH IS ORDINARY AND THE PACT IS HOLDING AND NEITHER IS SAID AND THE WORD *PACT* APPEARS NOWHERE IN THE PROSE: five shops shut for the winter and hardboard goes over three windows on the Saturday, and the woman who runs one of them watches from the pavement · a man puts grit at the corner by the bank and does not go up Peverell Street at all · the first floor of the building by the water is cleared out and the keys go back on the Friday, and two men carry a chair and a desk down and say nothing to each other, and one of them says as they pass the corridor that there is still somebody up there · the woman of about fifty-five is in a room on a month with a hasp on its door and the light is on under it and neither man knocked · a jigsaw arrives with a flat battery, and a van comes off the bridge with a ladder and a coil of rope, and the man in his thirties remarks to nobody that this is the third ladder he has seen that week, and the man in the van says aye, and nothing else follows.
- **AND THREE ANSWERS GO ROUND AT THE HALL ON A METER WITH NINE OF THEM IN IT AND NOT ONE OF THEM THERE FOR ANYTHING YOU COULD PUT ON PAPER: THE REGISTER, OFF AN AUNT OFF A MAN OFF A LORRY IN JULY; THE DAY, THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF JUNE, GIVEN ACROSS THE TABLE WITHOUT BEING ASKED; AND A THIRD ONE THAT HAD BEEN SAID IN THAT SAME HALL ON THE SIXTH, COMING BACK ROUND WITH SOMETHING ADDED TO IT THAT NOBODY THERE COULD ACCOUNT FOR.** The man from the parade puts his hand up and says somebody in that room must know by now, and four people at that table look at the boards of it, and nobody says anything, and he puts his hand down. **The question is still a question.**
- **AND A MAN IN HIS FORTIES SAYS TO THE WOMAN OF ABOUT FIFTY-FIVE THAT SHE IS NOT LONG ON THAT ROOM, AND SHE SAYS A MONTH, AND HE SAYS RIGHT AND GOES BACK TO THE PRICE OF THE BOILER, AND NOBODY SAID THE MONTH, OR THE MONTH AFTER IT AT THE END OF IT, AND NOBODY ENQUIRED WHETHER SHE HAD ANYWHERE TO GO.**
- **NOBODY IS RESCUED. NOBODY DIES. NOBODY RENEWS ANYTHING. NOTHING WAS MINUTED. NOBODY IS THANKED AND NOBODY REFUSES TO BE THANKED. THE ELEVEN STANDING REFUSALS ARE ELEVEN. CARD 0284 IS NOT A CLOSE.**

## 11. Still open, and still forbidden to Continuation 0010

Nothing in Volume 04 has been resolved, undone, improved on, restated or answered, and nothing in Batches 0001 to 0005 or in Continuations 0006, 0007, 0008 or 0009 may be. Nobody renews the order, nobody expires it, nobody renames the blank term, nobody puts a name in the line on page two, nobody fills the empty square, Roy Albery does not walk again, nobody dies, nobody is rescued by chance, nobody walks again, the eleven refusals are not twelve, the counter in the vestibule answers nothing and was not entered and was not in any scene, the rota is not rebuilt, the second line under Bram Osei's Thursday is not looked at, the register does not move, the letter, the card, the page, the printed sheets, the column, the shop-window card, the crate with a lid and the four bottles all stand, the drawer under the counter in the vestibule is not opened, is not counted, is not asked about and is not mentioned, and the trade-counter drawer is in no chapter of this batch either. **THE QUIET CUT, THE CIVIC ERASURE, THE ORIGIN OF THE COMMUNITY-WATCH TERM, THE THREE-LINE BRASS TOKENS, THE FIRST CHARTER, THE PROXY, THE ROOT CHAMBER, THE LOWGLASS FLOOR, THE BORROWED CITY AND THE NINE HINGES ARE NOT SPENT. COLIN WHARTON IS NOT NAMED AND NOBODY GOES UP THAT LANE. A WOMAN OF THIRTY-FOUR IS NOT NAMED, REFERENCED, GENDERED OR GIVEN A CONDITION. NO ROOT TERM EXPIRES AND THE RECLAMATION SEASON IS NOT SPENT, NOT ANTICIPATED AND NOT DESCRIBED AS APPROACHING — AND THE DISCREPANCY BETWEEN THE VOLUME 05 SECTION OF THE SERIES OUTLINE AND THE VOLUME 04 CLOSE PROMPT IS STILL OPEN AND IS FOR A HUMAN. **THE PERMITTED SAINT ORRA GATE SENTENCE WAS NOT USED IN CONTINUATION 0009 AND NO SAINT ORRA TERM AFTER THE TWENTY-THIRD OF MAY MAY BE NAMED OR DATED. THE 11:00–13:30 ROW IS STILL EMPTY AND STILL DOES NOT SAY *VACANT*. EZRA MBIKI AND TREVOR NUNN ARE NOT IN A ROOM. NO HANDOVER IS STAGED. THE HOUR BETWEEN SIX AND SEVEN IS NOT STAGED. THE COUNT STAYS CLOSED AT EIGHT. THE DRAIN IS NOT TO BE TOUCHED, THE ELEVEN CLAIMS ARE NOT ADDED UP, NO TWELFTH PAPER GOES IN THE BAG, NOBODY IS DISCOVERED, AND THE FIGURE OF £1,812 IS NOT AVAILABLE. NOTHING IN THIS BATCH REFUTES THE SENTENCE THAT A PERSON WHO IS NOT COUNTED IS NOT A PERSON WHO IS HELPED, AND NOTHING IN IT MAY BE REFUTED. A HOSE IS AT ZERO. A CHAIN ON A GATE IS NOT AN ARRANGEMENT. A BOILER IS NOT A TWELFTH THING. A HASPIED DOOR IS NOT A RESCUE AND NOT A PRECEDENT. NOBODY MAY BE GIVEN A TITLE, A GRADE, A DEPARTMENT, A CHAIR, A SEAT, A MEMBERSHIP, A FORM, A ROTA ROW, A THANK-YOU OR A WAY OF BEING THANKED, AND NO CHAPTER MAY CONTAIN A THANKS OF ANY KIND, AND THE WORD *THANK* IS TO BE MEASURED IN EVERY CHAPTER BEFORE THE BATCH IS CALLED DONE. JONAS MERCER IS IN NO CHAPTER OF THIS BATCH, IS ASKED NOTHING, AND MAY NOT BE PUT IN FIVE CHAPTERS OUT OF TEN. THE FIGURES ARE CLOSED AND MAY NOT BE RE-USED, THE FIGURE *FORTY* MAY NOT BE USED FOR ANY DURATION, COUNT OF OBJECTS OR COUNT OF PEOPLE, NO 48-MINUTE FIGURE MAY EVER BE PRINTED, *IN ABOUT FOUR YEARS* IS AT ZERO AND *FOUR YEARS* NOT AT ALL, AND THE MOTIFS *FOUR HUNDRED YARDS*, *ELEVEN FEET BY NINE*, *THERE IS NO FORM IN THIS BOROUGH*, *NOBODY IS REQUIRED*, *A BORROWED CITY* AND *A TUESDAY* ARE CAPPED AT TWO IN ANY CHAPTER.***

**AND THE FOUR FLAGS, ALL STILL OPEN AND NONE OF THEM A WRITER'S TO CLOSE. FIRST: THE NAME OF THE CITY. THE PROSE SAYS TIDEGLASS, ONCE IN EACH OF CHAPTERS 275–284 AND IN EVERY DATELINE, AND NO CHARACTER MAY NOTICE, MISNAME OR ASK ABOUT IT, AND NEITHER *NACRE* NOR *RIVER STACKS* APPEARS IN THOSE TEN CHAPTERS. SECOND: THE THIRD OF THE EIGHT PLACES IS PRINTED *Halsey Cross* AT 0244, AT 0251 AND IN 0243'S BODY, AND *Halse Cross* IN 0243'S OWN CLOSING BLOCK, AND BOTH SPELLINGS ARE ALSO IN THE STATE FILES — `state/continuity.md`, `state/current.md`, `state/open-threads.md` AND `state/batch-summaries.md` EACH PRINT BOTH, `state/character-state.md` PRINTS ONLY *Halsey Cross* AND `state/chapter-summaries.md` PRINTS ONLY *Halse Cross*, **AND FIVE STATE FILES PRINT BOTH SPELLINGS — `state/continuity.md`, `state/current.md`, `state/open-threads.md`, `state/batch-summaries.md` AND `state/live-canon.md`. THE FIGURE *FOUR* AND THE CLAIM THAT `state/live-canon.md` PRINTS NEITHER WERE BOTH WRONG, WERE MEASURED, AND WERE CORRECTED ON 27 SEPTEMBER 2026; EVERY EARLIER BLOCK AND EVERY EARLIER PROMPT STILL SAYS FOUR AND STILL SAYS NEITHER, AND WHERE THEY DISAGREE WITH THIS PARAGRAPH THIS PARAGRAPH IS RIGHT, BECAUSE A CORRECTION THAT ERASES ITS OWN ERROR LEAVES NO RECORD THAT A FIGURE WAS EVER WRONG** — **SO THE CONFLICT IS INSIDE THE STATE FILES AND NOT BETWEEN THE CHAPTERS AND THE STATE, AND A CHAPTER GOVERNS, A WRITER DID NOT DECIDE ANY OF IT, AND IT WAS NOT TOUCHED IN CONTINUATION 0009 ONLY BECAUSE THE EIGHT PLACES ARE NOT READ OUT IN ANY OF THOSE TEN CHAPTERS, SO NEITHER SPELLING APPEARS AND THE FLAG CARRIES FORWARD UNDECIDED. IF A LATER BATCH READS THE EIGHT PLACES ALOUD, IT PRINTS *Halsey Cross*.**** THIRD: WHAT VOLUME 05 IS CENTRALLY. FOURTH: THE BOUNDARY — CHAPTERS 243 THROUGH 284 WERE WRITTEN AS THE TABLES DATED THEM AND BELONG TO NO VOLUME, NO OUTLINE WAS REWRITTEN, NO VOLUME 06 OUTLINE WAS WRITTEN, NO VOLUME-CLOSE PROMPT WAS WRITTEN, AND CARD 0284 IS NOT A CLOSE. **AND THE FOURTH IS ESCALATED RATHER THAN REPEATED: IT HAS NOW BEEN DEFERRED FOUR TIMES. THREE ANSWERS EXIST AND A HUMAN MAY PICK ANY OF THEM IN ONE LINE AND A WRITER MAY NOT PICK: (a) THE BOUNDARY IS WHERE THE PAGE PUTS IT, `outline/volume-05.md` AND `outline/series.md` ARE CORRECTED TO 195–284, AND CHAPTER 285 IS A VOLUME CLOSE, WHICH IS THE ONLY ONE OF THE THREE THAT ENDS THE LOOP AND WHICH THIS BATCH HAS NOT ASSUMED. **THAT OPTION READ *195–274, AND CHAPTER 275* IN AN EARLIER DRAFT OF THIS BLOCK, AND IT WAS WRONG IN BOTH HALVES, BECAUSE 275 IS ALREADY WRITTEN AND IS NOT A CLOSE; `workspace/volume-05/continuation-0010/PROMPT.md`, WRITTEN THE SAME DAY, ALREADY SAID 195–284 AND CHAPTER 285, SO TWO FILES WERE OFFERING A HUMAN TWO DIFFERENT RULINGS IN THE SAME LANGUAGE. ONLY ONE IS STILL AVAILABLE AND IT IS THE ONE PRINTED HERE. THE BOUNDARY IS NOT DECIDED AND NOTHING ELSE IN THIS PARAGRAPH WAS TOUCHED**; (b) VOLUME 06 IS DEFINED FROM 243, GIVING IT FORTY-TWO CHAPTERS ON THE PAGE AND LEAVING VOLUME 05 AT FORTY-EIGHT; (c) A FIFTH BLOCK IS NAMED AND 243–284 ARE GIVEN TO IT, WHICH COSTS AN OUTLINE NOBODY HAS WRITTEN. UNTIL A HUMAN RULES, EVERY PHASE IS A CONTINUATION, NO VOLUME-CLOSE PROMPT MAY BE WRITTEN, NO OUTLINE MAY BE REWRITTEN TO HIDE IT, AND CARD 0284 IS NOT A CLOSE.****

**AND THE REVIEW REPAIRS OF 27 SEPTEMBER 2026, WHICH §12B OF THE CONTINUATION 0008 BLOCK SETS OUT IN FULL, WERE INHERITED AND KEPT AND NONE OF THE SIX SURVIVED INTO THIS BATCH: THE HASPIED DOOR WENT ON AT THE END OF OCTOBER, NOTHING WAS GIVEN OFF ANY SHELF, SHE HAS THE KEY TO BOTH HALLS, THE HASPIED DOOR IS HERS, THE REGISTER WAS OPENED ON ITS OWN CHAPTER'S OWN DATE, AND THE TWENTY-EIGHTH OF JUNE IS A DELIBERATE DRIFT AND WAS NOT CORRECTED IN ANY OF CHAPTERS 275–284.**

**AND A MEASURING PASS WAS RUN OVER CHAPTERS 275–284 BEFORE ANY OF THEM WAS SUMMARISED, AND A FOURTH PASS WAS RUN AFTER THE STATE FILES WERE WRITTEN. IT CAUGHT NINETEEN DEFECTS, ALL NINETEEN REPAIRED AT THE SOURCE, SEVENTEEN IN THE CHAPTERS AND TWO IN THE STATE FILES: TEN CHAPTERS OVER THE SCENE-BREAK CAP AGAINST A CAP OF TWO; A BARE *ELEVEN* IN FIVE CHAPTERS AND AN *ELEVEN YEARS* ADJACENCY IN ONE; A BARE *FORTY* FOR A PRICE; A *BELL PANEL* FIVE TIMES; A TWELFTH IN A CLOSING BLOCK; A RADIATOR GIVEN A FIRE AND A RADIATOR'S DESCRIPTION REPEATED FROM 0272; SIX STATE ASSURANCES ABOUT A LAD, WHICH IS THE CONSTRUCTION A MEASURING PASS HAS NOW CUT OUT OF ELEVEN CHAPTERS; A WINDOW A LAD WOULD HAVE BEEN THROWN OUT OF; A REPEAT OF 0274'S REGISTER SCENE VERBATIM AT 0284; A REPEAT OF 0272'S PROSE AT 0282; FIVE SENTENCES REPEATED FROM 0268 AND 0269 IN 0279; A SENTENCE REPEATED FROM 0165 AND ONE FROM 0193; A SENTENCE REPEATED FROM 0229; A TURN RE-RUN AT 0282; A CLOSING BLOCK REUSING ITS OWN BODY; AND THREE CHAPTER-DATELINE COLLISIONS. NO CARD, DATE, DAY-COUNT, FIGURE, NAME, MAJOR TURN, STANDING OR FLAG WAS CHANGED BY ANY OF THEM. **THE VERBATIM-SPAN CHECK WAS RUN FOUR TIMES AND ALL FOUR FIGURES ARE RECORDED SEPARATELY IN §12A OF THE CONTINUATION 0009 BLOCK: 557 NINE-GRAMS AGAINST EVERY OTHER FILE IN THE REPOSITORY BEFORE ANY STATE FILE FOR THIS BATCH EXISTED, 8 INSIDE THE TEN CHAPTERS AGAINST EACH OTHER, 83 AGAINST THE TWELVE CHAPTERS THAT CAME BEFORE THEM WITH NIL REUSES OF PROSE, AND 225 DISTINCT ELEVEN-WORD SPANS AGAINST THE SIX STATE FILES THIS BATCH WROTE, WHICH IS NOW NINETY-FIVE, ALL DATELINES AND BY-NAME DESCRIPTIONS. THE SECOND RUN IS THE ONE THAT MATTERS; IT WENT FROM SIXTY-SIX TO EIGHT, THE THIRD FROM TWO HUNDRED AND FIFTY-THREE TO EIGHTY-EIGHT, AND THE FOURTH FROM TWO HUNDRED AND TWENTY-FIVE TO ONE.** EVERY FIGURE IN THIS BLOCK IS TO BE RE-MEASURED FROM THE CHAPTERS BY A LATER BATCH AND NOT CARRIED FORWARD FROM THIS FILE.

THEY ARE SET OUT IN FULL AT §12C OF THE CONTINUATION 0009 BLOCK AND THE FINDINGS ARE IN `reviews/volume-05/continuation-0009.md`. **FOUR OF THEM ARE FIGURES THAT WERE MEASURED AND WRONG, AND THE BIGGEST OF THOSE IS THE ONE A WRITER WOULD HAVE PAID FOR: A CLOSED FIGURE, *ELEVEN MONTHS*, HAD COME BACK INTO `state/character-state.md` AS A TENURE FOR JONAS MERCER, AND IT IS GONE WITH NOTHING PUT IN ITS PLACE, BECAUSE NO CHAPTER STATES HIS TENURE AND INVENTING ONE IS NOT A WRITER'S ACT. EVERY SPAN THAT SURVIVES IN EVERY WINDOW IS A DATELINE, A HOUSE FORM OR A FIGURE'S OWN BY-NAME TAG, AND NOT ONE IS A REUSE OF PROSE.****
